
===== FILE backend/tests/test_correction_view_batch.py =====
import json
from copy import deepcopy
from types import SimpleNamespace as NS

import pytest

from app import correction_view as views
from app.correction_contract import correction_contract_hash
from test_correction_view import _contract, _node


@pytest.mark.parametrize("category", ["knowledge_graph", "model_3d_su", "inspiration_image"])
def test_batch_table_equals_full_projection_without_sharing_item_values(category, monkeypatch):
    editable = _node("call_a.title", path="call_a.title")
    editable["metadata"] = {"editable": True, "archived_value": "冻结",
        "field_identity": {"field_path": "title"}, "steps": ["人工核对"],
        "paid_rerun_required": True, "evidence_blob": {"nested": ["证据"] * 100},
        "code": "never exposed"}
    hidden = _node("rule.hidden")
    hidden["metadata"] = {"editable": False, "frozen_value": {"rules": ["冻结规则"] * 1000}}
    contract = _contract(editable, hidden)
    contract["category_key"] = category
    contract["contract_hash"] = correction_contract_hash(contract)
    frozen = json.dumps(contract)
    run = NS(id=9, category_key=category, correction_contract_json=frozen,
             correction_contract_hash=contract["contract_hash"], execution_snapshot_json="{}")
    previous_run = NS(id=8, correction_contract_json=frozen, correction_contract_hash=contract["contract_hash"])
    items, previous = [], {}
    for index, value in enumerate([None, False, 0], 1):
        evaluation = NS(precheck_json=json.dumps({"production_fields": {"title": "模型"}}),
            aesthetic_json="{}", scoring_json="{}", review_revision=index, reviews=[],
            correction_history_json=json.dumps([{"node_key": "call_a.title", "new_value": value}]))
        item = NS(id=index, asset_id=index, run_id=9, evaluation_id=index, evaluation=evaluation,
                  result_snapshot_json="{}", baseline_set_item=None)
        items.append(item)
        previous[index] = NS(run=previous_run, run_id=8, evaluation=NS(
            correction_history_json=json.dumps([{"node_key": "call_a.title", "new_value": "上一轮"}]),
            reviews=[]))
    expected = {item.id: views.table_correction_view(views.build_correction_view(
        None, run=run, item=item, previous_item=previous[item.asset_id])) for item in items}
    calls = []
    original = views._prepare_view_contract
    def prepare(value):
        calls.append(value.id)
        return original(value)
    monkeypatch.setattr(views, "_prepare_view_contract", prepare)
    actual = {key: views.table_correction_view(value) for key, value in views.build_correction_views(
        None, run=run, items=items, previous_by_asset=previous, table=True).items()}
    assert actual == expected
    assert calls == [9], "full frozen validation must occur once per request, before table filtering"
    assert [actual[i]["nodes"][0]["human_value"] for i in (1, 2, 3)] == [None, False, 0]
    actual[1]["nodes"][0]["metadata"]["field_identity"]["field_path"] = "mutated"
    assert actual[2]["nodes"][0]["metadata"]["field_identity"]["field_path"] == "title"
    assert run.correction_contract_json == frozen


def test_table_does_not_hide_invalid_frozen_nodes_or_reuse_old_request_validation():
    contract = _contract(_node("call_a.title"), _node("rule.hidden"))
    contract["nodes"][1]["metadata"] = {"editable": False}
    contract["contract_hash"] = correction_contract_hash(contract)
    run = NS(id=9, category_key="inspiration_image", correction_contract_json=json.dumps(contract),
             correction_contract_hash=contract["contract_hash"], execution_snapshot_json="{}")
    item = NS(id=1, asset_id=1, evaluation_id=1, evaluation=NS(reviews=[]), result_snapshot_json="{}")
    valid = views.build_correction_views(None, run=run, items=[item], previous_by_asset={}, table=True)[1]
    assert not valid["read_only"]
    # A hidden node is still validated. Keeping the stored hash unchanged must
    # also invalidate the next request, even on the same ORM object.
    contract["nodes"][1]["type"] = "unsupported-node-type"
    run.correction_contract_json = json.dumps(contract)
    invalid = views.build_correction_views(None, run=run, items=[item], previous_by_asset={}, table=True)[1]
    assert invalid["read_only"] and invalid["snapshot_status"] == "legacy_read_only"
    assert views.table_correction_view(invalid)["nodes"] == []
    assert any("哈希" in reason for reason in invalid["unavailable_nodes"])
    assert any("不受支持" in reason for reason in invalid["unavailable_nodes"])

===== FILE backend/tests/test_cover_attempts.py =====
import io
import json

import httpx
from PIL import Image
import pytest
from sqlalchemy import create_engine
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from app.cover_attempts import (CoverGenerationAttempt, advance, create_attempt,
                               latest_success, recover_local_stage, request_recheck, request_retry)
from app.cover_gateway import CoverGateway, CoverParameters


def png():
    b = io.BytesIO()
    Image.new("RGB", (8, 8), "white").save(b, "PNG")
    return b.getvalue()


@pytest.fixture
def db(tmp_path):
    engine = create_engine("sqlite:///" + str(tmp_path / "test.sqlite"))
    CoverGenerationAttempt.__table__.create(engine)
    with Session(engine) as session:
        yield session
    engine.dispose()


def make(db, tmp_path, sequence=1):
    row = create_attempt(db, sample_key="round-1:sample-1", sequence_no=sequence,
        image=png(), prompt="test", parameters=CoverParameters(), input_directory=tmp_path / "inputs")
    db.commit()
    return row.id


def test_restart_queries_same_attempt_and_pause_keeps_inflight_results(db, tmp_path):
    attempt = make(db, tmp_path)
    posts = []
    def handler(request):
        if request.url.path == "/v1/images":
            return httpx.Response(200, json={"image": "lan-" + "a" * 32 + ".png"})
        if request.method == "POST":
            posts.append(request)
            # Intent is durable before dispatch; emulate response loss after acceptance.
            with Session(db.bind) as observer:
                assert observer.get(CoverGenerationAttempt, attempt).state == "submitting"
            raise httpx.ReadTimeout("response lost", request=request)
        if request.url.path.endswith("/image"):
            return httpx.Response(200, content=png())
        return httpx.Response(200, json={"request_id": attempt, "prompt_id": "p1", "state": "succeeded"})
    with CoverGateway("http://gateway.test", transport=httpx.MockTransport(handler)) as gateway:
        def step(paused=False):
            return advance(db, attempt, gateway=gateway, input_directory=tmp_path / "inputs",
                           output_directory=tmp_path / "outputs", allow_submit=not paused)
        assert step(paused=True) == "prepared"
        assert step() == "ready"
        assert step(paused=True) == "ready"
        assert step() == "submitting"
        db.expire_all()  # recover from committed state; no in-memory response required
        assert step(paused=True) == "succeeded"
        assert step() == "succeeded"
    assert len(posts) == 1
    row = latest_success(db, "round-1:sample-1")
    assert row.id == attempt
    assert len(list((tmp_path / "outputs").iterdir())) == 1
    assert json.loads(row.result_json)["width"] == 8


def test_late_old_success_cannot_replace_new_success_and_failed_regeneration_keeps_previous(db, tmp_path):
    old = make(db, tmp_path)
    new = make(db, tmp_path, 2)
    failed = make(db, tmp_path, 3)
    db.get(CoverGenerationAttempt, new).state = "succeeded"
    db.get(CoverGenerationAttempt, old).state = "succeeded"
    db.get(CoverGenerationAttempt, failed).state = "failed"
    db.commit()
    assert latest_success(db, "round-1:sample-1").id == new


def test_missing_gateway_history_blocks_without_resubmit(db, tmp_path):
    attempt = make(db, tmp_path)
    db.get(CoverGenerationAttempt, attempt).state = "submitting"
    db.commit()
    calls = []
    def handler(request):
        calls.append(request)
        assert request.method == "GET"
        return httpx.Response(404)
    with CoverGateway("http://gateway.test", transport=httpx.MockTransport(handler)) as gateway:
        for _ in range(2):
            assert advance(db, attempt, gateway=gateway, input_directory=tmp_path / "inputs",
                           output_directory=tmp_path / "outputs") == "needs_review"
    assert len(calls) == 1
    assert not recover_local_stage(db, attempt)


def test_corrupted_input_stops_before_upload(db, tmp_path):
    attempt = make(db, tmp_path)
    next((tmp_path / "inputs").iterdir()).write_bytes(b"changed")
    with CoverGateway("http://gateway.test", transport=httpx.MockTransport(lambda r:
            pytest.fail("must not upload changed input"))) as gateway:
        assert advance(db, attempt, gateway=gateway, input_directory=tmp_path / "inputs",
                       output_directory=tmp_path / "outputs") == "needs_review"
    assert db.get(CoverGenerationAttempt, attempt).error_code == "input_snapshot_corrupted"


def test_confirmed_worker_exit_recovery_never_resets_submission(db, tmp_path):
    attempt = make(db, tmp_path)
    row = db.get(CoverGenerationAttempt, attempt)
    for source, expected in (("uploading", "prepared"), ("downloading", "download_pending")):
        row.state = source
        db.commit()
        assert recover_local_stage(db, attempt)
        assert row.state == expected
    row.state = "submitting"
    db.commit()
    assert not recover_local_stage(db, attempt)
    assert row.state == "submitting"


def test_queue_full_has_finite_backoff_and_manual_check_never_resubmits(db, tmp_path):
    attempt = make(db, tmp_path)
    posts = []
    def handler(request):
        if request.url.path == '/v1/images':
            return httpx.Response(200, json={'image': 'lan-' + 'a' * 32 + '.png'})
        if request.method == 'POST':
            posts.append(request)
            return httpx.Response(429)
        raise AssertionError('no query for a rejected submission')
    with CoverGateway('http://gateway.test', transport=httpx.MockTransport(handler)) as gateway:
        def step():
            return advance(db, attempt, gateway=gateway, input_directory=tmp_path/'inputs', output_directory=tmp_path/'outputs')
        assert step() == 'ready'
        assert step() == 'ready'
        assert step() == 'ready'  # retry time has not arrived
        assert len(posts) == 1
        for _ in range(3):
            db.get(CoverGenerationAttempt, attempt).retry_after = None
            db.commit()
            step()
        row = db.get(CoverGenerationAttempt, attempt)
        assert row.state == 'needs_review' and row.retry_count == 4
        assert not row.gateway_unsettled
    from app.cover_gateway import CoverGatewayError
    with pytest.raises(CoverGatewayError, match='attempt_not_recheckable'):
        request_recheck(db, attempt)
    request_retry(db, attempt); db.commit()
    assert db.get(CoverGenerationAttempt, attempt).state == 'ready'


def test_manual_recheck_queries_original_unknown_job_and_downloads(db, tmp_path):
    attempt = make(db, tmp_path)
    row = db.get(CoverGenerationAttempt, attempt)
    row.state = 'needs_review'; row.error_code = 'submission_unknown'; row.gateway_unsettled = True
    row.uploaded_image = 'lan-' + 'a' * 32 + '.png'
    db.commit()
    from app.cover_gateway import CoverGatewayError
    with pytest.raises(CoverGatewayError, match='original_attempt_not_reconciled'):
        request_retry(db, attempt)
    request_recheck(db, attempt); db.commit()
    calls = []
    def handler(request):
        calls.append(request)
        assert request.method == 'GET'
        if request.url.path.endswith('/image'):
            return httpx.Response(200, content=png())
        return httpx.Response(200, json={'request_id':attempt,'prompt_id':'original-prompt','state':'succeeded'})
    with CoverGateway('http://gateway.test', transport=httpx.MockTransport(handler)) as gateway:
        assert advance(db, attempt, gateway=gateway, input_directory=tmp_path/'inputs', output_directory=tmp_path/'outputs', allow_submit=False) == 'succeeded'
    assert len(calls) == 2
    assert not db.get(CoverGenerationAttempt, attempt).gateway_unsettled


def test_failed_download_retains_completed_job_and_retries_file_without_requery(db, tmp_path):
    attempt=make(db,tmp_path)
    row=db.get(CoverGenerationAttempt,attempt)
    row.state='submitted';row.gateway_unsettled=True;db.commit()
    calls=[]
    def handler(request):
        calls.append(request.url.path)
        if request.url.path.endswith('/image'):
            return httpx.Response(503) if calls.count(request.url.path)==1 else httpx.Response(200,content=png())
        assert calls.count(request.url.path)==1,'completed job must not be re-queried'
        return httpx.Response(200,json={'request_id':attempt,'prompt_id':'done','state':'succeeded'})
    with CoverGateway('http://gateway.test',transport=httpx.MockTransport(handler)) as gateway:
        args=dict(gateway=gateway,input_directory=tmp_path/'inputs',output_directory=tmp_path/'outputs')
        assert advance(db,attempt,**args)=='download_pending'
        row.retry_after=None;db.commit()
        assert advance(db,attempt,allow_submit=False,**args)=='succeeded'
    assert calls.count('/v1/jobs/'+attempt)==1


def test_query_errors_never_clear_unknown_submission_or_allow_post_retry(db, tmp_path):
    from app.cover_attempts import can_retry
    attempt=make(db,tmp_path)
    row=db.get(CoverGenerationAttempt,attempt)
    row.state='submitting';row.gateway_unsettled=True;row.uploaded_image='lan-'+'a'*32+'.png';db.commit()
    calls=[]
    def handler(request):
        calls.append(request.method)
        assert request.method=='GET'
        raise httpx.DecodingError('bad encoding',request=request)
    with CoverGateway('http://gateway.test',transport=httpx.MockTransport(handler)) as gateway:
        for _ in range(4):
            row.retry_after=None;db.commit()
            advance(db,attempt,gateway=gateway,input_directory=tmp_path/'inputs',output_directory=tmp_path/'outputs')
    assert row.state=='needs_review' and row.gateway_unsettled and not can_retry(row)
    assert calls==['GET']*4
    request_recheck(db,attempt);db.commit()
    assert row.state=='submitting'


def test_atomic_snapshot_repairs_truncated_file(db, tmp_path):
    first=make(db,tmp_path)
    frozen=json.loads(db.get(CoverGenerationAttempt,first).input_json)
    source=tmp_path/'inputs'/frozen['image_sha256']
    source.write_bytes(b'truncated')
    make(db,tmp_path,sequence=2)
    assert source.read_bytes()==png()


def test_operator_resolution_requires_investigation_and_keeps_audit(db, tmp_path):
    from app.models import AuditEvent
    from app.cover_attempts import resolve_unknown
    from app.cover_gateway import CoverGatewayError
    AuditEvent.__table__.create(db.bind)
    attempt=make(db,tmp_path)
    row=db.get(CoverGenerationAttempt,attempt)
    row.state='needs_review';row.error_code='history_unavailable';row.gateway_unsettled=True;db.commit()
    stamp=row.updated_at
    with pytest.raises(CoverGatewayError,match='investigation_evidence_required'):
        resolve_unknown(db,attempt,actor='operator',evidence='',expected_updated_at=stamp)
    resolve_unknown(db,attempt,actor='operator',evidence='核对原prompt对应执行已终止，队列无此任务',expected_updated_at=stamp)
    db.commit()
    assert row.state=='failed' and not row.gateway_unsettled
    assert json.loads(row.resolution_json)['previous_error']=='history_unavailable'
    assert db.query(AuditEvent).count()==1

===== FILE backend/tests/test_cover_gateway.py =====
import io
import json

import httpx
from PIL import Image
import pytest

from app.cover_gateway import CoverGateway, CoverGatewayError, CoverParameters


IMAGE = "lan-" + "a" * 32 + ".png"


def png():
    stream = io.BytesIO()
    Image.new("RGB", (8, 8), "white").save(stream, "PNG")
    return stream.getvalue()


def test_full_gateway_flow_stores_own_image_and_reuses_request_identity(tmp_path):
    requests = []
    def handler(request):
        requests.append(request)
        assert "origin" not in request.headers
        assert "authorization" not in request.headers
        if request.url.path == "/v1/images":
            assert b'name="image"' in request.content
            return httpx.Response(200, json={"image": IMAGE})
        if request.url.path.endswith("/image"):
            return httpx.Response(200, content=png())
        if request.method == "POST":
            assert json.loads(request.content)["image"] == IMAGE
        return httpx.Response(200, json={"request_id": "attempt-1", "prompt_id": "pid",
                                        "state": "submitted" if request.method == "POST" else "succeeded"})
    with CoverGateway("http://gateway.test", transport=httpx.MockTransport(handler)) as gateway:
        uploaded = gateway.upload(png())
        for _ in range(2):
            job = gateway.submit(request_id="attempt-1", image=uploaded, prompt="白底商品图",
                                 parameters=CoverParameters())
            assert job.request_id == "attempt-1"
        assert gateway.query("attempt-1").state == "succeeded"
        saved = gateway.download("attempt-1", directory=tmp_path)
        assert saved.path.read_bytes() == png()
        assert gateway.download("attempt-1", directory=tmp_path) == saved
    assert len(list(tmp_path.iterdir())) == 1
    assert len([r for r in requests if r.url.path == "/v1/images"]) == 1


@pytest.mark.parametrize("failure", ["timeout", "502", "409", "bad_json"])
def test_ambiguous_submission_never_retries(failure):
    calls = []
    def handler(request):
        calls.append(request)
        if failure == "timeout":
            raise httpx.ReadTimeout("sensitive detail", request=request)
        if failure == "bad_json":
            return httpx.Response(200, text="not json")
        return httpx.Response(int(failure), text="sensitive detail")
    with CoverGateway("http://gateway.test", transport=httpx.MockTransport(handler)) as gateway:
        with pytest.raises(CoverGatewayError) as caught:
            gateway.submit(request_id="attempt-1", image=IMAGE, prompt="test", parameters=CoverParameters())
        assert caught.value.uncertain
        assert not caught.value.retryable
        assert "sensitive" not in str(caught.value)
    assert len(calls) == 1


def test_full_queue_is_safe_retry_but_missing_record_is_not_new_submission():
    def handler(request):
        return httpx.Response(429 if request.method == "POST" else 404)
    with CoverGateway("http://gateway.test", transport=httpx.MockTransport(handler)) as gateway:
        with pytest.raises(CoverGatewayError) as caught:
            gateway.submit(request_id="attempt-1", image=IMAGE, prompt="test", parameters=CoverParameters())
        assert caught.value.retryable and not caught.value.uncertain
        with pytest.raises(CoverGatewayError) as caught:
            gateway.query("attempt-1")
        assert caught.value.uncertain and not caught.value.retryable


@pytest.mark.parametrize("state", ["submission_unknown", "history_unavailable"])
def test_unknown_gateway_state_is_preserved(state):
    with CoverGateway("http://gateway.test", transport=httpx.MockTransport(lambda r:
            httpx.Response(200, json={"request_id": "attempt-1", "state": state, "prompt_id": None}))) as gateway:
        assert gateway.query("attempt-1").state == state


def test_no_redirect_follow_and_invalid_result_not_saved(tmp_path):
    calls = []
    def handler(request):
        calls.append(request)
        return httpx.Response(302, headers={"location": "http://other.test/secret"})
    with CoverGateway("http://gateway.test", transport=httpx.MockTransport(handler)) as gateway:
        with pytest.raises(CoverGatewayError):
            gateway.download("attempt-1", directory=tmp_path)
    assert len(calls) == 1
    assert not list(tmp_path.iterdir())
    with CoverGateway("http://gateway.test", transport=httpx.MockTransport(lambda r:
            httpx.Response(200, content=b"not an image"))) as gateway:
        with pytest.raises(CoverGatewayError):
            gateway.download("attempt-1", directory=tmp_path)
    assert not list(tmp_path.iterdir())


@pytest.mark.parametrize("parameters", [CoverParameters(width=True), CoverParameters(width=300),
    CoverParameters(cfg=float("nan")), CoverParameters(seed=-1), CoverParameters(steps=41)])
def test_invalid_parameters_never_submit(parameters):
    with CoverGateway("http://gateway.test", transport=httpx.MockTransport(lambda r:
            pytest.fail("must not call gateway"))) as gateway:
        with pytest.raises(CoverGatewayError):
            gateway.submit(request_id="a", image=IMAGE, prompt="test", parameters=parameters)


def test_text_only_not_misrepresented_as_supported():
    with CoverGateway("http://gateway.test", transport=httpx.MockTransport(lambda r:
            pytest.fail("text generation not yet supported by gateway"))) as gateway:
        with pytest.raises(CoverGatewayError, match="text_to_image_not_supported"):
            gateway.submit(request_id="a", image=None, prompt="test", parameters=CoverParameters())


def test_wrong_job_identity_not_accepted():
    with CoverGateway("http://gateway.test", transport=httpx.MockTransport(lambda r:
            httpx.Response(200, json={"request_id": "other", "state": "succeeded", "prompt_id": "pid"}))) as gateway:
        with pytest.raises(CoverGatewayError, match="gateway_invalid_job"):
            gateway.query("attempt-1")

===== FILE backend/tests/test_cover_regression.py =====
import io
import json
from types import SimpleNamespace

import httpx
import pytest
from PIL import Image
from sqlalchemy import create_engine, select
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from app.database import Base
from app.models import Asset, BaselineSet, BaselineSetItem
from app.cover_attempts import CoverGenerationAttempt
from app.cover_gateway import CoverGateway, CoverGatewayError, CoverParameters
from app.cover_regression import (CoverRegressionItem, create_round, review_item, item_payload,
                                  regenerate_item, change_status, round_statistics, settle_rounds,
                                  prepare_input, retry_input)
from app.cover_worker import tick, scheduler_lock


@pytest.fixture
def setup(tmp_path):
    engine = create_engine("sqlite:///" + str(tmp_path / "test.db"))
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    settings = SimpleNamespace(data_dir=tmp_path, upload_dir=tmp_path / "uploads")
    settings.upload_dir.mkdir()
    image = io.BytesIO(); Image.new("RGB", (8, 8), "white").save(image, "PNG")
    (settings.upload_dir / "test.png").write_bytes(image.getvalue())
    with Session(engine) as db:
        import hashlib
        asset = Asset(original_name="test.png", stored_name="test.png", mime_type="image/png",
                      size_bytes=len(image.getvalue()), sha256=hashlib.sha256(image.getvalue()).hexdigest(), category_key="space_image")
        db.add(asset); db.flush()
        baseline = BaselineSet(name="images", category_key="space_image", default_expected_level="L1", fingerprint="b" * 64)
        db.add(baseline); db.flush()
        db.add(BaselineSetItem(baseline_set_id=baseline.id, asset_id=asset.id, asset_snapshot_json="{}"))
        db.commit()
        yield db, settings, baseline.id
    engine.dispose()


def make(db, settings, set_id, request="round-a"):
    row = create_round(db, baseline_set_id=set_id, prompt="白底", parameters=CoverParameters(),
                       actor="test", settings=settings, request_id=request)
    db.commit()
    for item in db.scalars(select(CoverRegressionItem).where(CoverRegressionItem.run_id==row.id)).all():
        prepare_input(db,item.id,settings=settings);db.commit()
    return row


def test_round_freezes_source_and_manual_review_is_optional_and_authoritative(setup):
    db, settings, set_id = setup
    run = make(db, settings, set_id)
    assert make(db, settings, set_id).id == run.id
    item = db.scalar(select(CoverRegressionItem))
    old = db.scalar(select(CoverGenerationAttempt))
    old.state = "succeeded"; old.result_json = '{"sha256":"old"}'
    db.commit()
    payload = item_payload(db, item)
    assert payload["review"] is None and payload["effective_attempt_id"] == old.id
    review_item(db, item_id=item.id, attempt_id=old.id, expected_revision=0,
                verdict="adopt", reason="保留此图", actor="test")
    db.commit()
    (settings.upload_dir / "test.png").write_bytes(b"source changed after launch")
    newer = regenerate_item(db, item_id=item.id, input_directory=settings.data_dir / "stock-images" / "inputs")
    newer.state = "succeeded"; newer.result_json = '{"sha256":"new"}'
    db.commit()
    payload = item_payload(db, item)
    assert payload["effective_attempt_id"] == old.id
    assert payload["attempts"][0]["id"] == newer.id
    assert newer.input_json == old.input_json
    with pytest.raises(CoverGatewayError, match="review_revision_conflict"):
        review_item(db, item_id=item.id, attempt_id=newer.id, expected_revision=0,
                    verdict="adopt", reason="stale", actor="test")


def test_round_robin_queue_and_pause(setup):
    db, settings, set_id = setup
    make(db, settings, set_id, "round-a"); make(db, settings, set_id, "round-b")
    posts = []
    def handler(request):
        if request.url.path == "/v1/images":
            return httpx.Response(200, json={"image": "lan-" + "a" * 32 + ".png"})
        if request.method == "POST":
            body = json.loads(request.content); posts.append(body["request_id"])
            return httpx.Response(202, json={"request_id": body["request_id"], "state": "submitted", "prompt_id": "pid"})
        raise AssertionError("unexpected request")
    with CoverGateway("http://gateway.test", transport=httpx.MockTransport(handler)) as gateway:
        assert tick(db.bind, directory=settings.data_dir / "stock-images", gateway=gateway)
        assert tick(db.bind, directory=settings.data_dir / "stock-images", gateway=gateway)
        db.expire_all()
        assert {r.state for r in db.scalars(select(CoverGenerationAttempt))} == {"ready"}
        change_status(db, "round-a", "pause"); db.commit()
        assert tick(db.bind, directory=settings.data_dir / "stock-images", gateway=gateway)
        db.expire_all()
        dispatched = db.get(CoverGenerationAttempt, posts[0])
        assert db.get(CoverRegressionItem, dispatched.sample_key).run_id == "round-b"
        assert len(posts) == 1


def test_sqlite_global_worker_lock_excludes_another_worker(setup):
    db, settings, _ = setup
    with scheduler_lock(db.bind, settings.data_dir) as first:
        assert first
        with scheduler_lock(db.bind, settings.data_dir) as second:
            assert not second


def test_ambiguous_attempt_cannot_be_regenerated(setup):
    db, settings, set_id = setup
    make(db, settings, set_id)
    item = db.scalar(select(CoverRegressionItem))
    attempt = db.scalar(select(CoverGenerationAttempt))
    attempt.state = "needs_review"; attempt.error_code = "submission_unknown"; attempt.gateway_unsettled=True; db.commit()
    assert not round_statistics(db, item.run_id)['execution_finished']
    with pytest.raises(CoverGatewayError, match="original_attempt_not_reconciled"):
        regenerate_item(db, item_id=item.id, input_directory=settings.data_dir / "stock-images" / "inputs")


def test_execution_completes_without_human_review_and_regeneration_reopens(setup):
    db, settings, set_id = setup
    run = make(db, settings, set_id)
    item = db.scalar(select(CoverRegressionItem))
    old = db.scalar(select(CoverGenerationAttempt))
    old.state = 'succeeded'; old.result_json = '{"sha256":"old"}'; db.commit()
    settle_rounds(db); db.commit()
    stats = round_statistics(db, run.id)
    assert run.status == 'completed'
    assert stats['succeeded'] == 1 and stats['reviewed'] == 0 and stats['execution_finished']
    regenerate_item(db, item_id=item.id, input_directory=settings.data_dir/'stock-images'/'inputs')
    db.commit()
    assert run.status == 'running'
    assert round_statistics(db, run.id)['pending'] == 1
    assert not round_statistics(db, run.id)['execution_finished']


def test_polling_inflight_does_not_skip_dispatch_to_second_slot(setup):
    db,settings,set_id=setup
    make(db,settings,set_id,'run-one');make(db,settings,set_id,'run-two')
    a=db.scalar(select(CoverGenerationAttempt).join(CoverRegressionItem,CoverRegressionItem.id==CoverGenerationAttempt.sample_key).where(CoverRegressionItem.run_id=='run-one'))
    a.state='running';a.prompt_id='first';a.updated_at='2020-01-01T00:00:00+00:00';a.gateway_unsettled=True;db.commit()
    calls=[]
    def handler(request):
        calls.append(request.url.path)
        if request.method=='GET':return httpx.Response(200,json={'request_id':a.id,'prompt_id':'first','state':'running'})
        assert request.url.path=='/v1/images'
        return httpx.Response(200,json={'image':'lan-'+'d'*32+'.png'})
    with CoverGateway('http://gateway.test',transport=httpx.MockTransport(handler)) as gateway:
        assert tick(db.bind,directory=settings.data_dir/'stock-images',gateway=gateway)
    assert '/v1/images' in calls


def test_concurrent_creation_replay_returns_one_frozen_round(setup):
    from concurrent.futures import ThreadPoolExecutor
    from threading import Barrier
    db,settings,set_id=setup
    engine=db.bind;barrier=Barrier(2)
    db.rollback()
    def create():
        with Session(engine) as s:
            barrier.wait(timeout=10)
            run=create_round(s,baseline_set_id=set_id,prompt='same',parameters=CoverParameters(),actor='parallel',settings=settings,request_id='parallel-request')
            s.commit()
            return run.id
    with ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=2) as executor:
        result=list(executor.map(lambda _:create(),range(2)))
    assert result==['parallel-request']*2


def test_launch_400_freezes_metadata_without_file_reads_and_worker_copies_one(setup,monkeypatch):
    import hashlib
    db,settings,set_id=setup
    source=db.scalar(select(Asset))
    for i in range(399):
        asset=Asset(original_name=f'sample-{i}.png',stored_name=f'missing-{i}.png',mime_type=source.mime_type,
            size_bytes=source.size_bytes,sha256=source.sha256,category_key=source.category_key)
        db.add(asset);db.flush();db.add(BaselineSetItem(baseline_set_id=set_id,asset_id=asset.id,asset_snapshot_json='{}'))
    db.commit()
    with monkeypatch.context() as m:
        m.setattr('app.nas_storage.resolve_asset_path',lambda *a,**kw:pytest.fail('HTTP launch must not read files'))
        run=create_round(db,baseline_set_id=set_id,prompt='fixed',parameters=CoverParameters(),actor='test',settings=settings,request_id='async-400')
        db.commit()
    assert db.query(CoverGenerationAttempt).count()==0
    assert round_statistics(db,run.id)['pending']==400
    first=db.scalar(select(CoverRegressionItem).where(CoverRegressionItem.run_id==run.id).order_by(CoverRegressionItem.baseline_item_id))
    attempt=prepare_input(db,first.id,settings=settings);db.commit()
    assert attempt is not None and db.query(CoverGenerationAttempt).count()==1
    assert json.loads(attempt.input_json)['image_sha256']==source.sha256


def test_changed_input_is_not_silently_followed_and_can_retry_original_version(setup):
    db,settings,set_id=setup
    original=(settings.upload_dir/'test.png').read_bytes()
    run=create_round(db,baseline_set_id=set_id,prompt='fixed',parameters=CoverParameters(),actor='test',settings=settings,request_id='async-changed')
    db.commit();item=db.scalar(select(CoverRegressionItem))
    (settings.upload_dir/'test.png').write_bytes(b'changed after launch')
    assert prepare_input(db,item.id,settings=settings) is None
    db.commit();assert item.input_error=='input_snapshot_changed'
    assert db.query(CoverGenerationAttempt).count()==0
    (settings.upload_dir/'test.png').write_bytes(original)
    retry_input(db,item.id);db.commit()
    assert prepare_input(db,item.id,settings=settings) is not None
    db.commit();assert item.input_error is None

def test_http_read_only_and_cross_category_guards(setup, monkeypatch):
    from fastapi import FastAPI
    from fastapi.testclient import TestClient
    from app.cover_regression_api import build_cover_regression_router
    from app.database import get_db
    from app.models import User
    db, settings, set_id = setup
    monkeypatch.setenv("COVER_GATEWAY_URL", "http://gateway.test")
    monkeypatch.setattr("app.cover_regression_api.get_settings", lambda: settings)
    user = User(username="reader", is_admin=False, role="viewer", category_keys_json="[]")
    app = FastAPI()
    app.include_router(build_cover_regression_router(lambda: user))
    app.dependency_overrides[get_db] = lambda: db
    client = TestClient(app)
    body = {"request_id": "http-test", "prompt": "白底"}
    assert client.post(f"/api/baseline-sets/{set_id}/cover-regressions", json=body).status_code == 403
    user.role = "category_manager"; user.category_keys_json = '["model_3d_su"]'
    assert client.post(f"/api/baseline-sets/{set_id}/cover-regressions", json=body).status_code == 403
    user.role = "product_rd"
    response = client.post(f"/api/baseline-sets/{set_id}/cover-regressions", json=body)
    assert response.status_code == 200, response.text
    assert client.get("/api/cover-regressions/http-test").json()["total"] == 1
    user.role = "viewer"
    assert client.post("/api/cover-regressions/http-test/control/pause").status_code == 403
    assert client.get("/api/cover-regressions/http-test").status_code == 200

===== FILE backend/tests/test_custom_scoring_primitives.py =====
from __future__ import annotations

from app.category_evaluation_aggregator import aggregate_category_evaluation
from copy import deepcopy

import pytest

from app.category_evaluation_contract import (
    CATEGORY_EVALUATION_CONTRACT_VERSION,
    CategoryEvaluationContractError,
    validate_category_evaluation_contract,
)
from app.redline_policy import REDLINE_POLICY_FORMAT_VERSION


def _contract() -> dict:
    return {
        "schema_version": CATEGORY_EVALUATION_CONTRACT_VERSION,
        "category_key": "operator_custom",
        "level_scale": {
            "version": "category-level-scale-v1",
            "levels": [
                {"level": "L1", "enabled": True, "min_score": 80},
                {"level": "L2", "enabled": True, "min_score": 60},
                {"level": "L3", "enabled": True, "min_score": 40},
                {"level": "L4", "enabled": True, "min_score": 0},
                {"level": "L5", "enabled": False},
            ],
        },
        "redline_policy": {
            "format_version": REDLINE_POLICY_FORMAT_VERSION,
            "enabled": False,
            "hit_level": "L4",
            "hit_score_cap": 40,
            "rules": [],
        },
        "track_classification": {
            "format_version": "track-classification-v1",
            "default_track": "class_one",
            "tracks": [
                {
                    "key": "class_one", "label": "一类", "base_score": 40,
                    "dimension_max": 60, "track_cap": 100,
                    "dimension_schema_ref": {"schema_key": "custom", "version": "1"},
                },
                {
                    "key": "class_two", "label": "二类", "base_score": 20,
                    "dimension_max": 60, "track_cap": 80,
                    "dimension_schema_ref": {"schema_key": "custom", "version": "1"},
                },
                {
                    "key": "class_three", "label": "三类", "base_score": 40,
                    "dimension_max": 30, "track_cap": 100,
                    "dimension_schema_ref": {"schema_key": "custom", "version": "1"},
                },
            ],
        },
        "common_modifiers": {
            "format_version": "common-modifiers-v1",
            "media_type_penalty": {
                "enabled": True, "baseline": "real_photo",
                "penalties": {"real_photo": 0, "render_3d": -5, "ai_image": -10, "other": -10},
            },
            "high_score_veto": {"enabled": False, "threshold": 80, "cap_to": 79},
            "hard_defect_penalty": {"enabled": True, "per_hit": 10, "source": "hard_defects"},
        },
        "track_adjustments": {
            "class_two": {"deduction": 20},
            "class_three": {"bonus": 30},
        },
    }


def _precheck(trait: str, hard_defects: list[str] | None = None) -> dict:
    payload = {"production_fields": {"trait": trait, "reason": []}}
    if hard_defects is not None:
        payload["hard_defects"] = hard_defects
    return payload


def test_custom_track_adjustments_and_hard_defect_penalty_are_applied() -> None:
    result = aggregate_category_evaluation(
        _contract(), _precheck("实景照片", ["a", "b"]), {"deductions": {}}, track_key="class_two"
    )

    assert result["score"] == 40
    assert result["track_adjustment"] == {"deduction": 20.0, "bonus": 0.0}
    assert result["hard_defect_penalty"] == 20.0
    assert {item["primitive"] for item in result["common_modifier_evidence"]} == {
        "track_adjustment", "media_penalty", "hard_defect_penalty",
    }


def test_custom_media_aliases_execute_render_ai_and_other_penalties() -> None:
    contract = _contract()
    render = aggregate_category_evaluation(contract, _precheck("3d_render"), {"deductions": {}}, track_key="class_one")
    ai = aggregate_category_evaluation(contract, _precheck("ai_generated"), {"deductions": {}}, track_key="class_one")
    other = aggregate_category_evaluation(contract, _precheck("other"), {"deductions": {}}, track_key="class_one")

    assert render["score"] == 95
    assert ai["score"] == 90
    assert other["score"] == 90


def _contract_owned_modifier_names() -> dict:
    contract = deepcopy(_contract())
    contract["common_modifiers"] = {
        "format_version": "common-modifiers-v2",
        "media_type_penalty": {
            "enabled": True,
            "baseline": "摄影成片",
            "fallback": "其它媒介",
            "aliases": {
                "实景照片": "摄影成片",
                "3D数字效果图": "模型渲染",
            },
            "penalties": {
                "摄影成片": 0,
                "模型渲染": -6,
                "其它媒介": -2,
            },
        },
        "high_score_veto": {"enabled": False},
        "hard_defect_penalty": {
            "enabled": True,
            "per_hit": 10,
            "source": "hard_defects",
        },
    }
    return contract


def test_contract_owned_modifier_names_validate_without_legacy_veto_tiers() -> None:
    validate_category_evaluation_contract(_contract_owned_modifier_names())


def test_contract_owned_media_alias_and_uncapped_hard_defects_execute() -> None:
    result = aggregate_category_evaluation(
        _contract_owned_modifier_names(),
        _precheck("3D数字效果图", ["透明棋盘格", "未完成手绘草稿"]),
        {"deductions": {}},
        track_key="class_one",
    )

    assert result["score"] == 74
    assert result["media_key"] == "模型渲染"
    assert result["media_penalty"] == -6
    assert result["hard_defect_penalty"] == 20
    assert result["hard_defect_action"] is None


def _step_note(result: dict, primitive: str) -> str:
    for step in result.get("steps") or []:
        if step.get("step") == primitive:
            return str(step.get("note") or "")
    return ""


def test_track_adjustment_note_states_only_the_side_that_applies() -> None:
    """证据句只写生效的一侧，避免运营认不出扣分已生效。

    原措辞固定输出「加 0.0，扣 20.0」，读的人要先分辨哪个是 0——运营在群里
    据此判断「没看到哪里有体现赛道扣分」，而当时扣分其实一直在生效。
    """

    deducted = aggregate_category_evaluation(
        _contract(), _precheck("实景照片"), {"deductions": {}}, track_key="class_two"
    )
    note = _step_note(deducted, "track_adjustment")
    assert "扣 20 分" in note
    assert "加" not in note
    assert "class_two" in note

    bonused = aggregate_category_evaluation(
        _contract(), _precheck("实景照片"), {"deductions": {}}, track_key="class_three"
    )
    bonus_note = _step_note(bonused, "track_adjustment")
    assert "加 30 分" in bonus_note
    assert "扣" not in bonus_note


def test_track_without_adjustment_reads_as_configuration_not_failure() -> None:
    """未配置加减分的赛道要说清是「没配」，不要读起来像故障。"""

    result = aggregate_category_evaluation(
        _contract(), _precheck("实景照片"), {"deductions": {}}, track_key="class_one"
    )
    note = _step_note(result, "track_adjustment_skipped")
    assert "不做固定调整" in note
    assert "未配置" in note
    assert "class_one" in note


def test_hard_defect_penalty_both_sources_counts_duplicates_only_without_switch() -> None:
    """冻结配置（无 count_distinct）保持逐条计数；显式开关后同一硬伤只扣一次。"""
    contract = _contract()
    contract["common_modifiers"]["hard_defect_penalty"] = {
        "enabled": True, "per_hit": 10, "source": "both",
    }
    precheck = _precheck("实景照片", ["watermark", "blur"])
    precheck["image_defects"] = ["watermark"]

    legacy = aggregate_category_evaluation(
        contract, precheck, {"deductions": {}}, track_key="class_one"
    )
    assert legacy["hard_defect_penalty"] == 30.0

    contract["common_modifiers"]["hard_defect_penalty"]["count_distinct"] = True
    distinct = aggregate_category_evaluation(
        contract, precheck, {"deductions": {}}, track_key="class_one"
    )
    assert distinct["hard_defect_penalty"] == 20.0
    evidence = next(
        item for item in distinct["common_modifier_evidence"]
        if item["primitive"] == "hard_defect_penalty"
    )
    assert evidence["hits"] == ["watermark", "blur"]
    assert evidence["count_distinct"] is True


def test_hard_defect_penalty_count_distinct_must_be_boolean() -> None:
    contract = _contract()
    contract["common_modifiers"]["hard_defect_penalty"]["count_distinct"] = "yes"
    with pytest.raises(CategoryEvaluationContractError):
        validate_category_evaluation_contract(contract)

===== FILE backend/tests/test_daily_ledger_lock_free.py =====
"""日预算账本：门槛关着时只记账不锁行；门槛开着时才锁行比对。

2026-09-16 慢查询日志 2 秒阈值 40 分钟：2,795 条里 2,782 条是这张表的 upsert，
执行 0.00 秒、等锁平均 2.58 秒。全表每 UTC 日一行，96 个并发预留者全在这一行上串行，
而门槛关着时全仓没有任何人读这行做决定。
"""
from datetime import date

import pytest
from sqlalchemy import create_engine, event, select
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session
from sqlalchemy.pool import StaticPool

import app.workflow_model_budget as budget
from app.database import Base
from app.models import WorkflowModelDailyBudget


@pytest.fixture
def db():
    engine = create_engine("sqlite://", connect_args={"check_same_thread": False}, poolclass=StaticPool)
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    session = Session(engine, expire_on_commit=False)
    statements = []
    event.listen(engine, "before_cursor_execute", lambda c, cur, s, *a: statements.append(s))
    try:
        yield session, statements
    finally:
        session.close()
        engine.dispose()


def _count(db, n, **kw):
    for _ in range(n):
        budget._enforce_incremental_daily_budget(db, **kw)
    db.commit()


def test_with_enforcement_off_the_ledger_counts_without_locking(db, monkeypatch):
    monkeypatch.setattr(budget, "INCREMENTAL_DAILY_BUDGET_ENFORCED", False)
    session, statements = db
    statements.clear()
    _count(session, 5, reserved_input=1000, reserved_output=100, estimated_cost=7)
    ledger_sql = [s for s in statements if "workflow_model_daily_budgets" in s]
    assert ledger_sql, "应当写了账本"
    assert not any("FOR UPDATE" in s.upper() for s in ledger_sql), "门槛关着时不许锁账本行"
    assert not any(s.lstrip().upper().startswith("SELECT") for s in ledger_sql), "门槛关着时不需要回读"
    row = session.scalar(select(WorkflowModelDailyBudget))
    assert (row.request_count, row.reserved_input_tokens, row.reserved_output_tokens, row.estimated_cost_micros) == (5, 5000, 500, 35)


def test_the_atomic_path_returns_todays_utc_date(db, monkeypatch):
    monkeypatch.setattr(budget, "INCREMENTAL_DAILY_BUDGET_ENFORCED", False)
    session, _ = db
    got = budget._enforce_incremental_daily_budget(session, reserved_input=1, reserved_output=1, estimated_cost=1)
    assert isinstance(got, date)


def test_with_enforcement_on_the_row_is_still_locked_and_the_limit_still_bites(db, monkeypatch):
    """开门槛必须先读再比，锁行是对的；这条保证我们没有顺手把门槛本身弄坏。"""

    monkeypatch.setattr(budget, "INCREMENTAL_DAILY_BUDGET_ENFORCED", True)
    monkeypatch.setattr(budget, "MAX_INCREMENTAL_REAL_DAILY_REQUESTS", 2)
    session, statements = db
    _count(session, 2, reserved_input=1, reserved_output=1, estimated_cost=1)
    assert any("FOR UPDATE" in s.upper() for s in statements if "workflow_model_daily_budgets" in s) or \
        session.get_bind().dialect.name == "sqlite", "非 SQLite 方言下开门槛应锁行"
    with pytest.raises(budget.WorkflowModelBudgetError) as exc:
        budget._enforce_incremental_daily_budget(session, reserved_input=1, reserved_output=1, estimated_cost=1)
    assert exc.value.code == "incremental_daily_requests_exhausted"
    session.rollback()
    row = session.scalar(select(WorkflowModelDailyBudget))
    assert row.request_count == 2, "被拒的那次不能记账"


def test_both_paths_land_on_the_same_row(db, monkeypatch):
    """开关切换不会造出第二行：同一 (日期, scope) 只有一条。"""

    session, _ = db
    monkeypatch.setattr(budget, "INCREMENTAL_DAILY_BUDGET_ENFORCED", False)
    _count(session, 3, reserved_input=1, reserved_output=1, estimated_cost=1)
    monkeypatch.setattr(budget, "INCREMENTAL_DAILY_BUDGET_ENFORCED", True)
    _count(session, 2, reserved_input=1, reserved_output=1, estimated_cost=1)
    rows = session.scalars(select(WorkflowModelDailyBudget)).all()
    assert len(rows) == 1 and rows[0].request_count == 5


def test_a_ledger_already_loaded_in_the_session_does_not_go_stale(db, monkeypatch):
    """test_incremental_autorun 抓到的回归：先走一次开门槛的锁读（对象进了 identity map），
    再关门槛走原子累加，随后不带 populate_existing 的 select 必须看到新计数，不能还回旧对象。"""

    session, _ = db
    monkeypatch.setattr(budget, "INCREMENTAL_DAILY_BUDGET_ENFORCED", True)
    monkeypatch.setattr(budget, "MAX_INCREMENTAL_REAL_DAILY_REQUESTS", 100)
    _count(session, 1, reserved_input=1, reserved_output=1, estimated_cost=1)
    cached = session.scalar(select(WorkflowModelDailyBudget))
    assert cached.request_count == 1
    monkeypatch.setattr(budget, "INCREMENTAL_DAILY_BUDGET_ENFORCED", False)
    _count(session, 1, reserved_input=1, reserved_output=1, estimated_cost=1)
    again = session.scalar(select(WorkflowModelDailyBudget))
    assert again.request_count == 2, "identity map 里的旧对象没有被刷新"


def test_reserve_counts_the_ledger_after_the_quota_pool_lock_not_before():
    """InnoDB 把 upsert 的行锁握到提交；放在预算投影之前就等于把整段 reserve 串行在
    「每 UTC 日一行」上。只记账路径必须排在 acquire_model_quota 之后、flush 之前。"""

    import inspect

    src = inspect.getsource(budget.reserve_model_invocation)
    quota_lock = src.rindex("acquire_model_quota(")
    ledger = src.rindex("_count_incremental_daily_ledger(")
    flush = src.rindex("db.flush()")
    assert quota_lock < ledger < flush, "只记账的 upsert 必须排在配额池加锁之后、flush 之前"
    early = src.index("_enforce_incremental_daily_budget(")
    assert "if INCREMENTAL_DAILY_BUDGET_ENFORCED" in src[:early], "开门槛时仍要先判再预留"

===== FILE backend/tests/test_daily_review_api.py =====
from __future__ import annotations

from datetime import date, datetime, timezone

from fastapi import FastAPI
from fastapi.testclient import TestClient
from sqlalchemy import create_engine, select
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session, sessionmaker
from sqlalchemy.pool import StaticPool

from app.daily_review_quota import build_daily_review_batch
from app.daily_review_selector import ReviewCandidate
from app.database import Base, get_db
from app.models import ConnectorPauseState, User


BUSINESS_DATE = date(2026, 8, 28)


def _client() -> tuple[TestClient, sessionmaker[Session], dict[str, str]]:
    from app.daily_review_api import build_daily_review_router

    engine = create_engine(
        "sqlite://",
        connect_args={"check_same_thread": False},
        poolclass=StaticPool,
    )
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    sessions = sessionmaker(bind=engine, expire_on_commit=False)
    role = {"value": "viewer"}

    def current_user() -> User:
        name = role["value"]
        return User(
            id=1,
            username=name,
            password_hash="x",
            display_name=name,
            role=name,
            is_admin=name == "admin",
        )

    def db_override():
        with sessions() as db:
            yield db

    app = FastAPI()
    app.include_router(
        build_daily_review_router(
            current_user,
            now=lambda: datetime(2026, 8, 28, 8, tzinfo=timezone.utc),
        )
    )
    app.dependency_overrides[get_db] = db_override
    return TestClient(app), sessions, role


def _candidate(number: int, *, redline: bool = False) -> ReviewCandidate:
    return ReviewCandidate(
        source_result_key=f"incremental:api:{number}",
        run_mode="incremental",
        category_key="knowledge_graph" if number % 2 else "inspiration_image",
        mechanism_version_key="workflow-version:17",
        result_stratum="risk",
        business_content_key=f"asset:{number}",
        preferred_bucket="risk_error",
        risk_severity=1 if redline else 100,
        confidence=0.5,
        impact_score=1.0,
        first_seen_at=datetime(2026, 8, 28, tzinfo=timezone.utc),
        reason_codes=("redline",) if redline else ("low_confidence",),
    )


def _seed_batch(sessions: sessionmaker[Session]) -> int:
    with sessions() as db:
        batch = build_daily_review_batch(
            db,
            BUSINESS_DATE,
            "quota-worker",
            candidates=tuple(
                [*(_candidate(index) for index in range(60)), _candidate(999, redline=True)]
            ),
        )
        db.commit()
        return batch.id


def test_today_payload_reconciles_persisted_batch_and_calls_overflow_unreviewed() -> None:
    client, sessions, _role = _client()
    try:
        batch_id = _seed_batch(sessions)

        response = client.get("/api/review-capacity/today")

        assert response.status_code == 200, response.text
        payload = response.json()
        assert payload["business_date"] == "2026-08-28"
        assert payload["capacity"] == 100
        assert payload["batch"]["id"] == batch_id
        assert payload["batch"]["selected_count"] == 60
        assert payload["batch"]["overflow_count"] == 1
        assert payload["progress"] == {"pending": 60, "reviewed": 0}
        assert payload["overflow"]["ordinary"] == 0
        assert payload["overflow"]["severe"] == 1
        assert payload["not_selected_label"] == "未抽查"
        assert payload["pauses"][0]["state_label"] == "已暂停"
        assert all(
            item["state_label"] in {"待审核", "已审核", "未抽查"}
            for item in payload["items"]
        )
        assert all(item["evaluation_id"] is None for item in payload["items"])
    finally:
        client.close()


def test_review_queue_exposes_only_valid_evaluation_result_identifiers() -> None:
    from app.daily_review_api import _evaluation_id_from_key

    assert _evaluation_id_from_key("evaluation-result:42") == 42
    assert _evaluation_id_from_key("evaluation-result:0") is None
    assert _evaluation_id_from_key("evaluation-result:not-a-number") is None
    assert _evaluation_id_from_key("incremental:result:42") is None


def test_generate_is_idempotent_and_requires_review_write_permission() -> None:
    client, sessions, role = _client()
    try:
        batch_id = _seed_batch(sessions)

        forbidden = client.post("/api/review-capacity/2026-08-28/generate")
        assert forbidden.status_code == 403
        assert forbidden.json()["detail"]["code"] == "review_capacity_write_forbidden"

        role["value"] = "admin"
        first = client.post("/api/review-capacity/2026-08-28/generate")
        second = client.post("/api/review-capacity/2026-08-28/generate")
        assert first.status_code == 200, first.text
        assert second.status_code == 200, second.text
        assert first.json()["batch"]["id"] == batch_id
        assert second.json()["batch"]["id"] == batch_id
        with sessions() as db:
            assert len(list(db.scalars(select(ConnectorPauseState)).all())) == 1
    finally:
        client.close()


def test_only_admin_can_resume_review_capacity_pause_with_reason() -> None:
    client, sessions, role = _client()
    try:
        _seed_batch(sessions)
        with sessions() as db:
            pause = db.scalar(select(ConnectorPauseState))
            assert pause is not None
            pause_id = pause.id

        role["value"] = "category_manager"
        forbidden = client.post(
            f"/api/review-capacity/pauses/{pause_id}/resume",
            json={"reason": "已经核对风险"},
        )
        assert forbidden.status_code == 403
        assert forbidden.json()["detail"]["code"] == "review_capacity_manage_forbidden"

        role["value"] = "admin"
        invalid = client.post(
            f"/api/review-capacity/pauses/{pause_id}/resume",
            json={"reason": " "},
        )
        assert invalid.status_code == 422

        resumed = client.post(
            f"/api/review-capacity/pauses/{pause_id}/resume",
            json={"reason": "已处理红线异常，批准恢复增量评测"},
        )
        assert resumed.status_code == 200, resumed.text
        assert resumed.json()["state"] == "active"
        assert resumed.json()["state_label"] == "已恢复"
    finally:
        client.close()

from __future__ import annotations

import json
import subprocess
import sys
from pathlib import Path


ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2]


def test_daily_review_acceptance_report_reconciles_capacity_and_overflow() -> None:
    completed = subprocess.run(
        [sys.executable, str(ROOT / "scripts" / "daily-review-capacity-acceptance.py"), "--json"],
        cwd=ROOT,
        check=True,
        capture_output=True,
        text=True,
    )
    report = json.loads(completed.stdout)

    assert report["status"] == "passed"
    assert report["business_date"] == "2026-08-28"
    assert report["candidate_count"] == 140
    assert report["selected_count"] == 100
    assert report["overflow_count"] == 40
    assert report["duplicate_count"] == 1
    assert report["bucket_counts"] == {
        "risk_error": 60,
        "cold_start_drift": 20,
        "stratified_random": 15,
        "golden_sentinel": 5,
    }
    assert report["database_api_delta"] == 0
    assert report["selection_hash"]
    assert report["policy_hash"]
    assert report["pause_scopes"] == [
        {
            "category_key": "knowledge_graph",
            "mechanism_key": "",
            "target_contract_key": "*",
        }
    ]
    assert report["audit_ids"]
    assert report["scenarios"] == {
        "all_risk_hard_cap": True,
        "cross_midnight_independent_dates": True,
        "duplicate_source_suppressed": True,
        "severe_overflow_scoped_pause": True,
        "under_capacity_no_overflow": True,
    }

===== FILE backend/tests/test_daily_review_overflow.py =====
from __future__ import annotations

from datetime import date, datetime, timezone

import pytest
from sqlalchemy import create_engine, func, select
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from app.audit import canonical_json
from app.daily_review_quota import build_daily_review_batch
from app.daily_review_selector import ReviewCandidate
from app.database import Base
from app.models import (
    ConnectorPauseState,
    HumanReviewOverflowFact,
    RuntimeAuditEvent,
    WorkflowDefinition,
    WorkflowVersion,
)


def _workflow_version(
    db: Session,
    *,
    category_key: str,
    suffix: str,
) -> WorkflowVersion:
    definition = WorkflowDefinition(
        workflow_key=f"review-overflow.{suffix}",
        name=f"Review overflow {suffix}",
        description="",
        owner="test",
        allowed_categories_json=canonical_json([category_key]),
        status="active",
        created_by="test",
    )
    db.add(definition)
    db.flush()
    version = WorkflowVersion(
        workflow_definition_id=definition.id,
        version="native-v1",
        status="active",
        workflow_schema_version="workflow-v3",
        step_manifest_json="[]",
        edge_manifest_json="[]",
        input_schema_json='{"type":"object"}',
        output_schema_json='{"type":"object"}',
        resource_policy_json=canonical_json(
            {
                "category_key": category_key,
                "lane_key": "incremental",
                "run_modes": ["incremental"],
                "queue_class": "production_batch",
                "layout": {},
            }
        ),
        canonical_hash=(suffix[0] * 64),
        validation_report_json='{"ok":true,"errors":[]}',
        created_by="test",
    )
    db.add(version)
    db.flush()
    return version


def _candidate(
    number: int,
    *,
    category_key: str,
    version: WorkflowVersion,
    reason_codes: tuple[str, ...],
    risk_severity: int,
) -> ReviewCandidate:
    return ReviewCandidate(
        source_result_key=f"incremental:overflow:{category_key}:{number}",
        run_mode="incremental",
        category_key=category_key,
        mechanism_version_key=f"workflow-version:{version.id}",
        result_stratum="risk",
        business_content_key=f"asset:{category_key}:{number}",
        preferred_bucket="risk_error",
        risk_severity=risk_severity,
        confidence=0.5,
        impact_score=1.0,
        first_seen_at=datetime(2026, 8, 28, tzinfo=timezone.utc),
        reason_codes=reason_codes,
        mechanism_version_id=version.id,
    )


def _risk_candidates(
    version: WorkflowVersion,
    *,
    category_key: str,
    overflow_reason: tuple[str, ...],
) -> tuple[ReviewCandidate, ...]:
    selected = [
        _candidate(
            number,
            category_key=category_key,
            version=version,
            reason_codes=("low_confidence",),
            risk_severity=100,
        )
        for number in range(60)
    ]
    overflow = _candidate(
        999,
        category_key=category_key,
        version=version,
        reason_codes=overflow_reason,
        risk_severity=1,
    )
    return (*selected, overflow)


def test_ordinary_overflow_is_unreviewed_without_pausing() -> None:
    engine = create_engine("sqlite://")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    try:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            version = _workflow_version(
                db,
                category_key="knowledge_graph",
                suffix="a",
            )
            batch = build_daily_review_batch(
                db,
                date(2026, 8, 28),
                "quota-worker",
                candidates=_risk_candidates(
                    version,
                    category_key="knowledge_graph",
                    overflow_reason=("low_confidence",),
                ),
            )
            db.commit()

            overflow = db.scalar(
                select(HumanReviewOverflowFact).where(
                    HumanReviewOverflowFact.batch_id == batch.id
                )
            )
            assert overflow is not None
            assert overflow.severity == "ordinary"
            assert overflow.pause_state_id is None
            assert db.scalar(select(func.count(ConnectorPauseState.id))) == 0
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def test_redline_overflow_pauses_only_affected_category_and_mechanism() -> None:
    engine = create_engine("sqlite://")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    try:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            affected = _workflow_version(
                db,
                category_key="knowledge_graph",
                suffix="b",
            )
            unrelated = _workflow_version(
                db,
                category_key="inspiration_image",
                suffix="c",
            )
            batch = build_daily_review_batch(
                db,
                date(2026, 8, 28),
                "quota-worker",
                candidates=(
                    *_risk_candidates(
                        affected,
                        category_key="knowledge_graph",
                        overflow_reason=("redline",),
                    ),
                    _candidate(
                        1,
                        category_key="inspiration_image",
                        version=unrelated,
                        reason_codes=("low_confidence",),
                        risk_severity=99,
                    ),
                ),
            )
            db.commit()

            pauses = list(db.scalars(select(ConnectorPauseState)).all())
            assert len(pauses) == 1
            pause = pauses[0]
            assert pause.category_key == "knowledge_graph"
            assert pause.mechanism_key == f"workflow-version:{affected.id}"
            assert pause.target_contract_key == "*"
            assert pause.state == "paused"
            assert pause.reason_code == "human_review_severe_overflow"
            overflow = db.scalar(
                select(HumanReviewOverflowFact).where(
                    HumanReviewOverflowFact.batch_id == batch.id,
                    HumanReviewOverflowFact.severity == "severe",
                )
            )
            assert overflow is not None
            assert overflow.pause_state_id == pause.id
            audit = db.scalar(
                select(RuntimeAuditEvent).where(
                    RuntimeAuditEvent.action == "incremental_review_overflow_paused"
                )
            )
            assert audit is not None
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def test_overflow_resume_requires_reason_and_is_audited() -> None:
    from app.daily_review_quota import resume_incremental_after_review_overflow

    engine = create_engine("sqlite://")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    try:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            version = _workflow_version(
                db,
                category_key="knowledge_graph",
                suffix="d",
            )
            build_daily_review_batch(
                db,
                date(2026, 8, 28),
                "quota-worker",
                candidates=_risk_candidates(
                    version,
                    category_key="knowledge_graph",
                    overflow_reason=("redline",),
                ),
            )
            db.commit()
            pause = db.scalar(select(ConnectorPauseState))
            assert pause is not None

            with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="恢复原因不能为空"):
                resume_incremental_after_review_overflow(
                    db,
                    pause.id,
                    "capacity-admin",
                    "   ",
                )

            resumed = resume_incremental_after_review_overflow(
                db,
                pause.id,
                "capacity-admin",
                "已处理红线异常，批准恢复自动交付",
            )
            db.commit()
            assert resumed.state == "active"
            assert resumed.resumed_by == "capacity-admin"
            assert resumed.resume_reason == "已处理红线异常，批准恢复自动交付"
            actions = list(
                db.scalars(
                    select(RuntimeAuditEvent.action).order_by(RuntimeAuditEvent.id)
                ).all()
            )
            assert actions == [
                "incremental_review_overflow_paused",
                "incremental_review_overflow_resumed",
            ]
    finally:
        engine.dispose()

===== FILE backend/tests/test_daily_review_quota.py =====
from __future__ import annotations

from datetime import date, datetime, timezone
from concurrent.futures import ThreadPoolExecutor
from threading import Barrier
from pathlib import Path

import pytest
from sqlalchemy import create_engine, func, select
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session
from sqlalchemy.engine import Engine

from app import models
from app.daily_review_selector import ReviewCandidate
from app.database import Base


def _candidate(number: int, *, bucket: str) -> ReviewCandidate:
    return ReviewCandidate(
        source_result_key=f"incremental:result:{number}",
        run_mode="incremental",
        category_key="knowledge_graph" if number % 2 else "inspiration_image",
        mechanism_version_key="native-v1",
        result_stratum=f"stratum-{number % 3}",
        business_content_key=f"asset-{number}",
        preferred_bucket=bucket,
        risk_severity=100 - (number % 10),
        confidence=0.5 + (number % 10) / 100,
        impact_score=1000 - number,
        first_seen_at=datetime(2026, 8, 28, tzinfo=timezone.utc),
        reason_codes=(bucket,),
    )


def _candidates() -> tuple[ReviewCandidate, ...]:
    return tuple([
        *[_candidate(index, bucket="risk_error") for index in range(70)],
        *[_candidate(index, bucket="cold_start_drift") for index in range(100, 130)],
        *[_candidate(index, bucket="stratified_random") for index in range(200, 230)],
        *[_candidate(index, bucket="golden_sentinel") for index in range(300, 310)],
    ])


def _engine(tmp_path: Path):
    engine = create_engine(f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / 'daily-review.db'}")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    return engine


def test_business_date_uses_asia_shanghai_midnight() -> None:
    from app.daily_review_quota import business_date_at

    assert business_date_at(datetime(2026, 8, 28, 15, 59, 59, tzinfo=timezone.utc)) == date(2026, 8, 28)
    assert business_date_at(datetime(2026, 8, 28, 16, 0, 0, tzinfo=timezone.utc)) == date(2026, 8, 29)


def test_daily_batch_is_idempotent_and_persists_selected_and_unselected_facts(
    tmp_path: Path,
) -> None:
    from app.daily_review_quota import build_daily_review_batch

    engine = _engine(tmp_path)
    try:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            first = build_daily_review_batch(
                db,
                business_date=date(2026, 8, 28),
                actor="operator",
                candidates=_candidates(),
            )
            db.commit()
            first_id = first.id
            first_hash = first.selection_hash

        with Session(engine) as db:
            replay = build_daily_review_batch(
                db,
                business_date=date(2026, 8, 28),
                actor="another-operator",
                candidates=tuple(reversed(_candidates())),
            )
            db.commit()
            assert replay.id == first_id
            assert replay.selection_hash == first_hash
            assert replay.selected_count == 100
            assert replay.overflow_count == 40
            assert db.scalar(
                select(func.count(models.HumanReviewDailyBatchItem.id)).where(
                    models.HumanReviewDailyBatchItem.batch_id == replay.id,
                    models.HumanReviewDailyBatchItem.selection_state == "selected_pending",
                )
            ) == 100
            assert db.scalar(
                select(func.count(models.HumanReviewDailyBatchItem.id)).where(
                    models.HumanReviewDailyBatchItem.batch_id == replay.id,
                    models.HumanReviewDailyBatchItem.selection_state == "not_selected",
                )
            ) == 40
            assert db.scalar(
                select(func.count(models.HumanReviewOverflowFact.id)).where(
                    models.HumanReviewOverflowFact.batch_id == replay.id,
                )
            ) == 40
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def test_batch_build_failure_leaves_no_partial_facts(monkeypatch, tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    import app.daily_review_quota as quota

    engine = _engine(tmp_path)
    try:
        def fail_selection(*_args, **_kwargs):
            raise RuntimeError("selection failed")

        monkeypatch.setattr(quota, "select_daily_review_candidates", fail_selection)
        with Session(engine) as db:
            with pytest.raises(RuntimeError, match="selection failed"):
                quota.build_daily_review_batch(
                    db,
                    business_date=date(2026, 8, 28),
                    actor="operator",
                    candidates=_candidates(),
                )
            db.rollback()
        with Session(engine) as db:
            assert db.scalar(select(func.count(models.HumanReviewDailyBatch.id))) == 0
            assert db.scalar(select(func.count(models.HumanReviewDailyBatchItem.id))) == 0
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def test_each_business_date_gets_an_independent_batch(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    from app.daily_review_quota import build_daily_review_batch

    engine = _engine(tmp_path)
    try:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            first = build_daily_review_batch(
                db,
                business_date=date(2026, 8, 28),
                actor="operator",
                candidates=_candidates(),
            )
            second = build_daily_review_batch(
                db,
                business_date=date(2026, 8, 29),
                actor="operator",
                candidates=_candidates(),
            )
            db.commit()
            assert first.id != second.id
            assert first.business_date != second.business_date
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def test_eight_mysql_builders_create_one_formal_daily_batch(
    mysql_engine: Engine,
) -> None:
    from app.daily_review_quota import build_daily_review_batch
    from app.migrations.mysql_baseline import create_mysql_baseline

    with mysql_engine.begin() as connection:
        create_mysql_baseline(connection, install_invariants=False)
    barrier = Barrier(8)

    def build(worker_number: int) -> tuple[int, str]:
        barrier.wait(timeout=10)
        with Session(mysql_engine) as db:
            batch = build_daily_review_batch(
                db,
                business_date=date(2026, 8, 28),
                actor=f"quota-worker-{worker_number}",
                candidates=_candidates(),
            )
            db.commit()
            return batch.id, batch.selection_hash

    with ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=8) as executor:
        results = list(executor.map(build, range(8)))

    assert len({item[0] for item in results}) == 1
    assert len({item[1] for item in results}) == 1
    with Session(mysql_engine) as db:
        assert db.scalar(select(func.count(models.HumanReviewDailyBatch.id))) == 1
        assert db.scalar(select(func.count(models.HumanReviewDailyBatchItem.id))) == 140
        assert db.scalar(select(func.count(models.HumanReviewOverflowFact.id))) == 40

===== FILE backend/tests/test_daily_review_quota_migration.py =====
from __future__ import annotations

from datetime import date
from pathlib import Path

import pytest
from sqlalchemy import create_engine, inspect
from sqlalchemy.exc import IntegrityError
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from app import models
from app.database import Base
from app.migrations import runner as migration_runner


def _policy(**overrides: object):
    values = {
        "version": 1,
        "timezone_name": "Asia/Shanghai",
        "reviewer_count": 1,
        "per_reviewer_capacity": 100,
        "total_capacity": 100,
        "risk_error_capacity": 60,
        "cold_start_drift_capacity": 20,
        "stratified_random_capacity": 15,
        "golden_sentinel_capacity": 5,
        "severe_overflow_threshold": 1,
        "severe_overflow_rules_json": '{"redline":true}',
        "status": "active",
        "active_slot": "global",
        "version_hash": "a" * 64,
        "created_by": "tester",
    }
    values.update(overrides)
    return models.HumanReviewQuotaPolicy(**values)


def test_schema_84_remains_recorded_after_later_additive_migrations(
    tmp_path: Path,
) -> None:
    engine = create_engine(f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / 'review-v84.db'}")
    try:
        Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
        with engine.begin() as connection:
            migration_runner.run_migrations(connection)
            assert connection.exec_driver_sql(
                "SELECT max(version) FROM schema_migrations"
            ).scalar_one() == max(
                migration.version for migration in migration_runner.MIGRATIONS
            )
            assert connection.exec_driver_sql(
                "SELECT name FROM schema_migrations WHERE version = 84"
            ).scalar_one() == "add_daily_human_review_quota"
            assert {
                "human_review_quota_policies",
                "human_review_daily_batches",
                "human_review_daily_batch_items",
                "human_review_overflow_facts",
            } <= set(inspect(connection).get_table_names())
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def test_only_one_review_quota_policy_can_be_active() -> None:
    engine = create_engine("sqlite://")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    try:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            db.add_all([_policy(), _policy(version=2, version_hash="b" * 64)])
            with pytest.raises(IntegrityError):
                db.commit()
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def test_seed_defaults_materializes_exactly_one_active_100_item_policy() -> None:
    from app.seed import seed_defaults

    engine = create_engine("sqlite://")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    try:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            seed_defaults(db)
            seed_defaults(db)
            policies = db.query(models.HumanReviewQuotaPolicy).all()

            assert len(policies) == 1
            policy = policies[0]
            assert policy.status == "active"
            assert policy.active_slot == "global"
            assert policy.reviewer_count == 1
            assert policy.per_reviewer_capacity == 100
            assert policy.total_capacity == 100
            assert (
                policy.risk_error_capacity
                + policy.cold_start_drift_capacity
                + policy.stratified_random_capacity
                + policy.golden_sentinel_capacity
            ) == 100
    finally:
