def test_review_batch_enforces_daily_identity_and_capacity() -> None:
    engine = create_engine("sqlite://")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    try:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            policy = _policy()
            db.add(policy)
            db.flush()
            common = {
                "business_date": date(2026, 8, 28),
                "policy_id": policy.id,
                "candidate_set_hash": "c" * 64,
                "selection_hash": "d" * 64,
                "capacity": 100,
                "selected_count": 100,
                "overflow_count": 20,
                "bucket_counts_json": '{"risk_error":60,"cold_start_drift":20,"stratified_random":15,"golden_sentinel":5}',
                "category_coverage_json": "{}",
                "status": "finalized",
                "created_by": "tester",
            }
            db.add_all([
                models.HumanReviewDailyBatch(**common),
                models.HumanReviewDailyBatch(**{**common, "selection_hash": "e" * 64}),
            ])
            with pytest.raises(IntegrityError):
                db.commit()
            db.rollback()

            db.add(policy := _policy(version=2, version_hash="b" * 64))
            policy.status = "retired"
            policy.active_slot = None
            db.flush()
            db.add(models.HumanReviewDailyBatch(**{
                **common,
                "business_date": date(2026, 8, 29),
                "policy_id": policy.id,
                "selected_count": 101,
            }))
            with pytest.raises(IntegrityError):
                db.commit()
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def test_review_item_identity_bucket_and_state_are_checked() -> None:
    engine = create_engine("sqlite://")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    try:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            policy = _policy()
            db.add(policy)
            db.flush()
            batch = models.HumanReviewDailyBatch(
                business_date=date(2026, 8, 28),
                policy_id=policy.id,
                candidate_set_hash="c" * 64,
                selection_hash="d" * 64,
                capacity=100,
                selected_count=1,
                overflow_count=0,
                bucket_counts_json='{"risk_error":1}',
                category_coverage_json='{"knowledge_graph":1}',
                status="finalized",
                created_by="tester",
            )
            db.add(batch)
            db.flush()
            common = {
                "batch_id": batch.id,
                "business_date": batch.business_date,
                "source_result_key": "incremental:result:1",
                "category_key": "knowledge_graph",
                "mechanism_version_id": None,
                "bucket": "risk_error",
                "stable_rank": 1,
                "reason_codes_json": '["redline"]',
                "selection_state": "selected_pending",
            }
            db.add_all([
                models.HumanReviewDailyBatchItem(**common),
                models.HumanReviewDailyBatchItem(**common),
            ])
            with pytest.raises(IntegrityError):
                db.commit()
            db.rollback()

            policy = _policy(version=2, version_hash="b" * 64)
            policy.status = "retired"
            policy.active_slot = None
            db.add(policy)
            db.flush()
            batch = models.HumanReviewDailyBatch(
                business_date=date(2026, 8, 29),
                policy_id=policy.id,
                candidate_set_hash="e" * 64,
                selection_hash="f" * 64,
                capacity=100,
                selected_count=1,
                overflow_count=0,
                bucket_counts_json="{}",
                category_coverage_json="{}",
                status="finalized",
                created_by="tester",
            )
            db.add(batch)
            db.flush()
            db.add(models.HumanReviewDailyBatchItem(
                batch_id=batch.id,
                business_date=batch.business_date,
                source_result_key="incremental:result:2",
                category_key="knowledge_graph",
                bucket="unknown",
                stable_rank=0,
                reason_codes_json="[]",
                selection_state="approved_without_review",
            ))
            with pytest.raises(IntegrityError):
                db.commit()
    finally:
        engine.dispose()

===== FILE backend/tests/test_daily_review_selector.py =====
from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import replace
from datetime import date, datetime, timezone
from itertools import permutations

from app.daily_review_selector import (
    ReviewCandidate,
    ReviewQuotaPolicy,
    select_daily_review_candidates,
)


POLICY = ReviewQuotaPolicy(
    total_capacity=100,
    risk_error_capacity=60,
    cold_start_drift_capacity=20,
    stratified_random_capacity=15,
    golden_sentinel_capacity=5,
)


def _candidate(
    number: int,
    *,
    bucket: str = "stratified_random",
    category: str = "knowledge_graph",
    mechanism: str = "native-v1",
    result_stratum: str = "valid",
    run_mode: str = "incremental",
    reviewed: bool = False,
) -> ReviewCandidate:
    return ReviewCandidate(
        source_result_key=f"incremental:result:{number}",
        run_mode=run_mode,
        category_key=category,
        mechanism_version_key=mechanism,
        result_stratum=result_stratum,
        business_content_key=f"asset-{number}",
        preferred_bucket=bucket,
        risk_severity=100 - (number % 10),
        confidence=(number % 100) / 100,
        impact_score=1000 - number,
        first_seen_at=datetime(2026, 8, 1, tzinfo=timezone.utc),
        reason_codes=(bucket,),
        already_reviewed=reviewed,
    )


def test_selector_allocates_exact_four_bucket_quota() -> None:
    candidates = [
        *[_candidate(index, bucket="risk_error") for index in range(0, 70)],
        *[_candidate(index, bucket="cold_start_drift") for index in range(100, 130)],
        *[_candidate(index, bucket="stratified_random") for index in range(200, 230)],
        *[_candidate(index, bucket="golden_sentinel") for index in range(300, 310)],
    ]

    selection = select_daily_review_candidates(POLICY, date(2026, 8, 28), candidates)

    assert selection.bucket_counts == {
        "risk_error": 60,
        "cold_start_drift": 20,
        "stratified_random": 15,
        "golden_sentinel": 5,
    }
    assert len(selection.selected) == 100
    assert len({item.candidate.source_result_key for item in selection.selected}) == 100
    assert all(item.reason_code != "human_approved" for item in selection.unselected)
    assert {item.reason_code for item in selection.unselected} == {
        "not_selected_capacity"
    }


def test_unused_capacity_flows_forward_only() -> None:
    candidates = [
        *[_candidate(index, bucket="risk_error") for index in range(0, 10)],
        *[_candidate(index, bucket="cold_start_drift") for index in range(100, 110)],
        *[_candidate(index, bucket="stratified_random") for index in range(200, 210)],
        *[_candidate(index, bucket="golden_sentinel") for index in range(300, 400)],
    ]

    selection = select_daily_review_candidates(POLICY, date(2026, 8, 28), candidates)

    assert selection.bucket_counts == {
        "risk_error": 10,
        "cold_start_drift": 10,
        "stratified_random": 10,
        "golden_sentinel": 70,
    }
    assert len(selection.selected) == 100


def test_selector_suppresses_duplicates_reviewed_and_non_incremental_inputs() -> None:
    shared = _candidate(1, bucket="risk_error")
    candidates = [
        shared,
        replace(shared, confidence=0.99),
        _candidate(2, reviewed=True),
        _candidate(3, run_mode="stock_regression"),
        _candidate(4, run_mode="correction"),
        _candidate(5),
    ]

    selection = select_daily_review_candidates(POLICY, date(2026, 8, 28), candidates)

    assert [item.candidate.source_result_key for item in selection.selected] == [
        "incremental:result:1",
        "incremental:result:5",
    ]
    assert selection.duplicate_count == 1
    assert selection.excluded_count == 3


def test_stratified_random_covers_available_category_mechanism_result_strata() -> None:
    candidates = [
        _candidate(
            index,
            category=f"category-{index % 3}",
            mechanism=f"mechanism-{index % 2}",
            result_stratum=f"result-{index % 2}",
        )
        for index in range(12)
    ]

    selection = select_daily_review_candidates(POLICY, date(2026, 8, 28), candidates)

    expected = {
        (
            item.category_key,
            item.mechanism_version_key,
            item.result_stratum,
        )
        for item in candidates
    }
    actual = {
        (
            item.candidate.category_key,
            item.candidate.mechanism_version_key,
            item.candidate.result_stratum,
        )
        for item in selection.selected
    }
    assert actual == expected


def test_selection_is_stable_across_input_order_and_never_exceeds_capacity() -> None:
    candidates = [
        _candidate(index, bucket=(
            "risk_error" if index < 3 else
            "cold_start_drift" if index < 5 else
            "stratified_random"
        ))
        for index in range(8)
    ]
    baseline = select_daily_review_candidates(POLICY, date(2026, 8, 28), candidates)

    for ordering in list(permutations(candidates[:5]))[:20]:
        variant = select_daily_review_candidates(
            POLICY,
            date(2026, 8, 28),
            [*ordering, *candidates[5:]],
        )
        assert variant.selection_hash == baseline.selection_hash
        assert variant.candidate_set_hash == baseline.candidate_set_hash
        assert [item.candidate.source_result_key for item in variant.selected] == [
            item.candidate.source_result_key for item in baseline.selected
        ]
        assert len(variant.selected) <= POLICY.total_capacity

===== FILE backend/tests/test_dashboard_summary.py =====
from __future__ import annotations

import json
from datetime import datetime, timedelta, timezone

from sqlalchemy import create_engine, event
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session
from sqlalchemy.pool import StaticPool

from app.dashboard_summary import dashboard_evaluation_summary, dashboard_final_level
from app.database import Base
from app.models import Asset, EvaluationJob, EvaluationResult, HumanReview, ReviewPanel


def _result(
    *,
    asset: Asset,
    job: EvaluationJob,
    level: str,
    created_at: datetime,
    needs_review: bool = False,
) -> EvaluationResult:
    return EvaluationResult(
        asset_id=asset.id,
        job_id=job.id,
        precheck_json="{}",
        scoring_json=json.dumps({"level": level}),
        raw_response_a="{}",
        score=70,
        level=level,
        confidence=0.9,
        needs_review=needs_review,
        model_id="dashboard-test-model",
        prompt_a_version="A1",
        rubric_version="R1",
        engine_version="E1",
        created_at=created_at,
    )


def test_dashboard_summary_uses_latest_result_and_bounded_queries() -> None:
    engine = create_engine(
        "sqlite://",
        connect_args={"check_same_thread": False},
        poolclass=StaticPool,
    )
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    db = Session(engine, expire_on_commit=False)
    base_time = datetime(2026, 8, 29, tzinfo=timezone.utc)

    assets: list[Asset] = []
    latest_results: list[EvaluationResult] = []
    for index in range(12):
        asset = Asset(
            original_name=f"dashboard-{index}.jpg",
            stored_name=f"dashboard-{index}.jpg",
            mime_type="image/jpeg",
            size_bytes=10,
            sha256=f"{index + 1:064x}",
        )
        db.add(asset)
        db.flush()
        job = EvaluationJob(
            asset_id=asset.id,
            status="completed",
            stage="done",
            progress=100,
        )
        db.add(job)
        db.flush()
        result = _result(
            asset=asset,
            job=job,
            level="L3",
            needs_review=index % 2 == 0,
            created_at=base_time + timedelta(minutes=index),
        )
        db.add(result)
        assets.append(asset)
        latest_results.append(result)

    db.flush()
    old_job = EvaluationJob(
        asset_id=assets[0].id,
        status="completed",
        stage="done",
        progress=100,
    )
    db.add(old_job)
    db.flush()
    db.add(
        _result(
            asset=assets[0],
            job=old_job,
            level="L1",
            created_at=base_time - timedelta(days=1),
        )
    )
    latest_results[0].review_stage = "completed"
    db.add(
        HumanReview(
            evaluation_id=latest_results[0].id,
            reviewer_name="dashboard-reviewer",
            decision="corrected",
            corrected_level="L4",
            corrected_score=40,
            corrections_json="[]",
        )
    )
    db.commit()
    db.expunge_all()

    statements: list[str] = []

    def count_statement(
        _connection: object,
        _cursor: object,
        statement: str,
        _parameters: object,
        _context: object,
        _executemany: bool,
    ) -> None:
        statements.append(statement)

    event.listen(engine, "before_cursor_execute", count_statement)
    try:
        summary = dashboard_evaluation_summary(db)
    finally:
        event.remove(engine, "before_cursor_execute", count_statement)

    assert summary.result_count == 12
    assert summary.needs_review == 6
    assert summary.levels == {"L3": 11, "L4": 1}
    assert len(statements) <= 3, "仪表盘查询数不能随评测结果数量线性增长"

    db.close()
    engine.dispose()


def test_dashboard_preserves_review_round_visibility_and_completed_priority():
    engine = create_engine("sqlite://")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    with Session(engine) as db:
        asset = Asset(original_name="panel.jpg", stored_name="panel.jpg", mime_type="image/jpeg",
                      size_bytes=10, sha256="a" * 64)
        db.add(asset); db.flush()
        job = EvaluationJob(asset_id=asset.id, status="completed", stage="done", progress=100)
        db.add(job); db.flush()
        now = datetime(2026, 9, 21, tzinfo=timezone.utc)
        result = _result(asset=asset, job=job, level="L3", created_at=now)
        db.add(result); db.flush()
        panel = ReviewPanel(evaluation_id=result.id, review_round=2, status="collecting")
        db.add(panel); db.flush()
        # An unfinished current round must not replace the last visible verdict.
        db.add_all([
            HumanReview(evaluation_id=result.id, reviewer_name="previous", decision="corrected",
                        corrected_level="L4", panel_id=panel.id, review_round=1, created_at=now),
            HumanReview(evaluation_id=result.id, reviewer_name="current", decision="corrected",
                        corrected_level="L2", panel_id=panel.id, review_round=2, created_at=now + timedelta(minutes=1)),
            HumanReview(evaluation_id=result.id, reviewer_name="standalone", decision="corrected",
                        corrected_level="L1", created_at=now + timedelta(minutes=2)),
        ])
        db.commit(); db.expire_all()
        for status, expected in [("collecting", "L1"), ("lead_adjudication", "L1"), ("completed", "L2")]:
            panel.status = status
            db.commit(); db.expire_all()
            assert dashboard_final_level(result) == expected
            assert dashboard_evaluation_summary(db).levels == {expected: 1}
    engine.dispose()

===== FILE backend/tests/test_database_capabilities.py =====
from __future__ import annotations

from types import SimpleNamespace

import pytest
from sqlalchemy import create_engine, select
from sqlalchemy.dialects import mysql, postgresql
from sqlalchemy.exc import OperationalError
from pymysql.err import OperationalError as PyMySQLOperationalError

from app.database import configure_mysql_dbapi_connection, engine_options
from app import database as database_module
from app import database_capabilities
from app.database_capabilities import (
    DatabaseCapabilities,
    DatabaseDialectError,
)
from app.models import RuntimeDispatchItem


class _ScalarResult:
    def __init__(self, value: object):
        self._value = value

    def scalar_one(self) -> object:
        return self._value

    def one(self):
        return self._value


class _MySQLConnectionStub:
    dialect = SimpleNamespace(name="mysql")

    def __init__(self, responses: dict[str, object]):
        self.responses = responses
        self.statements: list[str] = []

    def exec_driver_sql(self, statement: str) -> _ScalarResult:
        self.statements.append(statement)
        if statement.startswith("SELECT VERSION(),"):
            return _ScalarResult(tuple(
                self.responses["SELECT " + expression.strip()]
                for expression in statement.removeprefix("SELECT ").split(",")
            ))
        return _ScalarResult(self.responses[statement])


def _valid_mysql_runtime_responses() -> dict[str, object]:
    return {
        "SELECT VERSION()": "8.4.11",
        "SELECT @@version_comment": "MySQL Community Server - GPL",
        "SELECT @@global.read_only": 0,
        "SELECT @@session.default_storage_engine": "InnoDB",
        "SELECT @@session.transaction_isolation": "READ-COMMITTED",
        "SELECT @@session.time_zone": "+00:00",
        "SELECT @@character_set_client": "utf8mb4",
        "SELECT @@character_set_connection": "utf8mb4",
        "SELECT @@character_set_results": "utf8mb4",
        "SELECT @@session.sql_mode": (
            "STRICT_TRANS_TABLES,ERROR_FOR_DIVISION_BY_ZERO,"
            "NO_ENGINE_SUBSTITUTION"
        ),
        "SELECT DATABASE()": "labels",
        "SELECT @@collation_database": "utf8mb4_0900_as_cs",
        (
            "SELECT COUNT(*) FROM information_schema.TABLES "
            "WHERE TABLE_SCHEMA = DATABASE() AND TABLE_TYPE = 'BASE TABLE' "
            "AND ENGINE <> 'InnoDB'"
        ): 0,
    }


class _RecordingCursor:
    def __init__(self, statements: list[str]):
        self.statements = statements

    def execute(self, statement: str) -> None:
        self.statements.append(statement)

    def close(self) -> None:
        self.statements.append("CLOSE")


class _RecordingDbapiConnection:
    def __init__(self):
        self.statements: list[str] = []

    def cursor(self) -> _RecordingCursor:
        return _RecordingCursor(self.statements)


def test_postgres_claim_uses_row_lock_and_skip_locked() -> None:
    statement = DatabaseCapabilities.for_dialect(
        "postgresql"
    ).claim_lock_statement(
        select(RuntimeDispatchItem).where(RuntimeDispatchItem.status == "queued")
    )

    compiled = str(statement.compile(dialect=postgresql.dialect()))

    assert "FOR UPDATE SKIP LOCKED" in compiled


def test_mysql_claim_uses_row_lock_and_skip_locked() -> None:
    capabilities = DatabaseCapabilities.for_dialect("mysql")
    statement = capabilities.claim_lock_statement(
        select(RuntimeDispatchItem).where(RuntimeDispatchItem.status == "queued")
    )

    compiled = str(statement.compile(dialect=mysql.dialect()))

    assert capabilities.serializes_claims is False
    assert "FOR UPDATE SKIP LOCKED" in compiled


def test_sqlite_claim_keeps_serialized_immediate_transaction() -> None:
    capabilities = DatabaseCapabilities.for_dialect("sqlite")

    statement = capabilities.claim_lock_statement(select(RuntimeDispatchItem))

    assert capabilities.serializes_claims is True
    assert "FOR UPDATE" not in str(statement)


def test_unknown_database_dialect_fails_closed() -> None:
    with pytest.raises(DatabaseDialectError, match="不支持"):
        DatabaseCapabilities.for_dialect("oracle")


@pytest.mark.parametrize("error_code", [1205, 1213])
def test_mysql_transaction_deadlocks_and_timeouts_are_transient(
    error_code: int,
) -> None:
    error = OperationalError(
        "transaction boundary",
        {},
        PyMySQLOperationalError(error_code, "database detail hidden"),
    )

    assert database_capabilities.is_transient_database_transaction_error(error)


def test_mysql_business_uniqueness_conflict_is_not_a_transient_retry() -> None:
    error = OperationalError(
        "transaction boundary",
        {},
        PyMySQLOperationalError(1062, "database detail hidden"),
    )

    assert not database_capabilities.is_transient_database_transaction_error(
        error
    )


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    ("value", "expected"),
    [
        ("8.4.0", (8, 4, 0)),
        ("8.4.11-commercial", (8, 4, 11)),
    ],
)
def test_parse_mysql_version_accepts_84_patch_versions(
    value: str,
    expected: tuple[int, int, int],
) -> None:
    assert database_capabilities.parse_mysql_version(value) == expected


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    "value",
    [
        "5.7.44",
        "8.0.41",
        "9.6.0",
        "10.11.8-MariaDB",
        "not-a-version",
    ],
)
def test_parse_mysql_version_rejects_non_84_servers(value: str) -> None:
    with pytest.raises(DatabaseDialectError, match="MySQL 8.4"):
        database_capabilities.parse_mysql_version(value)


def test_mysql_runtime_contract_reports_safe_health_facts() -> None:
    connection = _MySQLConnectionStub(_valid_mysql_runtime_responses())

    health = database_capabilities.assert_mysql_runtime_contract(
        connection  # type: ignore[arg-type]
    )

    assert health == database_capabilities.DatabaseHealth(
        dialect="mysql",
        server_version="8.4.11",
        read_only=False,
        claim_strategy="for_update_skip_locked",
    )


def test_mysql_serving_probe_has_bounded_database_round_trips() -> None:
    connection = _MySQLConnectionStub(_valid_mysql_runtime_responses())
    database_capabilities.database_health(connection)  # type: ignore[arg-type]
    assert len(connection.statements) <= 2, "readiness must not pay thirteen network round trips"


def test_mysql_runtime_contract_accepts_oracle_homebrew_distribution() -> None:
    responses = _valid_mysql_runtime_responses()
    responses["SELECT @@version_comment"] = "Homebrew"

    health = database_capabilities.assert_mysql_runtime_contract(
        _MySQLConnectionStub(responses)  # type: ignore[arg-type]
    )

    assert health.server_version == "8.4.11"


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    "comment",
    ["MariaDB Server", "Percona Server", "unknown-compatible-server"],
)
def test_mysql_runtime_contract_rejects_non_oracle_product_comment(
    comment: str,
) -> None:
    responses = _valid_mysql_runtime_responses()
    responses["SELECT @@version_comment"] = comment
    connection = _MySQLConnectionStub(responses)

    with pytest.raises(DatabaseDialectError, match="Oracle MySQL 8.4"):
        database_capabilities.assert_mysql_runtime_contract(  # type: ignore[arg-type]
            connection
        )


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    ("statement", "value", "message"),
    [
        ("SELECT VERSION()", "8.0.41", "MySQL 8.4"),
        ("SELECT @@global.read_only", 1, "只读"),
        ("SELECT @@session.default_storage_engine", "MyISAM", "InnoDB"),
        (
            "SELECT @@session.transaction_isolation",
            "REPEATABLE-READ",
            "READ COMMITTED",
        ),
        ("SELECT @@session.time_zone", "SYSTEM", "UTC"),
        ("SELECT @@character_set_connection", "latin1", "utf8mb4"),
        ("SELECT @@character_set_client", "latin1", "utf8mb4"),
        ("SELECT @@character_set_results", "latin1", "utf8mb4"),
        ("SELECT @@collation_database", "utf8mb4_0900_ai_ci", "区分大小写"),
        (
            "SELECT @@session.sql_mode",
            "STRICT_TRANS_TABLES",
            "SQL mode",
        ),
        (
            "SELECT COUNT(*) FROM information_schema.TABLES "
            "WHERE TABLE_SCHEMA = DATABASE() AND TABLE_TYPE = 'BASE TABLE' "
            "AND ENGINE <> 'InnoDB'",
            1,
            "InnoDB",
        ),
    ],
)
def test_mysql_runtime_contract_fails_closed_on_unsafe_server_or_session(
    statement: str,
    value: object,
    message: str,
) -> None:
    responses = _valid_mysql_runtime_responses()
    responses[statement] = value

    with pytest.raises(DatabaseDialectError, match=message):
        database_capabilities.assert_mysql_runtime_contract(  # type: ignore[arg-type]
            _MySQLConnectionStub(responses)
        )


def test_mysql_probe_without_selected_schema_keeps_server_and_session_checks() -> None:
    responses = _valid_mysql_runtime_responses()
    responses["SELECT DATABASE()"] = None
    responses["SELECT @@collation_database"] = "utf8mb4_0900_ai_ci"
    connection = _MySQLConnectionStub(responses)
    assert database_capabilities.database_health(connection).read_only is False  # type: ignore[arg-type]
    assert len(connection.statements) == 1
    responses["SELECT @@global.read_only"] = 1
    with pytest.raises(DatabaseDialectError, match="只读"):
        database_capabilities.database_health(_MySQLConnectionStub(responses))  # type: ignore[arg-type]


def test_configure_mysql_dbapi_connection_preserves_modes_and_sets_session() -> None:
    connection = _RecordingDbapiConnection()

    configure_mysql_dbapi_connection(connection)

    assert connection.statements == [
        "SET SESSION time_zone = '+00:00'",
        "SET SESSION TRANSACTION ISOLATION LEVEL READ COMMITTED",
        "SET NAMES utf8mb4 COLLATE utf8mb4_0900_as_cs",
        (
            "SET SESSION sql_mode = CONCAT_WS(',', @@SESSION.sql_mode, "
            "'STRICT_TRANS_TABLES', 'ERROR_FOR_DIVISION_BY_ZERO', "
            "'NO_ENGINE_SUBSTITUTION')"
        ),
        "CLOSE",
    ]


def test_sqlite_engine_options_preserve_lock_timeout() -> None:
    assert engine_options(
        "sqlite+pysqlite:////tmp/app.db",
        pool_size=2,
        max_overflow=0,
    ) == {
        "connect_args": {"check_same_thread": False, "timeout": 10},
        "pool_pre_ping": True,
    }

        pool_size=3,
        max_overflow=1,
    ) == {

        "pool_recycle": 1800,

    ) == {
        "pool_pre_ping": True,
        "pool_recycle": 900,
        "pool_reset_on_return": "rollback",
        "pool_size": 2,

    }


def test_dispose_inherited_database_pool_keeps_parent_connections_open(
    monkeypatch,
) -> None:
    calls: list[dict[str, bool]] = []
    monkeypatch.setattr(
        database_module.engine,
        "dispose",
        lambda **kwargs: calls.append(kwargs),
    )

    database_module.dispose_inherited_database_pool()

    assert calls == [{"close": False}]


def test_engine_options_reject_unknown_database_dialect() -> None:
    with pytest.raises(DatabaseDialectError, match="不支持"):
        engine_options("oracle+cx_oracle://local/labels")


def test_database_health_reports_sqlite_version_and_claim_strategy() -> None:
    engine = create_engine("sqlite://")
    try:
        with engine.connect() as connection:
            health = database_capabilities.database_health(connection)
    finally:
        engine.dispose()

    assert health.dialect == "sqlite"
    assert health.server_version.count(".") >= 1
    assert health.read_only is False
    assert health.claim_strategy == "begin_immediate"

===== FILE backend/tests/test_dead_letter_api.py =====
"""失败待处理（死信）的人工处理入口。

2026-09-17 老大：运维中心此前只显示「死信 N 条」这个数字，没有列表也没有处理按钮，
运营找不到地方处理。补齐 API：运行时调度条目沿用原来的「只能放弃」（背后可能挂着已经
计过费的调用，重排等于再花一次钱），下游投递新增放弃与重放（投递按业务幂等键 + 修订
幂等，重放不会产生第二条下游记录）。
"""
from __future__ import annotations

from datetime import datetime, timedelta, timezone
from types import SimpleNamespace

import pytest
from fastapi import FastAPI
from fastapi.testclient import TestClient
from sqlalchemy import StaticPool, create_engine, select
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session, sessionmaker

from app.database import get_db
from app.models import (
    Base,
    ConnectorContractVersion,
    DownstreamPublishLedger,
    ProductionRun,
    ProductionStepAttempt,
    RuntimeAuditEvent,
    RuntimeDispatchItem,
    RuntimeOutboxEvent,
)
from app.operational_metrics import build_operational_metrics_router

NOW = datetime(2026, 9, 17, 8, 0, tzinfo=timezone.utc)


@pytest.fixture
def sessions() -> sessionmaker[Session]:
    engine = create_engine("sqlite://", connect_args={"check_same_thread": False}, poolclass=StaticPool)
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    return sessionmaker(bind=engine, expire_on_commit=False)


@pytest.fixture
def client(sessions: sessionmaker[Session], tmp_path) -> TestClient:
    settings = SimpleNamespace(
        worker_cluster_expected_count=1, worker_process_count=1, data_dir=tmp_path,
        backup_status_file=tmp_path / "backup.json", restore_status_file=tmp_path / "restore.json",
        operations_metrics_token="token",
    )

    def require_admin() -> object:
        return SimpleNamespace(username="老大", is_admin=True)

    def db_override():
        with sessions() as db:
            yield db

    app = FastAPI()
    app.include_router(build_operational_metrics_router(require_admin, settings_provider=lambda: settings, now=lambda: NOW))
    app.dependency_overrides[get_db] = db_override
    with TestClient(app) as running:
        yield running


def _dispatch(db: Session, item_id: int, *, queue_class: str = "production_batch") -> None:
    db.add(RuntimeDispatchItem(id=item_id, step_attempt_id=item_id, queue_class=queue_class,
        status="dead_lettered", attempt_count=3, last_error_code="model_invocation_outcome_unknown",
        available_at=NOW - timedelta(hours=1), dead_lettered_at=NOW - timedelta(minutes=item_id)))


def _contract(db: Session) -> int:
    row = db.scalar(select(ConnectorContractVersion).where(ConnectorContractVersion.id == 1))
    if row is None:
        db.add(ConnectorContractVersion(id=1, contract_key="factory.prod.downstream", version=1, direction="downstream",
            category_key="model_3d_su", logical_locator="factory-submit", adapter_key="factory_submit",
            schema_fingerprint="f" * 64, owner="平台", contract_hash="c" * 64))
        db.flush()
    return 1


def _outbox(db: Session, event_id: int, *, status: str = "dead_lettered") -> None:
    contract_version_id = _contract(db)
    db.add(RuntimeOutboxEvent(id=event_id, event_key=f"event-{event_id}", target_contract_key="factory.prod.downstream",
        target_contract_version_id=contract_version_id, business_idempotency_key=f"business-{event_id}",
        mechanism_version_id=1, payload_blob_id=1, payload_hash="a" * 64, available_at=NOW, status=status,
        attempt_count=2, revision=1,
        last_error_code="factory_submit_http_404", dead_lettered_at=NOW if status == "dead_lettered" else None))
    db.add(DownstreamPublishLedger(outbox_event_id=event_id, target_contract_key="factory.prod.downstream",
        business_idempotency_key=f"business-{event_id}", mechanism_version_id=1, delivery_status="dead_lettered"))


def test_the_list_shows_both_kinds_so_operators_can_find_them(client, sessions) -> None:
    with sessions() as db:
        _dispatch(db, 11)
        _outbox(db, 21)
        db.commit()
    body = client.get("/api/operations/dead-letters").json()
    # 旧形状（运行时调度）保持不变：发布闸门与 verify 脚本按它读
    assert body["total"] == 1 and body["queues"][0]["queue_class"] == "production_batch"
    assert body["queues"][0]["items"][0]["id"] == 11
    assert body["outbox"]["total"] == 1
    entry = body["outbox"]["items"][0]
    assert entry["id"] == 21 and entry["target_contract_key"] == "factory.prod.downstream"
    assert entry["business_idempotency_key"] == "business-21" and entry["last_error_code"] == "factory_submit_http_404"
    assert entry["dead_lettered_at"].endswith("+08:00")


def test_recovered_material_old_failure_is_evidence_not_pending_work(client, sessions):
    from app.runtime_capacity import _dispatch_counts
    with sessions() as db:
        run=ProductionRun(run_key='recovered',idempotency_key='recovered',source_type='content_ingress_event',
            workflow_definition_id=1,workflow_version_id=1,snapshot_json='{}',snapshot_hash='a'*64,
            requested_by='test',owner='test',status='succeeded')
        db.add(run);db.flush()
        attempt=ProductionStepAttempt(id=99,run_id=run.id,step_key='prompt_b',step_type='model_call',sequence=1,
            script_version_id=1,idempotency_key='old-failed-attempt',input_manifest_json='{}',input_hash='b'*64,status='failed')
        db.add(attempt);_dispatch(db,99);db.commit()
        assert sum(q['dead_lettered'] for q in _dispatch_counts(db).values())==0
        assert db.get(RuntimeDispatchItem,99).last_error_code=='model_invocation_outcome_unknown'
        assert db.get(ProductionStepAttempt,99).status=='failed'
    assert client.get('/api/operations/dead-letters').json()['total']==0


def test_a_stock_regression_failure_is_neither_listed_nor_described(client, sessions) -> None:
    """存量回归失败不是人工处理对象：重跑一轮新 run 就够了。

    它既不能进「失败待处理」（会被当成 3dsu 增量故障），也不该在卡片上单独交代——
    2026-09-20 老大：648 个运行号平铺在正文里，把真正要说的话挤出了屏幕。
    """
    with sessions() as db:
        run = ProductionRun(
            run_key="reg-1", idempotency_key="reg-1", source_type="baseline_regression_item",
            workflow_definition_id=1, workflow_version_id=1, snapshot_json="{}",
            snapshot_hash="a" * 64, queue_class="production_batch", requested_by="test", owner="test",
        )
        db.add(run); db.flush()
        attempt = ProductionStepAttempt(
            id=31, run_id=run.id, step_key="model", step_type="model_call", sequence=0,
            script_version_id=1, idempotency_key="reg-step-1", input_manifest_json="{}", input_hash="a" * 64,
        )
        db.add(attempt); db.flush()
        _dispatch(db, 31)
        _outbox(db, 41)
        db.commit()

    body = client.get("/api/operations/dead-letters").json()
    assert body["total"] == 0 and body["queues"] == [], "回归死信不能出现在人工处理列表里"
    assert "regression" not in body, "也不该把回归清单吐给前端——没有对应的人工动作"
    assert body["outbox"]["total"] == 1, "下游投递照常要能处理"


def test_an_empty_platform_answers_with_zero_not_an_error(client) -> None:
    body = client.get("/api/operations/dead-letters").json()
    assert body["total"] == 0 and body["queues"] == [] and body["outbox"] == {"total": 0, "items": []}


def test_discarding_a_downstream_dead_letter_writes_an_audit_row(client, sessions) -> None:
    with sessions() as db:
        _outbox(db, 31)
        db.commit()
    response = client.post("/api/operations/dead-letters/outbox/discard",
                           json={"event_ids": [31], "reason": "上游 404 已确认无效，抛弃"})
    assert response.status_code == 200
    body = response.json()
    assert body["ok_count"] == 1 and body["failed_count"] == 0 and body["results"][0]["status"] == "canceled"
    with sessions() as db:
        assert db.get(RuntimeOutboxEvent, 31).status == "canceled"
        assert db.scalars(select(RuntimeAuditEvent)).all()


def test_replaying_a_downstream_dead_letter_requeues_it(client, sessions) -> None:
    with sessions() as db:
        _outbox(db, 41)
        db.commit()
    body = client.post("/api/operations/dead-letters/outbox/replay",
                       json={"event_ids": [41], "reason": "下游已修复，重放"}).json()
    assert body["ok_count"] == 1
    with sessions() as db:
        assert db.get(RuntimeOutboxEvent, 41).status != "dead_lettered"


def test_每条各自成败_一条坏的不拖垮其余(client, sessions) -> None:
    with sessions() as db:
        _outbox(db, 51)
        _outbox(db, 52, status="delivered")  # 不是死信，处理不了
        db.commit()
    body = client.post("/api/operations/dead-letters/outbox/discard",
                       json={"event_ids": [51, 52, 999], "reason": "混合批"}).json()
    assert body["ok_count"] == 1 and body["failed_count"] == 2
    failures = {item["id"]: item for item in body["results"] if not item["ok"]}
    assert set(failures) == {52, 999}
    with sessions() as db:
        assert db.get(RuntimeOutboxEvent, 51).status == "canceled"
        assert db.get(RuntimeOutboxEvent, 52).status == "delivered"


def test_a_batch_that_cannot_touch_anything_is_refused_not_silently_ok(client, sessions) -> None:
    response = client.post("/api/operations/dead-letters/outbox/replay",
                           json={"event_ids": [777], "reason": "不存在"})
    assert response.status_code == 422
    assert response.json()["detail"]["results"][0]["ok"] is False


def test_a_downstream_action_without_a_reason_is_rejected(client) -> None:
    assert client.post("/api/operations/dead-letters/outbox/discard",
                       json={"event_ids": [1], "reason": "  "}).status_code in (400, 422)

===== FILE backend/tests/test_dead_letter_discard.py =====
"""人工放弃 outbox 死信：转 canceled、台账 rejected、写审计、不可重复。

2026-09-16：12 条 2026-09-15 的 factory 下游死信（任务 258–347）老大裁决直接抛弃。
此前只有重放路径，这些死信让 verify-operations 每轮都判 operations_critical。
"""
from datetime import datetime, timezone

import pytest
from sqlalchemy import create_engine, select
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from app.connector_delivery import ConnectorDeliveryError, discard_dead_letter_delivery
from app.models import DownstreamPublishLedger, RuntimeAuditEvent, RuntimeOutboxEvent

NOW = datetime(2026, 9, 16, 12, 0, tzinfo=timezone.utc)


@pytest.fixture
def db():
    engine = create_engine("sqlite://")
    for model in (RuntimeOutboxEvent, DownstreamPublishLedger, RuntimeAuditEvent):
        model.__table__.create(engine)
    session = Session(engine)
    try:
        yield session
    finally:
        session.close(); engine.dispose()


def _event(db, event_id, status="dead_lettered"):
    row = RuntimeOutboxEvent(id=event_id, event_key=f"event-{event_id}", target_contract_key="factory.prod.downstream",
        target_contract_version_id=None, business_idempotency_key=f"business-{event_id}", mechanism_version_id=1,
        payload_blob_id=1, payload_hash="a" * 64, available_at=NOW, status=status, attempt_count=1,
        last_error_code="factory_submit_http_404", dead_lettered_at=NOW if status == "dead_lettered" else None)
    db.add(row)
    db.add(DownstreamPublishLedger(outbox_event_id=event_id, target_contract_key="factory.prod.downstream",
        business_idempotency_key=f"business-{event_id}", mechanism_version_id=1, delivery_status="dead_lettered"))
    db.commit()
    return row


def test_discard_cancels_the_event_rejects_the_ledger_and_audits(db):
    _event(db, 258)
    event = discard_dead_letter_delivery(db, 258, actor="老大", reason="任务 258–347 直接抛弃")
    db.commit()
    assert event.status == "canceled" and event.last_error_code == "operator_discarded"
    assert event.attempt_count == 1, "历史不改写"
    ledger = db.scalar(select(DownstreamPublishLedger).where(DownstreamPublishLedger.outbox_event_id == 258))
    assert (ledger.delivery_status, ledger.rejection_code) == ("rejected", "operator_discarded")
    audit = db.scalar(select(RuntimeAuditEvent).where(RuntimeAuditEvent.entity_key == "258"))
    assert audit is not None and audit.action == "dead_letter_discarded" and audit.actor == "老大"
    assert "previous_error_code" in audit.details_json and "factory_submit_http_404" in audit.details_json


def test_only_dead_letters_can_be_discarded_and_only_once(db):
    _event(db, 259)
    _event(db, 260, status="queued")
    with pytest.raises(ConnectorDeliveryError) as exc:
        discard_dead_letter_delivery(db, 260, actor="ops", reason="x")
    assert exc.value.code == "downstream_discard_state_invalid"
    discard_dead_letter_delivery(db, 259, actor="ops", reason="x"); db.commit()
    with pytest.raises(ConnectorDeliveryError):
        discard_dead_letter_delivery(db, 259, actor="ops", reason="again")
    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        discard_dead_letter_delivery(db, 259, actor="", reason="")


def test_dead_letter_count_seen_by_operations_drops_to_zero(db):
    for event_id in range(261, 267):
        _event(db, event_id)
    for event_id in range(261, 267):
        discard_dead_letter_delivery(db, event_id, actor="ops", reason="批量放弃")
    db.commit()
    assert db.scalar(select(RuntimeOutboxEvent.id).where(RuntimeOutboxEvent.status == "dead_lettered")) is None

===== FILE backend/tests/test_deduction_rule_validator.py =====
from __future__ import annotations

from copy import deepcopy
import json

import pytest
from sqlalchemy import create_engine
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from app.category_evaluation_contract import BonusRule, DeductionRule
from app.dimension_composition import (
    DimensionCompositionError,
    dimension_rule_mode,
    validate_subcategory_dimensions,
)
from app.inspiration_category_seed import build_inspiration_subcategory_dimensions
from app.inspiration_category_seed import build_inspiration_v3_contract
from app.database import Base
from app.migrations.upgrade_v3_to_rule_deduction import upgrade_v3_to_rule_deduction
from app.models import CategoryEvaluationV3Config


def test_deduction_rule_requires_chinese_description_and_positive_deduction() -> None:
    rule = DeductionRule.model_validate(
        {
            "rule_id": "composition_minor",
            "description": "构图存在局部失衡",
            "deduction": 12.5,
            "tags": ["构图", "占位"],
        }
    )
    assert rule.deduction == 12.5
    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        DeductionRule.model_validate(
            {"rule_id": "bad", "description": "english only", "deduction": 10}
        )
    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        DeductionRule.model_validate(
            {"rule_id": "bad", "description": "扣分", "deduction": 0}
        )


def test_bonus_rule_requires_positive_value_and_chinese_description() -> None:
    rule = BonusRule.model_validate(
        {
            "rule_id": "composition_clear",
            "description": "构图层级清晰完整",
            "bonus": 8,
            "tags": ["构图"],
        }
    )
    assert rule.bonus == 8
    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        BonusRule.model_validate(
            {"rule_id": "bad", "description": "english only", "bonus": 5}
        )
    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        BonusRule.model_validate(
            {"rule_id": "bad", "description": "构图清晰", "bonus": 0}
        )


def test_dimension_contract_rejects_duplicate_rule_ids() -> None:
    config = deepcopy(build_inspiration_subcategory_dimensions()["class_one"])
    dimension = config["common_group"]["schema_definition"]["dimensions"][0]
    dimension["deduction_rules"].append(deepcopy(dimension["deduction_rules"][0]))
    with pytest.raises(DimensionCompositionError) as excinfo:
        validate_subcategory_dimensions(config)
    assert "duplicate" in excinfo.value.code


def test_rule_ids_are_unique_across_deduction_and_bonus() -> None:
    config = deepcopy(build_inspiration_subcategory_dimensions()["class_one"])
    dimension = config["common_group"]["schema_definition"]["dimensions"][0]
    dimension["dimension_score_cap"] = 100
    dimension["bonus_rules"] = [
        {
            "rule_id": dimension["deduction_rules"][0]["rule_id"],
            "description": "重复标识的正向规则",
            "bonus": 5,
            "tags": ["构图"],
        }
    ]
    with pytest.raises(DimensionCompositionError) as excinfo:
        validate_subcategory_dimensions(config)
    assert excinfo.value.code.endswith("rule_id_duplicate")


def test_old_contract_mode_is_not_changed_by_read_defaults() -> None:
    old = {
        "key": "legacy_grade",
        "weight": 1.0,
        "grade_points": {"1": 0, "2": 25, "3": 50, "4": 75, "5": 100},
    }
    assert dimension_rule_mode(old) == "grade_fallback"
    assert "bonus_rules" not in old
    assert "dimension_score_cap" not in old


@pytest.mark.parametrize("value", [-1, 100.1, float("nan"), float("inf"), True])
def test_dimension_deduction_cap_rejects_invalid_values(value: object) -> None:
    config = deepcopy(build_inspiration_subcategory_dimensions()["class_one"])
    dimension = config["common_group"]["schema_definition"]["dimensions"][0]
    dimension["dimension_score_cap"] = 100
    dimension["bonus_rules"] = []
    dimension["dimension_deduction_cap"] = value
    with pytest.raises(DimensionCompositionError) as excinfo:
        validate_subcategory_dimensions(config)
    assert excinfo.value.code.endswith("dimension_deduction_cap_invalid")


def test_dimension_deduction_cap_is_optional_for_legacy_and_defaults_to_full_range() -> None:
    config = deepcopy(build_inspiration_subcategory_dimensions()["class_one"])
    dimension = config["common_group"]["schema_definition"]["dimensions"][0]
    dimension.pop("dimension_deduction_cap", None)
    validate_subcategory_dimensions(config)


def test_grade_points_remain_a_deprecated_compatible_fallback() -> None:
    config = deepcopy(build_inspiration_subcategory_dimensions()["class_one"])
    for dimension in config["common_group"]["schema_definition"]["dimensions"]:
        dimension.pop("deduction_rules")
    validate_subcategory_dimensions(config)


def test_data_upgrade_is_idempotent_and_preserves_lifecycle_state() -> None:
    engine = create_engine("sqlite://")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    contract = build_inspiration_v3_contract()
    contract["common_modifiers"]["media_type_penalty"].pop("enabled")
    dimensions = build_inspiration_subcategory_dimensions()
    for config in dimensions.values():
        for dimension in config["common_group"]["schema_definition"]["dimensions"]:
            dimension.pop("deduction_rules")
    with Session(engine) as db:
        db.add(
            CategoryEvaluationV3Config(
                category_key="inspiration_image",
                display_name="灵感图",
                status="active",
                contract_json=json.dumps(contract, ensure_ascii=False),
                classification_map_json='{"legacy":true}',
                subcategory_dimensions_json=json.dumps(dimensions, ensure_ascii=False),
                revision=7,
                contract_hash="old",
            )
        )
        db.commit()
        first = upgrade_v3_to_rule_deduction(db)
        db.commit()
        row = db.query(CategoryEvaluationV3Config).one()
        assert first["changed_keys"] == ["inspiration_image"]
        assert row.status == "active"
        assert row.revision == 8
        assert row.media_penalty_enabled is False
        assert json.loads(row.dimension_deduction_rules_json)
        second = upgrade_v3_to_rule_deduction(db)
        db.commit()
        assert second["changed_count"] == 0
        assert row.revision == 8
    engine.dispose()

===== FILE backend/tests/test_definition_pinned_batch_route.py =====
"""fallback_policy is what switches the batch transport machine on, so keep it.

It reads like "fall back to online when batch fails", and on 2026-09-15 it was
stripped from a definition pinned to batch on exactly that reading, to stop the
frozen worst case having to cover three batch attempts plus an online call.

That broke batch execution. workflow_fixture_executor decides whether to run the
transport state machine - submission waits, pacing, the 24 hour deadline - by
testing whether recovery.policy(runtime) is None, and that function returns
route['fallback_policy']. Without it a batch call degrades to one plain HTTP
request against a queue endpoint: both production calls hung for the full 600
second client timeout and settled as model_invocation_outcome_unknown, which may
never be replayed.

"Batch and never online" is a different mechanism, batch_experiment.
batch_only_experiment, an operator action recorded per run. The quote inflation
the removal was chasing belongs on the reservation side, which prices the
expected prompt instead of the model's context ceiling.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import pytest

from app.doubao_endpoints import select_inference_route


class _Endpoint:
    def __init__(self, endpoint_id, *, model="doubao-seed-2-0-lite-260428"):
        self.endpoint_id = endpoint_id
        self.status = "active"
        self.verification_status = "verified"
        self.foundation_model = model
        self.model_version = ""
        self.revision = 1


class _Definition:
    def __init__(self, mode):
        self.doubao_inference_mode = mode
        self.doubao_endpoint_row_id = 1
        self.doubao_batch_endpoint_row_id = 2


class _Db:
    def __init__(self, mode):
        self._definition = _Definition(mode)
        self._rows = {
            1: _Endpoint("ep-20260907175346-dpbwf"),
            2: _Endpoint("ep-bi-20260909110052-lbmtc"),
        }

    def get(self, model, key):
        if key is None:
            return None
        return self._definition if getattr(model, "__name__", "") == "WorkflowDefinition" \
            else self._rows.get(key)


@pytest.fixture
def fallback_on(monkeypatch):
    monkeypatch.setenv("LABEL_SYSTEM_BATCH_ONLINE_FALLBACK", "true")


def _route(monkeypatch, definition_mode, **kwargs):
    import app.doubao_endpoints as module

    db = _Db(definition_mode)
    monkeypatch.setattr(module, "WorkflowDefinition", type("WorkflowDefinition", (), {}))
    monkeypatch.setattr(
        module, "DoubaoInferenceEndpoint", type("DoubaoInferenceEndpoint", (), {})
    )
    return select_inference_route(db, 16, **kwargs)


def test_a_definition_pinned_to_batch_keeps_the_transport_policy(monkeypatch, fallback_on):
    # Stripping this is what left two production calls hanging to the timeout.
    route = _route(monkeypatch, "batch", offline=True)
    assert route["mode"] == "batch"
    assert route["endpoint_id"] == "ep-bi-20260909110052-lbmtc"
    assert "fallback_policy" in route


def test_auto_resolved_batch_also_keeps_it(monkeypatch, fallback_on):
    route = _route(monkeypatch, "auto", offline=True)
    assert route["mode"] == "batch"
    assert "fallback_policy" in route


def test_the_policy_carries_what_the_transport_machine_needs(monkeypatch, fallback_on):
    policy = _route(monkeypatch, "batch", offline=True)["fallback_policy"]
    assert policy["version"] == "batch-online-fallback-v1"
    assert policy["batch_max_attempts"] == 3
    assert policy["deadline_seconds"] == 86400
    assert policy["online_route"]["mode"] == "online"
    assert policy["online_route"]["endpoint_id"] == "ep-20260907175346-dpbwf"


def test_only_the_caller_flag_removes_it(monkeypatch, fallback_on):
    # The documented way to resolve batch without a fallback, for callers that
    # know they are not going through the transport machine.
    assert "fallback_policy" in _route(monkeypatch, "auto", mode="batch")
    assert "fallback_policy" not in _route(
        monkeypatch, "auto", mode="batch", allow_online_fallback=False
    )


def test_the_switch_being_off_removes_it_for_every_caller(monkeypatch):
    monkeypatch.setenv("LABEL_SYSTEM_BATCH_ONLINE_FALLBACK", "false")
    assert "fallback_policy" not in _route(monkeypatch, "batch", offline=True)


def test_an_online_route_never_carries_a_fallback(monkeypatch, fallback_on):
    route = _route(monkeypatch, "online", offline=True)
    assert route["mode"] == "online"
    assert route["endpoint_id"] == "ep-20260907175346-dpbwf"
    assert "fallback_policy" not in route


def test_the_definition_pin_only_applies_to_the_offline_resolution(monkeypatch, fallback_on):
    route = _route(monkeypatch, "batch", mode="online")
    assert route["mode"] == "online"

===== FILE backend/tests/test_deploy_test_script.py =====
from __future__ import annotations

import importlib.util
from pathlib import Path
import subprocess

import pytest


SCRIPT_PATH = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2] / "scripts" / "deploy-test.py"
SPEC = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location("deploy_test_script", SCRIPT_PATH)
assert SPEC is not None and SPEC.loader is not None
deploy_test = importlib.util.module_from_spec(SPEC)
SPEC.loader.exec_module(deploy_test)


def test_select_codeup_remote_prefers_ssh_over_https_origin() -> None:
    assert deploy_test.select_codeup_remote(
        [
            ("origin", deploy_test.CODEUP_URL),
            ("codeup-ssh", deploy_test.CODEUP_SSH_URL),
        ]
    ) == "codeup-ssh"


def test_select_codeup_remote_falls_back_to_valid_origin() -> None:
    assert deploy_test.select_codeup_remote(
        [("origin", deploy_test.CODEUP_URL)]
    ) == "origin"


def test_select_codeup_remote_rejects_unapproved_repositories() -> None:
    with pytest.raises(deploy_test.DeployError, match="Codeup remote"):
        deploy_test.select_codeup_remote(
            [("origin", "https://example.com/not-the-label-system.git")]
        )


def test_prepare_release_fetches_and_bundles_selected_ssh_remote(
    monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch,
    tmp_path: Path,
) -> None:
    calls: list[tuple[str, ...]] = []
    commit = "a" * 40

    def fake_run_capture(args: list[str], cwd: Path) -> str:
        calls.append(tuple(args))
        if args == ["git", "rev-parse", "--show-toplevel"]:
            return str(tmp_path)
        if args == ["git", "remote"]:
            return "origin\ncodeup-ssh"
        if args == ["git", "remote", "get-url", "origin"]:
            return deploy_test.CODEUP_URL
        if args == ["git", "remote", "get-url", "codeup-ssh"]:
            return deploy_test.CODEUP_SSH_URL
        if args == ["git", "rev-parse", "origin/main"]:
            return commit
        return ""

    monkeypatch.setattr(deploy_test, "run_capture", fake_run_capture)
    release = deploy_test.prepare_release(tmp_path)
    try:
        assert (
            "git",
            "fetch",
            "codeup-ssh",
            "refs/heads/main:refs/remotes/origin/main",
        ) in calls
        assert ("git", "rev-parse", "origin/main") in calls
        assert any(
            call[:3] == ("git", "bundle", "create")
            and call[-1] == "origin/main"
            for call in calls
        )
    finally:
        deploy_test.cleanup_release(release)


def test_create_verified_bundle_can_be_fetched_by_server_expected_ref(
    tmp_path: Path,
) -> None:
    source = tmp_path / "source"
    receiver = tmp_path / "receiver"
    bundle = tmp_path / "main.bundle"
    source.mkdir()
    receiver.mkdir()

    subprocess.run(["git", "init", "-q"], cwd=source, check=True)
    subprocess.run(["git", "config", "user.name", "Deploy Test"], cwd=source, check=True)
    subprocess.run(
        ["git", "config", "user.email", "deploy-test@example.invalid"],
        cwd=source,
        check=True,
    )
    (source / "release.txt").write_text("release\n", encoding="utf-8")
    subprocess.run(["git", "add", "release.txt"], cwd=source, check=True)
    subprocess.run(["git", "commit", "-qm", "release"], cwd=source, check=True)
    subprocess.run(
        ["git", "update-ref", "refs/remotes/origin/main", "HEAD"],
        cwd=source,
        check=True,
    )

    deploy_test.create_verified_bundle(source, bundle, "origin/main")

    subprocess.run(["git", "init", "-q"], cwd=receiver, check=True)
    fetched = subprocess.run(
        ["git", "fetch", str(bundle), "refs/remotes/origin/main"],
        cwd=receiver,
        check=False,
        capture_output=True,
        text=True,
    )
    assert fetched.returncode == 0, fetched.stderr
    source_commit = subprocess.run(
        ["git", "rev-parse", "HEAD"],
        cwd=source,
        check=True,
        capture_output=True,
        text=True,
    ).stdout.strip()
    subprocess.run(
        ["git", "cat-file", "-e", f"{source_commit}^{{commit}}"],
        cwd=receiver,
        check=True,
    )

===== FILE backend/tests/test_design_case_complete_synthetic_workflow.py =====
"""Zero-fee full case graph with synthetic score inputs and fake model answers.

These are plumbing checks, not evidence of image/case quality. Only fresh local
test workflow rows are configured; no deployed/frozen production version is used.
"""
import hashlib
import json
import socket

import pytest
from sqlalchemy import create_engine, select
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from app.audit import canonical_json
from app.models import BaselineRegressionRun, ProductionRun, ProductionStepAttempt, WorkflowVersion
from app.workflow_registry import persisted_workflow_manifest


@pytest.mark.parametrize('emit_image_grades', [False, True])
def test_complete_case_graph_projects_case_and_optional_image_grades(tmp_path, monkeypatch, emit_image_grades):
    from tests import test_legacy_workflow_release as release_fixture
    from test_native_workflow_real_regression import (
        _available_test_model_secret,
        test_materialized_design_case_real_graph_calls_two_images_then_case_prompt as exercise_graph,
    )

    # Reuse the existing full native graph driver: real local JPEGs, two fake
    # image responses (82 each), one fake case response (70), actual scheduler,
    # foreach, aggregation, material summary, scoring and result persistence.
    original_workflow = release_fixture._workflow
    captured = {}
    bands = [
        {'level': 'L1', 'min_inclusive': 85.0},
        {'level': 'L2', 'min_inclusive': 72.0},
        {'level': 'L3', 'min_inclusive': 60.0},
        {'level': 'L4', 'min_inclusive': 0.0},
    ]

    def configure_fresh_fixture(db, key):
        workflow = original_workflow(db, key)
        if key == 'design_case.legacy_doubao_review':
            nodes = json.loads(workflow.step_manifest_json)
            aggregate = next(node for node in nodes if node['kind'] == 'aggregate_case')
            aggregate['config']['emit_image_grades'] = emit_image_grades
            if emit_image_grades:
                aggregate['config']['image_grade_bands'] = bands
            workflow.step_manifest_json = canonical_json(nodes)
            workflow.canonical_hash = hashlib.sha256(
                canonical_json(persisted_workflow_manifest(workflow)).encode()).hexdigest()
            captured.update(workflow_id=workflow.id, manifest_hash=workflow.canonical_hash)
        return workflow

    def forbid_network(*_args, **_kwargs):
        pytest.fail('synthetic case workflow must not open network connections')

    monkeypatch.setattr(release_fixture, '_workflow', configure_fresh_fixture)
    monkeypatch.setattr(socket.socket, 'connect', forbid_network)
    monkeypatch.setattr(socket.socket, 'connect_ex', forbid_network)
    _available_test_model_secret.__wrapped__(monkeypatch)
    exercise_graph(tmp_path, monkeypatch)

    # The reused fixture stores only this temporary database. Its driver also
    # verifies exactly two image and one text invocation, all succeeded.
    engine = create_engine(f'sqlite:///{tmp_path / "strict-release.db"}')
    try:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            run = db.scalar(select(ProductionRun).where(
                ProductionRun.workflow_version_id == captured['workflow_id']))
            assert run is not None and run.status == 'succeeded'
            snapshot = json.loads(run.snapshot_json)
            case = snapshot['runtime_context']['input_manifest']['case']
            assert {key: case[key] for key in ('quality_score', 'time_score', 'filter_threshold')} == {
                'quality_score': 10, 'time_score': 5, 'filter_threshold': 60,
            }
            assert len(case['images']) == 2
            assert snapshot['workflow']['canonical_hash'] == captured['manifest_hash']
            assert db.get(WorkflowVersion, captured['workflow_id']).canonical_hash == captured['manifest_hash']
            attempts = list(db.scalars(select(ProductionStepAttempt).where(ProductionStepAttempt.run_id == run.id)))
            assert attempts and all(row.status == 'succeeded' for row in attempts)
            baseline = db.scalar(select(BaselineRegressionRun))
            assert baseline.status == 'completed'
            result = json.loads(baseline.items[0].result_snapshot_json)['normalized_result']
            # Synthetic case score: fake model 70 + supplied quality 10 + time 5.
            assert (result['score'], result['level']) == (85, 'L1')
            assert result['side_effect_executed'] is False
            assert result['deletion_mode'] == 'recommend_only'
            if emit_image_grades:
                assert result['images'] == [
                    {'cloud_id': 'real-image-a', 'level': 'L2'},
                    {'cloud_id': 'real-image-b', 'level': 'L2'},
                ]
                assert result['image_grade_detail']['bands'] == bands
                assert [row['score'] for row in result['image_grade_detail']['images']] == [82, 82]
            else:
                assert 'images' not in result
                assert 'image_grade_detail' not in result
    finally:
        engine.dispose()

===== FILE backend/tests/test_design_case_dimension_import.py =====
import pytest

from app.design_case_dimension_import import (
    DesignCaseDimensionError,
    build_aggregate_truth,
    build_dimension_truth,
    normalize_trait,
    parse_case_directory,
    parse_count_text,
    parse_sample_root,
    summarize,
)

CASE_TEXT = """素材标题：普陀山码头裸崖建筑
分类：建筑设计-公共建筑
图片总张数：3张
所有表现图的分数：40分有1张,75分有1张,85分有1张
所有图片的类型：实景照片有2张,其他有1张
"""


def _make_case(tmp_path, tier="02中等", name="中等_675556", images=None, text=CASE_TEXT):
    case_dir = tmp_path / tier / name
    case_dir.mkdir(parents=True)
    (case_dir / f"{name.split('_')[1]}.txt").write_text(text, encoding="utf-8")
    for filename in images or ["好_101.png", "中等_102.png", "极差_103.jpg"]:
        (case_dir / filename).write_bytes(b"")
    return case_dir


def test_parse_case_directory_reads_grades_from_names(tmp_path):
    case = parse_case_directory(_make_case(tmp_path), grade="中等")
    assert case.cloud_id == "675556"
    assert case.level == "L2"
    assert case.case_name == "普陀山码头裸崖建筑"
    assert {image.cloud_id: image.level for image in case.images} == {
        "101": "L1",
        "102": "L2",
        "103": "L4",
    }


def test_case_directory_grade_must_match_its_tier(tmp_path):
    with pytest.raises(DesignCaseDimensionError) as excinfo:
        parse_case_directory(_make_case(tmp_path, name="好_675556"), grade="中等")
    assert excinfo.value.code == "design_case_dir_grade_mismatch"


def test_filtered_grade_is_rejected_on_a_single_image(tmp_path):
    """图片维度没有过滤档——文件名正则本身就不接受「过滤_」。"""

    case_dir = _make_case(tmp_path, images=["好_101.png"])
    (case_dir / "过滤_104.png").write_bytes(b"")
    with pytest.raises(DesignCaseDimensionError) as excinfo:
        parse_case_directory(case_dir, grade="中等")
    assert excinfo.value.code == "design_case_image_name_invalid"


def test_duplicate_image_id_inside_one_case_is_rejected(tmp_path):
    case_dir = _make_case(tmp_path, images=["好_101.png", "中差_101.jpg"])
    with pytest.raises(DesignCaseDimensionError) as excinfo:
        parse_case_directory(case_dir, grade="中等")
    assert excinfo.value.code == "design_case_image_duplicate"


def test_case_without_images_or_text_is_rejected(tmp_path):
    empty = tmp_path / "02中等" / "中等_1"
    empty.mkdir(parents=True)
    (empty / "1.txt").write_text(CASE_TEXT, encoding="utf-8")
    with pytest.raises(DesignCaseDimensionError) as excinfo:
        parse_case_directory(empty, grade="中等")
    assert excinfo.value.code == "design_case_no_images"

    no_text = tmp_path / "02中等" / "中等_2"
    no_text.mkdir(parents=True)
    (no_text / "好_1.png").write_bytes(b"")
    with pytest.raises(DesignCaseDimensionError) as excinfo:
        parse_case_directory(no_text, grade="中等")
    assert excinfo.value.code == "design_case_text_missing"


def test_unknown_tier_directory_stops_the_import(tmp_path):
    _make_case(tmp_path)
    (tmp_path / "06新档").mkdir()
    with pytest.raises(DesignCaseDimensionError) as excinfo:
        parse_sample_root(tmp_path)
    assert excinfo.value.code == "design_case_tier_dir_unknown"


def test_same_case_twice_is_a_delivery_accident(tmp_path):
    """一张图只会出现在一个案例内，案例自然也只能出现一次。"""

    _make_case(tmp_path, tier="02中等", name="中等_900")
    _make_case(tmp_path, tier="03中差", name="中差_900")
    with pytest.raises(DesignCaseDimensionError) as excinfo:
        parse_sample_root(tmp_path)
    assert excinfo.value.code == "design_case_duplicate"


def test_parse_sample_root_covers_all_five_tiers(tmp_path):
    for tier, name in (
        ("01好", "好_1"),
        ("02中等", "中等_2"),
        ("03中差", "中差_3"),
        ("04极差", "极差_4"),
        ("05过滤", "过滤_5"),
    ):
        _make_case(tmp_path, tier=tier, name=name)
    cases = parse_sample_root(tmp_path)
    assert [case.level for case in cases] == ["L1", "L2", "L3", "L4", "L5"]


def test_trait_normalization_and_dropping():
    assert normalize_trait("其他") == "其它"
    assert normalize_trait("实景照片") == "实景照片"
    assert normalize_trait("未返回trait") is None
    assert normalize_trait("  ") is None


def test_parse_count_text_shares_the_material_dimension_wording():
    assert parse_count_text("实景照片有15张,技术图有1张") == {
        "实景照片": 15,
        "技术图": 1,
    }
    assert parse_count_text("") == {}


def test_multi_label_images_are_counted_per_label():
    """``分析图,3D数字效果图有1张`` 是一张图带两个类型标签。

    既有口径把省略计数的片段当 1 张，拆成两个标签各记一次，与平台
    aggregate_case 对多值特征逐个计数一致；因此各标签之和可以超过图片总数。
    真实交付 中差_895798 就是这样：30 张图，标签合计 32。
    """

    assert parse_count_text("分析图有7张,分析图,3D数字效果图有1张") == {
        "分析图": 8,
        "3D数字效果图": 1,
    }


def test_build_dimension_truth_sorts_by_cloud_id(tmp_path):
    case = parse_case_directory(_make_case(tmp_path), grade="中等")
    truth = build_dimension_truth(case)
    assert truth["level"] == "L2"
    assert [image["cloud_id"] for image in truth["images"]] == ["101", "102", "103"]


def test_build_aggregate_truth_passes_operator_wording_through(tmp_path):
    """三行原话原样带过去，计数由 baseline_truth 统一反解，避免口径分叉。"""

    case = parse_case_directory(_make_case(tmp_path), grade="中等")
    truth = build_aggregate_truth(case)
    assert truth["image_total_text"] == "3张"
    assert truth["score_distribution_text"] == "40分有1张,75分有1张,85分有1张"
    assert truth["feature_texts"]["trait"] == "实景照片有2张,其它有1张"


def test_summarize_counts_dropped_traits(tmp_path):
    _make_case(
        tmp_path,
        text=CASE_TEXT.replace("其他有1张", "未返回trait有1张"),
    )
    summary = summarize(parse_sample_root(tmp_path))
    assert summary["cases"] == 1
    assert summary["images"] == 3
    assert summary["case_levels"] == {"L2": 1}
    assert summary["image_levels"] == {"L1": 1, "L2": 1, "L4": 1}
    assert summary["traits"] == {"实景照片": 2}
    assert summary["dropped_trait_images"] == 1

===== FILE backend/tests/test_design_case_dimension_persistence.py =====
"""Synthetic import regressions: immutable identity, readable assets, and NAS boundary."""
from copy import deepcopy
from pathlib import Path
from types import SimpleNamespace
import json

import pytest
from sqlalchemy import create_engine, select, func
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from app.models import Base, Asset, DesignCaseSample, MaterialPackage, MaterialPackageItem
from app.design_case_dimension_service import build_import_plan, import_dimension_samples
from app.design_case_dimension_import import DesignCaseDimensionError
from app.design_case_sample_service import case_content_hash, DesignCaseSampleServiceError
from app.nas_storage import NasStorageError, resolve_asset_path
from test_design_case_dimension_pipeline import CASE_TEXT, _png_bytes


@pytest.fixture
def delivery(tmp_path):
    root = tmp_path / 'nas'
    directory = root / 'delivery' / '02中等' / '中等_1'
    directory.mkdir(parents=True)
    (directory / '1.txt').write_text(CASE_TEXT, encoding='utf8')
    (directory / '好_101.png').write_bytes(_png_bytes())
    return root, directory


@pytest.fixture
def db():
    engine = create_engine('sqlite:///:memory:')
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    with Session(engine) as session:
        yield session
    engine.dispose()


def load_plan(delivery):
    return build_import_plan('nas://maps/delivery', nas_root=delivery[0])[0]


def save(db, plans):
    return import_dimension_samples(db, plans=plans, category_key='design_case', package_name='synthetic', actor='test')


def test_imported_asset_is_readable_by_runtime(db, delivery):
    result = save(db, load_plan(delivery))
    sample = db.get(DesignCaseSample, result['items'][0]['sample_id'])
    image = json.loads(sample.image_grades_json)['images'][0]
    asset = db.get(Asset, image['asset_id'])
    assert resolve_asset_path(asset, SimpleNamespace(nas_maps_root=delivery[0])) == delivery[1] / '好_101.png'


@pytest.mark.parametrize('change', ['case_level', 'image_level', 'image_content', 'image_identity', 'case_text'])
def test_changed_delivery_creates_immutable_new_sample(db, delivery, change):
    plans = load_plan(delivery)
    original = save(db, plans)
    before = db.get(DesignCaseSample, original['items'][0]['sample_id']).image_grades_json
    changed = deepcopy(plans)
    if change == 'case_level': changed[0].level = 'L3'
    if change == 'image_level': changed[0].images[0].level = 'L4'
    if change == 'image_content':
        changed[0].images[0].sha256 = 'b' * 64
        changed[0].images[0].uri = 'nas://maps/delivery-v2/好_101.png'
    if change == 'image_identity': changed[0].images[0].cloud_id = '202'
    if change == 'case_text': changed[0].case['case_text'] = 'changed text'
    result = save(db, changed)
    assert result['summary']['created_count'] == 1
    assert result['items'][0]['asset_id'] != original['items'][0]['asset_id']
    assert db.get(DesignCaseSample, original['items'][0]['sample_id']).image_grades_json == before


def test_identical_delivery_retry_reuses_package_without_empty_package(db, delivery):
    plans = load_plan(delivery)
    first = save(db, plans)
    retry = save(db, deepcopy(plans))
    assert retry['package']['id'] == first['package']['id']
    assert retry['summary']['created_count'] == 0
    assert db.scalar(select(func.count()).select_from(MaterialPackage)) == 1
    assert db.scalar(select(func.count()).select_from(DesignCaseSample)) == 1


def test_legacy_material_sample_does_not_prevent_image_import(db, delivery):
    plans = load_plan(delivery)
    plan = plans[0]
    asset = Asset(original_name='legacy',stored_name='legacy',mime_type='application/json',size_bytes=1,sha256='c'*64,category_key='design_case')
    db.add(asset); db.flush()
    legacy = DesignCaseSample(asset_id=asset.id,category_key='design_case',case_json=json.dumps(plan.case),image_summary_json=json.dumps(plan.image_summary),content_hash=case_content_hash(plan.case,plan.image_summary))
    db.add(legacy); db.flush()
    result = save(db, plans)
    assert result['summary']['created_count'] == 1
    assert legacy.image_grades_json == '{}'


def test_partial_repeat_package_contains_old_and_new_samples(db, delivery):
    plans = load_plan(delivery)
    first = save(db, plans)
    new = deepcopy(plans[0]); new.case['cloud_id'] = new.cloud_id = '2'
    second = save(db, [plans[0], new])
    members = set(db.scalars(select(MaterialPackageItem.asset_id).where(MaterialPackageItem.package_id == second['package']['id'])))
    assert first['items'][0]['asset_id'] in members
    assert len(members) == 2


def test_changed_content_at_existing_uri_rejected_before_any_writes(db, delivery):
    plans = load_plan(delivery)
    save(db, plans)
    db.commit()
    before = {model: db.scalar(select(func.count()).select_from(model))
              for model in (Asset, DesignCaseSample, MaterialPackage, MaterialPackageItem)}
    fresh = deepcopy(plans[0])
    fresh.case['cloud_id'] = fresh.cloud_id = '2'
    fresh.images[0].uri = 'nas://maps/new/好_202.png'
    fresh.images[0].sha256 = 'c' * 64
    changed = deepcopy(plans[0])
    changed.images[0].sha256 = 'b' * 64
    with pytest.raises(DesignCaseSampleServiceError) as error:
        save(db, [fresh, changed])
    assert error.value.status_code == 422
    assert error.value.code == 'design_case_image_content_changed'
    assert '新路径' in str(error.value)
    assert not db.new and not db.dirty
    assert before == {model: db.scalar(select(func.count()).select_from(model)) for model in before}


@pytest.mark.parametrize('target', ['txt', 'case', 'tier'])
def test_delivery_rejects_symlinks_before_reading_any_text(delivery, tmp_path, target):
    root, directory = delivery
    source = {'txt': directory / '1.txt', 'case': directory, 'tier': directory.parent}[target]
    outside = tmp_path / ('outside.txt' if target == 'txt' else 'outside')
    source.rename(outside)
    source.symlink_to(outside, target_is_directory=target != 'txt')
    with pytest.raises((DesignCaseDimensionError, NasStorageError)) as error:
        load_plan(delivery)
    assert 'SYMLINK' in error.value.code.upper()


def test_same_case_shared_content_preserves_both_delivery_identities(delivery):
    (delivery[1] / '中等_102.png').write_bytes(_png_bytes())
    plans = load_plan(delivery)
    assert {image.cloud_id: image.level for image in plans[0].images} == {'101': 'L1', '102': 'L2'}
    assert len({image.sha256 for image in plans[0].images}) == 1

===== FILE backend/tests/test_design_case_dimension_pipeline.py =====
"""图片维度整条链路：NAS 交付目录 → 素材 → 基准集真值 → 终态输出 → 指标。"""

from pathlib import Path

import pytest

from app.baseline_truth import (
    DESIGN_CASE_DIMENSION_TRUTH_CONTRACT,
    normalize_baseline_workflow_input,
    normalize_design_case_dimension_expected_result,
)
from app.design_case_dimension_service import build_import_plan
from app.native_baseline_regression import compute_image_grade_metrics
from app.workflow_result_finalizer import _attach_image_grades

CASE_TEXT = """素材标题：普陀山码头裸崖建筑
分类：建筑设计-公共建筑
标签：码头,现代
风格：其它
案例正文：正文内容
城市：中国
图片总张数：3张
所有表现图的分数：40分有1张,75分有1张,88分有1张
所有图片的类型：实景照片有2张,其他有1张
"""


def _png_bytes() -> bytes:
    """1×1 PNG——inspect_nas_file 会真的用 PIL 打开校验。"""

    import base64

    return base64.b64decode(
        "iVBORw0KGgoAAAANSUhEUgAAAAEAAAABCAYAAAAfFcSJAAAADUlEQVR42mP8z8BQDwAEhQGAhKmMIQAAAABJRU5ErkJggg=="
    )


# 交付目录总是共享内的子目录；nas://maps/ 指向共享根本身会被拒绝。
DELIVERY = "案例-普通样本/测试"
DELIVERY_URI = f"nas://maps/{DELIVERY}"


@pytest.fixture()
def nas_root(tmp_path: Path) -> Path:
    case_dir = tmp_path / DELIVERY / "02中等" / "中等_675556"
    case_dir.mkdir(parents=True)
    (case_dir / "675556.txt").write_text(CASE_TEXT, encoding="utf-8")
    # 三张图内容各不相同，否则会被内容哈希判成同一张。
    for index, name in enumerate(("好_101.png", "中差_102.png", "极差_103.png")):
        (case_dir / name).write_bytes(_png_bytes() + bytes([index]))
    return tmp_path


def test_import_plan_freezes_grades_and_asset_identity(nas_root: Path):
    plans, stats = build_import_plan(DELIVERY_URI, nas_root=nas_root)
    assert stats["cases"] == 1 and stats["images"] == 3
    assert stats["image_levels"] == {"L1": 1, "L3": 1, "L4": 1}
    plan = plans[0]
    assert plan.cloud_id == "675556"
    assert plan.level == "L2"
    assert {image.cloud_id: image.level for image in plan.images} == {
        "101": "L1",
        "102": "L3",
        "103": "L4",
    }
    # 每张图都拿到了真实的内容哈希，基准集才能冻结资产身份。
    assert all(len(image.sha256) == 64 for image in plan.images)
    assert len({image.sha256 for image in plan.images}) == 3


def test_same_image_in_two_cases_stops_the_import(tmp_path: Path):
    """一张图只会出现在一个案例内；重复交付会冻结出两份互相矛盾的真值。"""

    from app.design_case_dimension_import import DesignCaseDimensionError

    shared = _png_bytes()
    for tier, name in (("02中等", "中等_1"), ("03中差", "中差_2")):
        case_dir = tmp_path / DELIVERY / tier / name
        case_dir.mkdir(parents=True)
        (case_dir / f"{name.split('_')[1]}.txt").write_text(CASE_TEXT, encoding="utf-8")
        (case_dir / "好_101.png").write_bytes(shared)
    with pytest.raises(DesignCaseDimensionError) as excinfo:
        build_import_plan(DELIVERY_URI, nas_root=tmp_path)
    assert excinfo.value.code == "design_case_image_cross_case_duplicate"


def _frozen_truth(plan) -> tuple[dict, dict]:
    workflow_input = normalize_baseline_workflow_input(
        {
            "case": {
                "cloud_id": plan.cloud_id,
                "case_name": plan.case_name,
                "images": [
                    {
                        "cloud_id": image.cloud_id,
                        "asset_id": index + 1,
                        "asset_sha256": image.sha256,
                    }
                    for index, image in enumerate(plan.images)
                ],
            }
        },
        truth_contract_key=DESIGN_CASE_DIMENSION_TRUTH_CONTRACT,
    )
    truth = normalize_design_case_dimension_expected_result(
        {
            "level": plan.level,
            "images": [
                {"cloud_id": image.cloud_id, "level": image.level}
                for image in plan.images
            ],
        },
        workflow_input=workflow_input,
    )
    return workflow_input, truth


def _manifest_and_outputs(scores: dict[str, float], *, bands=None):
    config = {"foreach_node_id": "foreach_image", "emit_image_grades": True}
    if bands is not None:
        config["image_grade_bands"] = bands
    manifest = {
        "nodes": [
            {
                "node_instance_id": "aggregate",
                "kind": "aggregate_case",
                "config": config,
            }
        ]
    }
    outputs = {
        "aggregate": {
            "schema_version": "workflow-node-result-v1",
            "status": "succeeded",
            "node_instance_id": "aggregate",
            "output": {
                "schema_version": "workflow-case-aggregate-v1",
                "results": [
                    {"item_key": cloud_id, "status": "succeeded", "score": score}
                    for cloud_id, score in scores.items()
                ],
            },
        }
    }
    return manifest, outputs


def test_end_to_end_operator_bands_reproduce_the_measured_gap(nas_root: Path):
    """按运营口径（优 85 / 中 72 / 中差 60）投影，与人工真值逐图比对。

    这一案例的三张图人工标的是 L1/L3/L4，模型分 88/75/40 经运营阈值得到
    L1/L2/L4——中间那张判高了一档。交付样本整体的同一现象（误差全是「预测
    高一档」）就是这样被度量出来的。
    """

    plans, _ = build_import_plan(DELIVERY_URI, nas_root=nas_root)
    _, truth = _frozen_truth(plans[0])
    manifest, outputs = _manifest_and_outputs({"101": 88, "102": 75, "103": 40})
    predicted = _attach_image_grades(manifest, outputs, {})

    # 预测侧的 images 必须与真值同形状，指标层的集合比对才能逐张对上。
    assert predicted["images"] == [
        {"cloud_id": "101", "level": "L1"},
        {"cloud_id": "102", "level": "L2"},
        {"cloud_id": "103", "level": "L4"},
    ]
    # 分数与用过的阈值另放，纠偏时能回看「这张图为什么被判成这档」。
    assert predicted["image_grade_detail"]["bands"][0] == {
        "level": "L1",
        "min_inclusive": 85.0,
    }

    metrics = compute_image_grade_metrics([(truth["images"], predicted["images"])])
    assert metrics["compared"] == 3
    assert metrics["correct"] == 2
    assert metrics["over_graded"] == 1
    assert metrics["under_graded"] == 0
    assert metrics["confusion"]["L3"]["L2"] == 1


def test_bands_are_frozen_per_version(nas_root: Path):
    """换一套阈值就是换一个工作流版本，旧回归的口径不会被追溯改写。"""

    plans, _ = build_import_plan(DELIVERY_URI, nas_root=nas_root)
    _, truth = _frozen_truth(plans[0])
    manifest, outputs = _manifest_and_outputs(
        {"101": 88, "102": 75, "103": 40},
        bands=[
            {"level": "L1", "min_inclusive": 92},
            {"level": "L2", "min_inclusive": 81},
            {"level": "L3", "min_inclusive": 67},
            {"level": "L4", "min_inclusive": 0},
        ],
    )
    predicted = _attach_image_grades(manifest, outputs, {})
    assert [image["level"] for image in predicted["images"]] == ["L2", "L3", "L4"]
    metrics = compute_image_grade_metrics([(truth["images"], predicted["images"])])
    # 样本反推出来的阈值下，这三张图里判对了两张（L3、L4），L1 被判低成 L2。
    assert metrics["correct"] == 2
    assert metrics["under_graded"] == 1


def test_unscored_images_are_reported_not_counted_as_wrong(nas_root: Path):
    """非表现图没有分数，不猜档位；它们要出现在 missing 里，不能拉低准确率。"""

    plans, _ = build_import_plan(DELIVERY_URI, nas_root=nas_root)
    _, truth = _frozen_truth(plans[0])
    manifest, outputs = _manifest_and_outputs({"101": 88, "102": 75})
