    COMBINED_WEIGHTS,
    ENGINE_VERSION,
    GRADE_POINTS,
    WEIGHTS,
    _level_for_score,
)


def _engine(tmp_path, name: str):
    engine = create_engine(
        f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / name}",
        connect_args={"check_same_thread": False},
    )

    @event.listens_for(engine, "connect")
    def _enable_foreign_keys(dbapi_connection, _connection_record) -> None:
        cursor = dbapi_connection.cursor()
        cursor.execute("PRAGMA foreign_keys=ON")
        cursor.close()

    return engine


@pytest.fixture
def engine(tmp_path):
    result = _engine(tmp_path, "dimension-schema.db")
    Base.metadata.create_all(bind=result)
    with result.begin() as connection:
        run_migrations(connection)
    try:
        yield result
    finally:
        result.dispose()


def _definition_by_version(db: Session, version: str) -> dict:
    schema = db.scalar(
        select(DimensionSchema).where(
            DimensionSchema.schema_key == SPACE_SCHEMA_KEY,
            DimensionSchema.version == version,
        )
    )
    assert schema is not None
    return json.loads(schema.definition_json)


def _weights(definition: dict) -> dict[str, float]:
    return {
        dimension["key"]: dimension["weight"]
        for dimension in definition["dimensions"]
    }


def _grade_points(definition: dict) -> dict[int, float]:
    return {
        int(grade): points
        for grade, points in definition["aggregation"]["grade_points"].items()
    }


def _managed_definition(definition: dict) -> dict:
    managed = json.loads(json.dumps(definition))
    common_anchors = managed.get("common_grade_anchors", {})
    for dimension in managed["dimensions"]:
        dimension["description"] = (
            dimension.get("description")
            or f"评审{dimension['label']}的完成度、证据与代表性"
        )
        dimension["anchors"] = {
            str(level): dimension.get("anchors", {}).get(str(level))
            or common_anchors.get(str(level))
            or f"{dimension['label']} {level} 级表现"
            for level in range(1, 6)
        }
    return managed


def test_materialized_space_revisions_equal_live_scoring_constants(
    engine,
) -> None:
    with Session(engine) as db:
        schemas = db.scalars(
            select(DimensionSchema)
            .where(DimensionSchema.schema_key == SPACE_SCHEMA_KEY)
            .order_by(DimensionSchema.version)
        ).all()
        assert len(schemas) == 2
        assert {schema.version for schema in schemas} == {
            HISTORICAL_DEFAULT_VERSION,
            ACTIVE_V13_VERSION,
        }
        assert all(schema.status == "published" for schema in schemas)
        assert all(
            schema.canonical_hash
            == canonical_hash(json.loads(schema.definition_json))
            for schema in schemas
        )

        historical = _definition_by_version(db, HISTORICAL_DEFAULT_VERSION)
        active = _definition_by_version(db, ACTIVE_V13_VERSION)

    assert _weights(historical) == WEIGHTS
    assert _weights(active) == COMBINED_WEIGHTS
    assert _grade_points(historical) == GRADE_POINTS
    assert _grade_points(active) == GRADE_POINTS
    assert historical["aggregation"]["engine_version"] == ENGINE_VERSION
    assert active["aggregation"]["engine_version"] == ENGINE_VERSION

    thresholds = active["aggregation"]["level_thresholds"]
    assert thresholds == historical["aggregation"]["level_thresholds"]
    for threshold_key, lower_level, level_at_threshold in (
        ("L2", "L1", "L2"),
        ("L3", "L2", "L3"),
        ("L4", "L3", "L4"),
        ("L5", "L4", "L5"),
    ):
        threshold = thresholds[threshold_key]
        assert _level_for_score(threshold - 0.01) == lower_level
        assert _level_for_score(threshold) == level_at_threshold

    for definition in (historical, active):
        risk_review = definition["risk_review"]
        assert risk_review["version"] == RISK_REVIEW_VERSION
        assert risk_review["dimension_keys"] == list(DIMENSION_KEYS)
        assert risk_review["quality_rank"] == QUALITY_RANK
        assert risk_review["cap_rank"] == CAP_RANK
        assert definition["output_contract"]["dimension_output_keys"] == list(
            DIMENSION_KEYS
        )


def test_dimension_schema_draft_crud_and_constraints(engine) -> None:
    definition = {"format_version": "test-v1", "dimensions": []}
    with Session(engine, expire_on_commit=False) as db:
        schema = DimensionSchema(
            schema_key="test_schema",
            version="0.1.0",
            schema_type="extension",
            family_key="space",
            display_name="测试草稿",
            status="draft",
            definition_json=canonical_json(definition),
            canonical_hash=canonical_hash(definition),
            created_by="test",
        )
        db.add(schema)
        db.commit()
        schema_id = schema.id

        schema.display_name = "测试候选"
        schema.status = "candidate"
        db.commit()
        assert db.get(DimensionSchema, schema_id).display_name == "测试候选"

        db.delete(schema)
        db.commit()
        assert db.get(DimensionSchema, schema_id) is None

        duplicate_definition = {"format_version": "test-v2", "dimensions": []}
        db.add_all(
            [
                DimensionSchema(
                    schema_key="duplicate",
                    version="1.0.0",
                    schema_type="extension",
                    family_key="space",
                    display_name="重复一",
                    definition_json=canonical_json(duplicate_definition),
                    canonical_hash=canonical_hash(duplicate_definition),
                    created_by="test",
                ),
                DimensionSchema(
                    schema_key="duplicate",
                    version="1.0.0",
                    schema_type="extension",
                    family_key="space",
                    display_name="重复二",
                    definition_json=canonical_json(
                        {"format_version": "test-v3", "dimensions": []}
                    ),
                    canonical_hash=canonical_hash(
                        {"format_version": "test-v3", "dimensions": []}
                    ),
                    created_by="test",
                ),
            ]
        )
        with pytest.raises(IntegrityError):
            db.commit()


def test_published_schema_rejects_orm_and_database_update_delete(engine) -> None:
    with Session(engine, expire_on_commit=False) as db:
        schema = db.scalar(
            select(DimensionSchema).where(
                DimensionSchema.schema_key == SPACE_SCHEMA_KEY,
                DimensionSchema.version == ACTIVE_V13_VERSION,
            )
        )
        assert schema is not None
        schema_id = schema.id

        schema.display_name = "禁止覆盖"
        with pytest.raises(DimensionSchemaImmutableError):
            db.commit()
        db.rollback()

        schema = db.get(DimensionSchema, schema_id)
        assert schema is not None
        db.delete(schema)
        with pytest.raises(DimensionSchemaImmutableError):
            db.commit()
        db.rollback()

    with pytest.raises(IntegrityError, match="immutable"):
        with engine.begin() as connection:
            connection.exec_driver_sql(
                "UPDATE dimension_schemas SET display_name='raw-update' "
                "WHERE id=?",
                (schema_id,),
            )
    with pytest.raises(IntegrityError, match="cannot be deleted"):
        with engine.begin() as connection:
            connection.exec_driver_sql(
                "DELETE FROM dimension_schemas WHERE id=?",
                (schema_id,),
            )


def test_migration_26_foreign_keys_and_true_insert_smoke(tmp_path) -> None:
    engine = _engine(tmp_path, "dimension-schema-migration.db")
    old_tables = [
        table
        for table in Base.metadata.tables.values()
        if table.name != "dimension_schemas"
    ]
    Base.metadata.create_all(bind=engine, tables=old_tables)
    try:
        with engine.begin() as connection:
            connection.exec_driver_sql("""
                CREATE TABLE schema_migrations (
                    version INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,
                    name VARCHAR(200) NOT NULL,
                    applied_at DATETIME NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
                )
            """)
            for migration in MIGRATIONS[:25]:
                connection.exec_driver_sql(
                    "INSERT INTO schema_migrations (version, name) VALUES (?, ?)",
                    (migration.version, migration.name),
                )
            run_migrations(connection)

            assert connection.exec_driver_sql(
                "SELECT max(version) FROM schema_migrations"
            ).scalar_one() == len(MIGRATIONS)
            assert connection.exec_driver_sql(
                "PRAGMA foreign_key_check"
            ).all() == []
            foreign_keys = {
                (row[3], row[2])
                for row in connection.exec_driver_sql(
                    "PRAGMA foreign_key_list(dimension_schemas)"
                )
            }
            assert foreign_keys >= {
                ("parent_schema_id", "dimension_schemas"),
                ("core_schema_id", "dimension_schemas"),
                ("source_optimization_run_id", "prompt_optimization_runs"),
            }

            parent_definition = {"format_version": "smoke-parent-v1"}
            child_definition = {"format_version": "smoke-child-v1"}
            connection.exec_driver_sql(
                """
                INSERT INTO dimension_schemas (
                    id, schema_key, version, schema_type, family_key,
                    display_name, status, definition_json, canonical_hash,
                    created_by
                ) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
                """,
                (
                    9001,
                    "smoke_core",
                    "1.0.0",
                    "core",
                    "common",
                    "冒烟核心",
                    "draft",
                    canonical_json(parent_definition),
                    canonical_hash(parent_definition),
                    "test",
                ),
            )
            connection.exec_driver_sql(
                """
                INSERT INTO dimension_schemas (
                    id, schema_key, version, schema_type, family_key,
                    display_name, status, parent_schema_id, core_schema_id,
                    definition_json, canonical_hash, created_by
                ) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
                """,
                (
                    9002,
                    "smoke_family",
                    "1.0.0",
                    "family_pack",
                    "space",
                    "冒烟族包",
                    "draft",
                    9001,
                    9001,
                    canonical_json(child_definition),
                    canonical_hash(child_definition),
                    "test",
                ),
            )
            assert connection.exec_driver_sql(
                "SELECT parent_schema_id, core_schema_id "
                "FROM dimension_schemas WHERE id=9002"
            ).one() == (9001, 9001)
            assert connection.exec_driver_sql(
                "PRAGMA foreign_key_check"
            ).all() == []
    finally:
        engine.dispose()

    finally:
        app.dependency_overrides.clear()

    finally:
        app.dependency_overrides.clear()

===== FILE backend/tests/test_dimension_strategy_binding.py =====
from __future__ import annotations

import hashlib
import json

import pytest
from sqlalchemy import create_engine, select
from sqlalchemy.exc import IntegrityError
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from app.database import Base
from app.dimension_schema_registry import (
    ACTIVE_V13_VERSION,
    HISTORICAL_DEFAULT_VERSION,
    SPACE_SCHEMA_KEY,
)
from app.migrations import run_migrations
from app.models import (
    Asset,
    EvaluationJob,
    EvaluationResult,
    ModelConfig,
    PromptVersion,
    SamplingPolicy,
    StrategyBundle,
    StrategySnapshotRequiredError,
)
from app.strategy_bundle import (
    LEGACY_STRATEGY_SCHEMA_VERSION,
    STRATEGY_SCHEMA_VERSION,
    build_evaluation_strategy_snapshot,
    build_strategy_snapshot,
    get_or_create_bundle,
)


def _canonical_json(value: object) -> str:
    return json.dumps(
        value,
        ensure_ascii=False,
        sort_keys=True,
        separators=(",", ":"),
    )


def _sha256(value: object) -> str:
    return hashlib.sha256(
        _canonical_json(value).encode("utf-8")
    ).hexdigest()


@pytest.fixture
def db(tmp_path):
    engine = create_engine(f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / 'dimension-binding.db'}")
    Base.metadata.create_all(bind=engine)
    with engine.begin() as connection:
        run_migrations(connection)
    session = Session(engine, expire_on_commit=False)
    try:
        yield session
    finally:
        session.close()
        engine.dispose()


def _seed_inputs(
    db: Session,
) -> tuple[ModelConfig, PromptVersion, PromptVersion, SamplingPolicy]:
    model = ModelConfig(
        name="dimension-test-model",
        provider="test",
        base_url="https://example.test/v1",
        api_path="/chat/completions",
        model_id="dimension-test-model",
        encrypted_api_key="encrypted",
        temperature=0.1,
        max_tokens=4096,
        timeout_seconds=120,
        max_retries=1,
        max_concurrency=2,
        structured_output=True,
        high_risk_review_enabled=False,
    )
    prompt_a = PromptVersion(
        stage="A",
        name="dimension-a",
        version="dimension-a-v1",
        system_prompt="system A",
        user_prompt="user A",
        rubric_version="space-rubric-v1.4",
        status="published",
    )
    prompt_b = PromptVersion(
        stage="B",
        name="dimension-b",
        version="dimension-b-v1",
        system_prompt="system B",
        user_prompt="user B",
        rubric_version="space-rubric-v1.4",
        status="published",
    )
    policy = SamplingPolicy(
        id=1,
        revision=3,
        sample_rate=10,
        low_confidence_threshold=0.7,
        medium_confidence_threshold=0.9,
        cold_start_required_count=5,
        high_level_required_from=4,
    )
    db.add_all([model, prompt_a, prompt_b, policy])
    db.commit()
    return model, prompt_a, prompt_b, policy


def _v2_bundle(
    db: Session,
) -> tuple[StrategyBundle, PromptVersion, PromptVersion, SamplingPolicy]:
    model, prompt_a, prompt_b, policy = _seed_inputs(db)
    bundle = get_or_create_bundle(
        db=db,
        model_config=model,
        prompt_a=prompt_a,
        prompt_b=prompt_b,
        rubric_version=prompt_b.rubric_version,
        engine_version="engine-v2.5.0",
        risk_review_version=None,
        sampling_policy=policy,
    )
    db.commit()
    return bundle, prompt_a, prompt_b, policy


def _asset_job(db: Session) -> tuple[Asset, EvaluationJob]:
    asset = Asset(
        original_name="dimension.jpg",
        stored_name="dimension.jpg",
        mime_type="image/jpeg",
        size_bytes=100,
        sha256="d" * 64,
    )
    db.add(asset)
    db.flush()
    job = EvaluationJob(asset_id=asset.id, status="completed")
    db.add(job)
    db.commit()
    return asset, job


def _result(
    *,
    asset: Asset,
    job: EvaluationJob,
    bundle: StrategyBundle,
    snapshot: str,
) -> EvaluationResult:
    return EvaluationResult(
        asset_id=asset.id,
        job_id=job.id,
        strategy_bundle_id=bundle.id,
        strategy_snapshot_json=snapshot,
        precheck_json="{}",
        aesthetic_json="{}",
        scoring_json="{}",
        raw_response_a="{}",
        raw_response_b="{}",
        model_id=bundle.model_id,
        prompt_a_version=bundle.prompt_a_version,
        prompt_b_version=bundle.prompt_b_version,
        risk_review_version=bundle.risk_review_version,
        rubric_version=bundle.rubric_version,
        engine_version=bundle.engine_version,
    )


def test_v2_bundle_freezes_schema_set_and_result_resolves_active_revision(
    db: Session,
) -> None:
    bundle, prompt_a, prompt_b, policy = _v2_bundle(db)
    assert bundle.strategy_schema_version == STRATEGY_SCHEMA_VERSION

    base_snapshot = json.loads(
        build_strategy_snapshot(bundle, prompt_a, prompt_b, policy)
    )
    versions = {
        item["version"]
        for item in base_snapshot["dimension_schema_set"]["schemas"]
    }
    assert versions == {
        HISTORICAL_DEFAULT_VERSION,
        ACTIVE_V13_VERSION,
    }
    assert "resolved_dimension_schema_id" not in base_snapshot

    result_snapshot = build_evaluation_strategy_snapshot(
        db=db,
        bundle=bundle,
        prompt_a=prompt_a,
        prompt_b=prompt_b,
        sampling_policy=policy,
        aesthetic={"scoring_profile": "space_aesthetic_v1.3"},
    )
    payload = json.loads(result_snapshot)
    assert payload["resolved_dimension_schema_key"] == SPACE_SCHEMA_KEY
    assert (
        payload["resolved_dimension_schema_version"]
        == ACTIVE_V13_VERSION
    )
    assert (
        payload["route_decision_snapshot"]["reason"]
        == "scoring_profile_matches_active_v1_3"
    )

    asset, job = _asset_job(db)
    db.add(
        _result(
            asset=asset,
            job=job,
            bundle=bundle,
            snapshot=result_snapshot,
        )
    )
    db.commit()


def test_same_bundle_resolves_historical_revision_without_identity_drift(
    db: Session,
) -> None:
    bundle, prompt_a, prompt_b, policy = _v2_bundle(db)
    active = json.loads(
        build_evaluation_strategy_snapshot(
            db=db,
            bundle=bundle,
            prompt_a=prompt_a,
            prompt_b=prompt_b,
            sampling_policy=policy,
            aesthetic={"scoring_profile": "space_aesthetic_v1.3"},
        )
    )
    historical = json.loads(
        build_evaluation_strategy_snapshot(
            db=db,
            bundle=bundle,
            prompt_a=prompt_a,
            prompt_b=prompt_b,
            sampling_policy=policy,
            aesthetic={"scoring_profile": "legacy"},
        )
    )
    assert active["canonical_hash"] == historical["canonical_hash"]
    assert (
        historical["resolved_dimension_schema_version"]
        == HISTORICAL_DEFAULT_VERSION
    )
    assert (
        active["resolved_snapshot_hash"]
        != historical["resolved_snapshot_hash"]
    )


def test_explicit_dimension_contract_records_override_reason(
    db: Session,
) -> None:
    bundle, prompt_a, prompt_b, policy = _v2_bundle(db)

    payload = json.loads(
        build_evaluation_strategy_snapshot(
            db=db,
            bundle=bundle,
            prompt_a=prompt_a,
            prompt_b=prompt_b,
            sampling_policy=policy,
            aesthetic={"scoring_profile": "legacy"},
            dimension_schema_key=SPACE_SCHEMA_KEY,
            dimension_schema_version=ACTIVE_V13_VERSION,
        )
    )

    assert payload["resolved_dimension_schema_version"] == ACTIVE_V13_VERSION
    assert payload["route_decision_snapshot"]["input"] == {
        "scoring_profile": "legacy"
    }
    assert (
        payload["route_decision_snapshot"]["reason"]
        == "explicit_category_contract_override"
    )


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    "tamper",
    ("schema_hash", "definition", "prompt_hash", "resolution_hash"),
)
def test_v2_result_rejects_tampered_dimension_identity(
    db: Session,
    tamper: str,
) -> None:
    bundle, prompt_a, prompt_b, policy = _v2_bundle(db)
    payload = json.loads(
        build_evaluation_strategy_snapshot(
            db=db,
            bundle=bundle,
            prompt_a=prompt_a,
            prompt_b=prompt_b,
            sampling_policy=policy,
            aesthetic={"scoring_profile": "space_aesthetic_v1.3"},
        )
    )
    if tamper == "schema_hash":
        payload["resolved_dimension_schema_hash"] = "f" * 64
    elif tamper == "definition":
        payload["resolved_dimensions_snapshot"]["dimensions"][0][
            "label"
        ] = "伪造维度"
    elif tamper == "prompt_hash":
        payload["resolved_prompt_b_hash"] = "f" * 64
    else:
        payload["resolved_snapshot_hash"] = "f" * 64

    asset, job = _asset_job(db)
    db.add(
        _result(
            asset=asset,
            job=job,
            bundle=bundle,
            snapshot=_canonical_json(payload),
        )
    )
    with pytest.raises(StrategySnapshotRequiredError):
        db.commit()
    db.rollback()


def test_database_trigger_rejects_nonexistent_resolved_schema(
    db: Session,
) -> None:
    bundle, prompt_a, prompt_b, policy = _v2_bundle(db)
    payload = json.loads(
        build_evaluation_strategy_snapshot(
            db=db,
            bundle=bundle,
            prompt_a=prompt_a,
            prompt_b=prompt_b,
            sampling_policy=policy,
            aesthetic={"scoring_profile": "space_aesthetic_v1.3"},
        )
    )
    payload["resolved_dimension_schema_id"] = 999999
    payload["route_decision_snapshot"]["dimension_schema_id"] = 999999
    resolution = {
        key: payload[key]
        for key in (
            "resolved_dimension_schema_id",
            "resolved_dimension_schema_key",
            "resolved_dimension_schema_version",
            "resolved_dimension_schema_hash",
            "resolved_dimensions_snapshot",
            "resolved_prompt_b_hash",
            "route_decision_snapshot",
        )
    }
    payload["resolved_snapshot_hash"] = _sha256(resolution)
    asset, job = _asset_job(db)

    with pytest.raises(IntegrityError, match="strategy binding"):
        db.connection().exec_driver_sql(
            """
            INSERT INTO evaluation_results (
                asset_id, job_id, strategy_bundle_id,
                strategy_snapshot_json, precheck_json, aesthetic_json,
                scoring_json, raw_response_a, raw_response_b,
                needs_review, review_stage, review_revision,
                model_id, prompt_a_version, prompt_b_version,
                rubric_version, engine_version, created_at, updated_at
            ) VALUES (
                ?, ?, ?, ?, '{}', '{}', '{}', '{}', '{}',
                0, 'initial', 0, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?,
                CURRENT_TIMESTAMP, CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
            )
            """,
            (
                asset.id,
                job.id,
                bundle.id,
                _canonical_json(payload),
                bundle.model_id,
                bundle.prompt_a_version,
                bundle.prompt_b_version,
                bundle.rubric_version,
                bundle.engine_version,
            ),
        )
    db.rollback()


def test_legacy_v1_bundle_and_result_remain_readable_and_insertable(
    db: Session,
) -> None:
    model, prompt_a, prompt_b, policy = _seed_inputs(db)
    model_snapshot = {
        "name": model.name,
        "provider": model.provider,
        "base_url": model.base_url,
        "api_path": model.api_path,
        "model_id": model.model_id,
        "temperature": model.temperature,
        "max_tokens": model.max_tokens,
        "timeout_seconds": model.timeout_seconds,
        "max_retries": model.max_retries,
        "max_concurrency": model.max_concurrency,
        "structured_output": model.structured_output,
        "high_risk_review_enabled": model.high_risk_review_enabled,
    }
    prompt = lambda item: {
        "id": item.id,
        "stage": item.stage,
        "version": item.version,
        "name": item.name,
        "rubric_version": item.rubric_version,
        "system_prompt": item.system_prompt,
        "user_prompt": item.user_prompt,
    }
    definition = {
        "schema_version": LEGACY_STRATEGY_SCHEMA_VERSION,
        "model_id": model.model_id,
        "model_config": model_snapshot,
        "prompt_a": prompt(prompt_a),
        "prompt_b": prompt(prompt_b),
        "rubric_version": prompt_b.rubric_version,
        "engine_version": "engine-v2.5.0",
        "sampling_policy": {
            "id": policy.id,
            "revision": policy.revision,
            "sample_rate": policy.sample_rate,
            "low_confidence_threshold": policy.low_confidence_threshold,
            "medium_confidence_threshold": policy.medium_confidence_threshold,
            "cold_start_required_count": policy.cold_start_required_count,
            "high_level_required_from": policy.high_level_required_from,
        },
        "risk_review_version": None,
        "agent_plan_version": "controlled-agent-plan-v1",
    }
    bundle = StrategyBundle(
        canonical_hash=_sha256(definition),
        strategy_schema_version=LEGACY_STRATEGY_SCHEMA_VERSION,
        model_id=model.model_id,
        model_config_snapshot=_canonical_json(model_snapshot),
        prompt_a_version=prompt_a.version,
        prompt_b_version=prompt_b.version,
        rubric_version=prompt_b.rubric_version,
        engine_version="engine-v2.5.0",
        sampling_policy_revision=policy.revision,
        risk_review_version=None,
        agent_plan_version="controlled-agent-plan-v1",
    )
    db.add(bundle)
    db.flush()
    snapshot = _canonical_json(
        {
            "bundle_id": bundle.id,
            "canonical_hash": bundle.canonical_hash,
            **definition,
        }
    )
    asset, job = _asset_job(db)
    db.add(
        _result(
            asset=asset,
            job=job,
            bundle=bundle,
            snapshot=snapshot,
        )
    )
    db.commit()
    assert db.scalar(
        select(StrategyBundle).where(StrategyBundle.id == bundle.id)
    ) is not None

===== FILE backend/tests/test_docker_secret_storage.py =====
from __future__ import annotations

import stat
import pytest

from app import security


def test_datasource_credential_has_separate_authenticated_namespace(tmp_path,monkeypatch):
    monkeypatch.setenv(security.FILE_AEAD_KEY_ENV,str(tmp_path/'master.key'))
    monkeypatch.setattr(security.sys,'platform','linux')
    reference=security.protect_secret('fixture-only-password',account='datasource-factory-test-gray')
    assert security.unprotect_secret(reference)=='fixture-only-password'
    with pytest.raises(security.SecretStorageError):
        security.unprotect_secret(reference.replace('datasource-factory-test-gray','datasource-other',1))
    with pytest.raises(security.SecretStorageError):
        security.protect_secret('fixture-only-password',account='datasource-../private')


def test_linux_file_aead_round_trip_survives_reload(tmp_path, monkeypatch) -> None:
    key_path = tmp_path / "data" / "secrets" / "master.key"
    monkeypatch.setenv(security.FILE_AEAD_KEY_ENV, str(key_path))
    monkeypatch.setattr(security.sys, "platform", "linux")
    reference = security.protect_secret("test-api-key", account="model-config-9")
    assert reference.startswith("file-aead:v1:model-config-9:")
    assert "test-api-key" not in reference
    assert security.unprotect_secret(reference) == "test-api-key"
    assert stat.S_IMODE(key_path.stat().st_mode) == 0o600


def test_file_aead_binds_ciphertext_to_account(tmp_path, monkeypatch) -> None:
    monkeypatch.setenv(security.FILE_AEAD_KEY_ENV, str(tmp_path / "master.key"))
    monkeypatch.setattr(security.sys, "platform", "linux")
    reference = security.protect_secret("test-api-key", account="model-config-9")
    tampered = reference.replace("model-config-9", "model-config-8", 1)
    try:
        security.unprotect_secret(tampered)
    except security.SecretStorageError as error:
        assert error.reason == "FILE_AEAD_DECRYPT_FAILED"
    else:
        raise AssertionError("tampered account must not decrypt")

"""豆包推理接入点路由、火山账单同步与成本对账（schema 107）。"""
from __future__ import annotations

import json
from datetime import datetime, timedelta, timezone
from types import SimpleNamespace

import pytest
from fastapi import FastAPI
from fastapi.testclient import TestClient
from sqlalchemy import func, select

from app import volcengine_billing as billing
from app.database import get_db
from app.doubao import DoubaoClient
from app.doubao_endpoint_models import (
    DoubaoInferenceEndpoint,
    ModelCostReconciliation,
    VolcengineBillingSettings,
    VolcengineBillingSyncRun,
    VolcengineUsageWindow,
)
from app.doubao_endpoints import (
    DoubaoEndpointError,
    endpoint_readiness_blocker,
    manifest_requires_doubao_endpoint,
    resolve_run_endpoint,

    sign_ark_request,
)
from test_content_ingress_incremental_routing import _close, _fixture

SIGNED_AT = datetime(2026, 9, 7, 5, 0, tzinfo=timezone.utc)
NOW = datetime(2026, 9, 7, 5, 0, tzinfo=timezone.utc)  # 北京时间 09-07 13:00
EP_A = "ep-20260901120000-abcde"
EP_B = "ep-20260901120000-fghij"


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# 签名与解析

def test_sign_ark_request_matches_official_shape() -> None:
    request = sign_ark_request(
        access_key="AKTEST", secret_key="SKTEST", region="cn-beijing",
        action="GetInferenceUsage", body={"QueryInterval": "Hour"}, signed_at=SIGNED_AT,
    )
    assert request.url == "https://ark.cn-beijing.volcengineapi.com/?Action=GetInferenceUsage&Version=2024-01-01"
    assert request.headers["X-Date"] == "20260907T050000Z"
    assert request.headers["Authorization"].startswith(
        "HMAC-SHA256 Credential=AKTEST/20260907/cn-beijing/ark/request, "
        "SignedHeaders=content-type;host;x-content-sha256;x-date, Signature="
    )
    lines = request.canonical_request.split("\n")
    assert lines[:3] == ["POST", "/", "Action=GetInferenceUsage&Version=2024-01-01"]
    assert lines[3] == "content-type:application/json"
    assert lines[4] == "host:ark.cn-beijing.volcengineapi.com"
    other = sign_ark_request(
        access_key="AKTEST", secret_key="SKOTHER", region="cn-beijing",
        action="GetInferenceUsage", body={"QueryInterval": "Hour"}, signed_at=SIGNED_AT,
    )
    assert other.headers["Authorization"] != request.headers["Authorization"]
    with pytest.raises(VolcengineOpenApiError) as forbidden:
        sign_ark_request(access_key="a", secret_key="b", region="cn-beijing", action="DeleteEndpoint", body={}, signed_at=SIGNED_AT)
    assert forbidden.value.code == "volcengine_action_forbidden"


def _usage_document(rows: list[list[str]]) -> dict:
    return {
        "Fields": [{"Name": name, "Type": "STRING"} for name in (
            "AccountID", "ModelEndpoint", "Day", "Hour", "InputTokens", "OutputTokens", "ImageCount", "TotalTokens", "ReqCnt")],
        "Data": rows,
        "DataCount": len(rows),
    }


def test_parse_inference_usage_maps_columns_by_name() -> None:
    rows = parse_inference_usage(_usage_document([["1", EP_A, "2026-09-06", "16", "3062", "914", "0", "3976", "1"]]))
    assert rows[0].endpoint_id == EP_A and rows[0].usage_date == "2026-09-06" and rows[0].usage_hour == 16
    assert (rows[0].input_tokens, rows[0].output_tokens, rows[0].total_tokens, rows[0].request_count) == (3062, 914, 3976, 1)
    with pytest.raises(VolcengineOpenApiError):
        parse_inference_usage({"Fields": [{"Name": "Day"}], "Data": [["x"]]})


def test_doubao_client_routes_request_model_through_endpoint() -> None:
    config = SimpleNamespace(provider="doubao", protocol="openai_chat", model_id="doubao-seed-2-0-lite-260215",
        endpoint_id=EP_A, base_url="https://ark.cn-beijing.volces.com/api/v3", api_path="/chat/completions")
    client = object.__new__(DoubaoClient)
    client.config = config
    assert client._protocol_payload("s", "u")["model"] == EP_A
    config.endpoint_id = None
    assert client._protocol_payload("s", "u")["model"] == "doubao-seed-2-0-lite-260215"
    config.endpoint_id, config.provider = EP_A, "openai"
    assert client._protocol_payload("s", "u")["model"] == "doubao-seed-2-0-lite-260215"


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# 接入点 API
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def _operator(db, role="admin"):
    app = FastAPI()
    app.include_router(build_doubao_endpoints_router(lambda: User(
        username=role, role=role, is_admin=role == "admin", password_hash="x", display_name=role,
    )))
    app.dependency_overrides[get_db] = lambda: (yield db)
    return TestClient(app)


def test_endpoint_registry_bind_and_permissions() -> None:
    engine, db, _, _ = _fixture()
    try:
        admin = _operator(db)
        definition = db.scalar(select(WorkflowDefinition))
        bad = admin.post("/api/doubao-endpoints", json={"endpoint_id": "not-an-endpoint", "display_name": "x", "category_key": "space_image"})
        assert bad.status_code == 422, bad.text
        created = admin.post("/api/doubao-endpoints", json={
            "endpoint_id": EP_A, "display_name": "空间图主接入点", "category_key": "space_image",
            "foundation_model": "doubao-seed-2-0-lite"})
        assert created.status_code == 201, created.text
        row = created.json()
        assert row["revision"] == 1 and row["bindings"] == []
        assert admin.post("/api/doubao-endpoints", json={"endpoint_id": EP_A, "display_name": "dup", "category_key": "space_image"}).status_code == 409
        assert _operator(db, "manager").post("/api/doubao-endpoints", json={"endpoint_id": EP_B, "display_name": "x", "category_key": "space_image"}).status_code == 403
        bound = admin.post(f"/api/doubao-endpoints/{row['id']}/bind", json={
            "workflow_definition_id": definition.id, "expected_revision": 1, "reason": "接入正式链路"})
        assert bound.status_code == 200, bound.text
        assert bound.json()["workflow_definition_ids"] == [definition.id] and bound.json()["revision"] == 2
        assert bound.json()["bindings"][0]["workflow_key"] == definition.workflow_key
        stale = admin.post(f"/api/doubao-endpoints/{row['id']}/bind", json={
            "workflow_definition_id": definition.id, "expected_revision": 1, "reason": "重复"})
        assert stale.status_code == 409 and stale.json()["detail"]["code"] == "doubao_endpoint_revision_conflict"
        # 同一接入点可再绑定第二条同类目工作流（存量回归多条工作流并行）
        sibling = WorkflowDefinition(workflow_key="space_image.regression_b", name="空间图回归 B", owner="ops",
            allowed_categories_json=json.dumps(["space_image"]), created_by="t")
        db.add(sibling)
        db.commit()
        again = admin.post(f"/api/doubao-endpoints/{row['id']}/bind", json={
            "workflow_definition_id": sibling.id, "expected_revision": 2, "reason": "回归第二条"})
        assert again.status_code == 200, again.text
        assert again.json()["workflow_definition_ids"] == [definition.id, sibling.id] and again.json()["revision"] == 3
        repeat = admin.post(f"/api/doubao-endpoints/{row['id']}/bind", json={
            "workflow_definition_id": sibling.id, "expected_revision": 3, "reason": "重复绑定"})
        assert repeat.status_code == 409 and repeat.json()["detail"]["code"] == "doubao_endpoint_already_bound"
        # 工作流改绑到另一接入点：直接切换并留痕，原接入点不再列出该工作流
        second = admin.post("/api/doubao-endpoints", json={"endpoint_id": EP_B, "display_name": "备用", "category_key": "space_image"}).json()
        moved = admin.post(f"/api/doubao-endpoints/{second['id']}/bind", json={
            "workflow_definition_id": definition.id, "expected_revision": 1, "reason": "切到备用"})
        assert moved.status_code == 200 and moved.json()["workflow_definition_ids"] == [definition.id]
        listed = _operator(db, "analyst").get("/api/doubao-endpoints")
        assert listed.status_code == 200 and listed.json()["can_manage"] is False
        by_id = {item["endpoint_id"]: item for item in listed.json()["items"]}
        assert set(by_id) == {EP_A, EP_B}
        assert by_id[EP_A]["workflow_definition_ids"] == [sibling.id] and by_id[EP_B]["workflow_definition_ids"] == [definition.id]
        not_bound = admin.post(f"/api/doubao-endpoints/{row['id']}/unbind", json={
            "workflow_definition_id": definition.id, "expected_revision": 3, "reason": "误操作"})
        assert not_bound.status_code == 409 and not_bound.json()["detail"]["code"] == "doubao_endpoint_not_bound"
        unbound = admin.post(f"/api/doubao-endpoints/{row['id']}/unbind", json={
            "workflow_definition_id": sibling.id, "expected_revision": 3, "reason": "切换"})
        assert unbound.status_code == 200 and unbound.json()["bindings"] == []
        assert db.scalar(select(func.count(AuditEvent.id)).where(AuditEvent.category == "doubao_endpoints")) == 6
    finally:
        _close(engine, db)


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# 运行时解析与就绪
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def _settings(db, **fields) -> VolcengineBillingSettings:
    row = db.get(VolcengineBillingSettings, 1) or VolcengineBillingSettings(id=1)
    for key, value in fields.items():
        setattr(row, key, value)
    db.add(row)
    db.flush()
    return row


def test_resolve_run_endpoint_freezes_on_run_and_blocks_when_routing_required() -> None:
    engine, db, _, _ = _fixture()
    try:
        definition = db.scalar(select(WorkflowDefinition))
        run = SimpleNamespace(workflow_definition_id=definition.id, doubao_endpoint_id=None)
        assert resolve_run_endpoint(db, run, provider="doubao", model_id="doubao-seed-2-0-lite") is None
        _settings(db, endpoint_routing_enabled=True)
        with pytest.raises(DoubaoEndpointError) as blocked:
            resolve_run_endpoint(db, run, provider="doubao", model_id="doubao-seed-2-0-lite")
        assert blocked.value.code == "doubao_endpoint_missing"
        # 非豆包 2.0 模型不受路由开关约束
        assert resolve_run_endpoint(db, run, provider="doubao", model_id="doubao-1-5-vision-pro") is None
        assert resolve_run_endpoint(db, run, provider="openai", model_id="gpt-6") is None
        endpoint = DoubaoInferenceEndpoint(endpoint_id=EP_A, display_name="主", category_key="space_image",
            created_by="t", updated_by="t")
        db.add(endpoint)
        db.flush()
        definition.doubao_endpoint_row_id = endpoint.id
        db.flush()
        # 同一工作流若跑的是 1.8 旧链路模型，绑定不生效、不冻结
        legacy_run = SimpleNamespace(workflow_definition_id=definition.id, doubao_endpoint_id=None)
        assert resolve_run_endpoint(db, legacy_run, provider="doubao", model_id="doubao-seed-1-8-251228") is None
        assert legacy_run.doubao_endpoint_id is None
        assert resolve_run_endpoint(db, run, provider="doubao", model_id="doubao-seed-2-0-lite") == EP_A
        assert run.doubao_endpoint_id == EP_A
        # 运行已冻结：改绑/停用后仍沿用原接入点
        db.scalar(select(DoubaoInferenceEndpoint)).status = "disabled"
        db.flush()
        assert resolve_run_endpoint(db, run, provider="doubao", model_id="doubao-seed-2-0-lite") == EP_A
        fresh = SimpleNamespace(workflow_definition_id=definition.id, doubao_endpoint_id=None)
        with pytest.raises(DoubaoEndpointError):
            resolve_run_endpoint(db, fresh, provider="doubao", model_id="doubao-seed-2-0-lite")
    finally:
        _close(engine, db)


def test_readiness_blocker_only_for_doubao_seed_2_workflows() -> None:
    engine, db, _, _ = _fixture()
    try:
        definition = db.scalar(select(WorkflowDefinition))
        seed2 = ModelRegistryEntry(name="seed2", provider="doubao", model_id="doubao-seed-2-0-lite-260215",
            base_url="https://ark.cn-beijing.volces.com/api/v3", api_path="/chat/completions")
        legacy = ModelRegistryEntry(name="legacy", provider="doubao", model_id="doubao-1-5-vision-pro-32k",
            base_url="https://ark.cn-beijing.volces.com/api/v3", api_path="/chat/completions")
        db.add_all([seed2, legacy])
        db.flush()
        seed2_manifest = {"nodes": [{"kind": "prompt", "config": {"model_registry_id": seed2.id}}]}
        foreach_manifest = {"nodes": [{"kind": "foreach_image", "config": {"item_executor": {"model_registry_id": seed2.id}}}]}
        legacy_manifest = {"nodes": [{"kind": "prompt", "config": {"model_registry_id": legacy.id}}]}
        assert manifest_requires_doubao_endpoint(db, seed2_manifest)
        assert manifest_requires_doubao_endpoint(db, foreach_manifest)
        assert not manifest_requires_doubao_endpoint(db, legacy_manifest)
        assert endpoint_readiness_blocker(db, seed2_manifest, definition.id) is None
        _settings(db, endpoint_routing_enabled=True)
        assert "尚未绑定" in endpoint_readiness_blocker(db, seed2_manifest, definition.id)
        assert endpoint_readiness_blocker(db, legacy_manifest, definition.id) is None
        endpoint = DoubaoInferenceEndpoint(endpoint_id=EP_A, display_name="主", category_key="space_image",
            created_by="t", updated_by="t")
        db.add(endpoint)
        db.flush()
        definition.doubao_endpoint_row_id = endpoint.id
        db.flush()
        assert endpoint_readiness_blocker(db, seed2_manifest, definition.id) is None
    finally:
        _close(engine, db)


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# 账单同步与对账
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def _fake_transport(rows: list[list[str]], calls: list[dict]):
    def transport(method, url, headers, body):
        calls.append({"method": method, "url": url, "body": json.loads(body.decode("utf-8")), "auth": headers["Authorization"]})
        return 200, json.dumps({"ResponseMetadata": {"Action": "GetInferenceUsage"}, "Result": _usage_document(rows)}).encode("utf-8")
    return transport


def test_usage_sync_upserts_windows_and_freezes_previous_day(monkeypatch) -> None:
    engine, db, _, _ = _fixture()
    try:
        monkeypatch.setattr(billing, "_credentials", lambda row: ("AK", "SK"))
        _settings(db, sync_enabled=True, encrypted_access_key="ref-ak", encrypted_secret_key="ref-sk", lookback_days=1)
        db.add(DoubaoInferenceEndpoint(endpoint_id=EP_A, display_name="主", category_key="space_image",
            foundation_model="doubao-seed-2-0-lite", created_by="t", updated_by="t"))
        db.flush()
        local = {("2026-09-06"): {"calls": 5, "input_tokens": 10000, "output_tokens": 2800, "cached_input_tokens": 4000, "cost_micros": 12345, "unresolved_calls": 0},
                 ("2026-09-07"): {"calls": 1, "input_tokens": 500, "output_tokens": 100, "cached_input_tokens": 0, "cost_micros": 700, "unresolved_calls": 1}}
        monkeypatch.setattr(billing, "local_day_aggregate", lambda db_, endpoint_id, day: dict(local.get(day) or
            {"calls": 0, "input_tokens": 0, "output_tokens": 0, "cached_input_tokens": 0, "cost_micros": 0, "unresolved_calls": 0}))
        calls: list[dict] = []
        rows = [
            ["1", EP_A, "2026-09-06", "10", "6000", "1800", "0", "7800", "3"],
            ["1", EP_A, "2026-09-06", "11", "4000", "1000", "0", "5000", "2"],
            ["1", EP_A, "2026-09-07", "12", "500", "100", "0", "600", "1"],
        ]
        summary = billing.sync_due_usage_windows(db, actor="w1", now=NOW, transport=_fake_transport(rows, calls))
        db.commit()
        assert summary["ran"] is True and summary["status"] == "succeeded", summary
        assert calls[0]["body"]["QueryInterval"] == "Hour" and calls[0]["body"]["Filters"] == [{"Key": "ModelEndpoint", "Values": [EP_A]}]
        assert calls[0]["body"]["StartTime"] == "2026-09-06" and calls[0]["body"]["EndTime"] == "2026-09-08"
        assert db.scalar(select(func.count(VolcengineUsageWindow.id))) == 3
        frozen = db.scalar(select(ModelCostReconciliation).where(ModelCostReconciliation.business_date == "2026-09-06"))
        assert frozen.status == "frozen" and frozen.match_status == "matched"
        assert (frozen.remote_input_tokens, frozen.remote_output_tokens, frozen.remote_requests) == (10000, 2800, 5)
        assert frozen.local_cached_input_tokens == 4000 and frozen.local_cost_micros == 12345
        # 参考费用：首档 600/3600 微元每千 token，无缓存折扣
        assert frozen.remote_reference_cost_micros == 10 * 600 + (2800 * 3600 + 999) // 1000
        today = db.scalar(select(ModelCostReconciliation).where(ModelCostReconciliation.business_date == "2026-09-07"))
        assert today.status == "open" and today.match_status == "matched"
        settings = db.get(VolcengineBillingSettings, 1)
        assert settings.consecutive_failures == 0 and settings.last_sync_succeeded_at is not None
        # 间隔未到不再拉取；冻结行不会被后续同步改写
        again = billing.sync_due_usage_windows(db, actor="w1", now=NOW + timedelta(minutes=10), transport=_fake_transport(rows, calls))
        assert again["ran"] is False and again["reason"] == "not_due"
        local["2026-09-06"]["input_tokens"] = 1
        billing.sync_due_usage_windows(db, actor="w1", now=NOW + timedelta(hours=2), transport=_fake_transport(rows, calls))
        db.refresh(frozen)
        assert frozen.local_input_tokens == 10000 and frozen.match_status == "matched"
        dashboard = billing.billing_dashboard(db, now=NOW + timedelta(hours=2))
        assert dashboard["endpoints"][0]["yesterday"]["status"] == "frozen"
        assert dashboard["endpoints"][0]["today"]["local"]["calls"] == 1
    finally:
        _close(engine, db)


def test_usage_sync_failure_records_error_and_never_freezes(monkeypatch) -> None:
    engine, db, _, _ = _fixture()
    try:
        monkeypatch.setattr(billing, "_credentials", lambda row: ("AK", "SK"))
        _settings(db, sync_enabled=True, encrypted_access_key="ref-ak", encrypted_secret_key="ref-sk", lookback_days=1)
        db.add(DoubaoInferenceEndpoint(endpoint_id=EP_A, display_name="主", category_key="space_image", created_by="t", updated_by="t"))
        db.flush()
        monkeypatch.setattr(billing, "local_day_aggregate", lambda db_, endpoint_id, day:
            {"calls": 3, "input_tokens": 900, "output_tokens": 90, "cached_input_tokens": 0, "cost_micros": 500, "unresolved_calls": 0})

        def broken(method, url, headers, body):
            return 403, json.dumps({"ResponseMetadata": {"Error": {"Code": "AccessDenied", "Message": "no permission"}}}).encode("utf-8")

        summary = billing.sync_due_usage_windows(db, actor="w1", now=NOW, transport=broken)
        db.commit()
        assert summary["ran"] is True and summary["status"] == "failed" and summary["error_code"] == "volcengine_AccessDenied"
        settings = db.get(VolcengineBillingSettings, 1)
        assert settings.consecutive_failures == 1 and "AccessDenied" in settings.last_sync_error
        run = db.scalar(select(VolcengineBillingSyncRun))
        assert run.status == "failed" and run.reconciliations_frozen == 0
        # 本地视角仍然刷新，但业务日不冻结，且远端缺失被标记出来
        yesterday = db.scalar(select(ModelCostReconciliation).where(ModelCostReconciliation.business_date == "2026-09-06"))
        assert yesterday.status == "open" and yesterday.match_status == "remote_missing"
        health = billing.billing_health(db, now=NOW)
        assert health["consecutive_failures"] == 1 and health["overdue_open_days"] == 1
        alerts = {item["code"]: item["severity"] for item in evaluate_operational_alerts({"volcengine_billing": health})}
        assert alerts["volcengine_billing_sync_failed"] == "warning"
        assert alerts["volcengine_billing_sync_stale"] == "critical"
        assert alerts["volcengine_billing_freeze_stalled"] == "warning"
        disabled = billing.sync_due_usage_windows(db, actor="w1", now=NOW, transport=broken)
        assert disabled["ran"] is False  # 上次启动时间已记录，未到间隔
    finally:
        _close(engine, db)


def test_billing_settings_api_masks_secrets_and_guards_switch(monkeypatch) -> None:
    engine, db, _, _ = _fixture()
    try:
        monkeypatch.setattr(billing, "protect_secret", lambda secret, *, account: f"ref:{account}")
        admin = _operator(db)
        initial = admin.get("/api/doubao-endpoints/billing/settings")
        assert initial.status_code == 200 and initial.json()["revision"] == 1 and initial.json()["credentials_ready"] is False
        blocked = admin.put("/api/doubao-endpoints/billing/settings", json={"expected_revision": 1, "sync_enabled": True})
        assert blocked.status_code == 422 and blocked.json()["detail"]["code"] == "volcengine_credentials_missing"
        saved = admin.put("/api/doubao-endpoints/billing/settings", json={
            "expected_revision": 1, "access_key": "AKREAL", "secret_key": "SKREAL", "sync_enabled": True,
            "endpoint_routing_enabled": True, "reason": "启用对账"})
        assert saved.status_code == 200, saved.text
        body = saved.json()
        assert body["credentials_ready"] is True and body["sync_enabled"] is True and body["endpoint_routing_enabled"] is True
        assert "AKREAL" not in saved.text and "SKREAL" not in saved.text
        assert body["revision"] == 2
        assert _operator(db, "manager").put("/api/doubao-endpoints/billing/settings", json={"expected_revision": 2, "sync_enabled": False}).status_code == 403
        audit = db.scalar(select(AuditEvent).where(AuditEvent.category == "volcengine_billing"))
        assert "AKREAL" not in audit.payload_json and "ref:" not in audit.payload_json
        dashboard = admin.get("/api/doubao-endpoints/billing/dashboard")
        assert dashboard.status_code == 200 and dashboard.json()["settings"]["endpoint_routing_enabled"] is True
    finally:
        _close(engine, db)


def test_provision_endpoint_dry_runs_then_creates_tagged_endpoint_and_binds() -> None:
    engine, db, _, _ = _fixture()
    try:
        from app.doubao_endpoints import provision_endpoint
        definition = db.scalar(select(WorkflowDefinition))
        calls: list[dict] = []

        class FakeClient:
            def create_endpoint(self, **kwargs):
                calls.append(dict(kwargs))
                return None if kwargs["dry_run"] else "ep-20260907120000-prov1"

        row = provision_endpoint(
            db, client=FakeClient(), workflow_definition_id=definition.id, category_key="space_image",
            foundation_model="doubao-seed-2-0-lite", model_version="260215", deploy_environment="test",
            actor="sol", rpm=600, reason="自动创建",
        )
        db.commit()
        assert [c["dry_run"] for c in calls] == [True, False]
        assert calls[1]["name"] == f"label-test-space_image-{definition.workflow_key.lower()}"
        assert calls[1]["tags"] == {"platform": "3d66-label-system", "managed_by": "label-system", "env": "test",
            "category": "space_image", "workflow": definition.workflow_key}
        assert calls[1]["rpm"] == 600 and calls[1]["foundation_model"] == "doubao-seed-2-0-lite"
        assert row.endpoint_id == "ep-20260907120000-prov1" and definition.doubao_endpoint_row_id == row.id
        assert row.provisioned_by_platform is True and row.remote_name.startswith("label-test-")
        assert json.loads(row.tags_json)[0] == {"Key": "platform", "Value": "3d66-label-system"}
        # 已有生效绑定时拒绝再创建，且不会调用火山
        with pytest.raises(DoubaoEndpointError) as blocked:
            provision_endpoint(db, client=FakeClient(), workflow_definition_id=definition.id, category_key="space_image",
                foundation_model="doubao-seed-2-0-lite", model_version="", deploy_environment="test", actor="sol")
        assert blocked.value.code == "doubao_endpoint_workflow_occupied" and len(calls) == 2
        listed = _operator(db, "analyst").get("/api/doubao-endpoints").json()["items"][0]
        assert listed["provisioned_by_platform"] is True and listed["tags"][2] == {"Key": "env", "Value": "test"}
    finally:
        _close(engine, db)


def test_create_endpoint_request_shape_and_forbidden_actions() -> None:
    from app.volcengine_ark_openapi import ArkControlPlaneClient
    calls: list[dict] = []

    def transport(method, url, headers, body):
        calls.append({"url": url, "body": json.loads(body.decode("utf-8"))})
        return 200, json.dumps({"ResponseMetadata": {}, "Result": {"Id": "ep-20260907120000-abcde"}}).encode("utf-8")

    client = ArkControlPlaneClient(access_key="AK", secret_key="SK", transport=transport, now=lambda: SIGNED_AT)
    created = client.create_endpoint(name="label-test-x", foundation_model="doubao-seed-2-0-lite", model_version="260215",
        tags={"env": "test"}, rpm=100, dry_run=False)
    assert created == "ep-20260907120000-abcde"
    assert "Action=CreateEndpoint" in calls[0]["url"]
    assert calls[0]["body"]["ModelReference"] == {"FoundationModel": {"Name": "doubao-seed-2-0-lite", "ModelVersion": "260215"}}
    assert calls[0]["body"]["Tags"] == [{"Key": "env", "Value": "test"}] and calls[0]["body"]["RateLimit"] == {"Rpm": 100}
    assert calls[0]["body"]["DryRun"] is False
    for action in ("DeleteEndpoint", "UpdateEndpoint", "StopEndpoint"):
        with pytest.raises(VolcengineOpenApiError):
            sign_ark_request(access_key="a", secret_key="b", region="cn-beijing", action=action, body={}, signed_at=SIGNED_AT)

===== FILE backend/tests/test_doubao_multi_image_text.py =====
from __future__ import annotations

import asyncio
from types import SimpleNamespace

import pytest
import httpx
from PIL import Image

from app.doubao import (
    CacheUsageObservation,
    DoubaoClient,
    DoubaoParseError,
    DoubaoHTTPError,
    DoubaoStructuredOutputError,
    response_cache_usage,
    response_media_usage,
)


def test_truncated_json_keeps_provider_usage_and_cache_evidence():
    client = _client()
    async def fake_post(payload):
        return {"choices": [{"finish_reason": "length", "message": {"content": '{"score":'}}],
                "usage": {"prompt_tokens": 100, "completion_tokens": 50, "total_tokens": 150,
                          "prompt_tokens_details": {"cached_tokens": 40}}}
    client._post = fake_post
    with pytest.raises(DoubaoParseError) as error:
        asyncio.run(client.chat_json("system", "user", max_attempts=1))
    assert error.value.provider_usage == {"input_tokens": 100, "output_tokens": 50,
        "cached_input_tokens": 40, "cache_observation_status": "hit"}
    assert error.value.finish_reason == "length"
    assert error.value.technical_error_type == "json_truncated"


@pytest.mark.parametrize("multi_image", [False, True])
def test_early_unterminated_string_uses_provider_length_reason(tmp_path, multi_image):
    client = _client()
    text = '{"reason":"' + '重复描述' * 100
    async def fake_post(payload):
        return {"choices": [{"finish_reason": "length", "message": {"content": text}}],
                "usage": {"prompt_tokens": 80, "completion_tokens": 1000,
                          "total_tokens": 1080}}
    client._post = fake_post
    with pytest.raises(DoubaoParseError) as caught:
        if multi_image:
            image = tmp_path / "sample.png"
            Image.new("RGB", (2, 2), "white").save(image)
            asyncio.run(client.chat_json_images("system", [("第1页", image, "image/png")],
                                                max_attempts=1))
        else:
            asyncio.run(client.chat_json("system", "user", max_attempts=1))
    error = caught.value
    assert error.evidence["json_parse"]["offset"] < 20
    assert error.technical_error_type == "json_truncated"
    assert error.finish_reason == "length"
    assert error.provider_usage["output_tokens"] == 1000


def test_http_429_retains_only_bounded_provider_code(monkeypatch):
    # Initialize typed transport definitions before replacing AsyncClient with a
    # test factory; otherwise a cold import evaluates function | None.
    from app import provider_http_pool  # noqa: F401
    client = _client()
    client.config.timeout_seconds = 5
    client.config.base_url = "https://provider.invalid"
    client.config.api_path = "/chat/completions"
    client._dispatch_hook = None
    transport = httpx.MockTransport(lambda request: httpx.Response(429,
        headers={"Retry-After": "1"}, json={"error": {"code": "ServerOverloaded",
        "message": "sensitive provider text must not be copied"}}))
    original = httpx.AsyncClient
    monkeypatch.setattr(httpx, "AsyncClient", lambda **kwargs: original(transport=transport, **kwargs))
    with pytest.raises(DoubaoHTTPError) as error:
        asyncio.run(client._http_post(payload={"model": "fixture"}))
    assert error.value.provider_error_code == "ServerOverloaded"
    assert error.value.retry_after_seconds == 1
    assert "sensitive" not in str(error.value)


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    ("payload", "input_tokens", "expected"),
    [
        (
            {
                "usage": {
                    "prompt_tokens_details": {"cached_tokens": 7_000}
                }
            },
            8_000,
            CacheUsageObservation(
                status="hit",
                cached_input_tokens=7_000,
                source="usage.prompt_tokens_details.cached_tokens",
            ),
        ),
        (
            {
                "usage": {
                    "input_tokens_details": {"cached_tokens": 0}
                }
            },
            8_000,
            CacheUsageObservation(
                status="miss",
                cached_input_tokens=0,
                source="usage.input_tokens_details.cached_tokens",
            ),
        ),
        (
            {"usage": {"prompt_tokens_details": {}}},
            8_000,
            CacheUsageObservation(
                status="unreported",
                cached_input_tokens=None,
                source=None,
            ),
        ),
    ],
)
def test_response_cache_usage_reads_only_documented_fields(
    payload: dict,
    input_tokens: int,
    expected: CacheUsageObservation,
) -> None:
    assert response_cache_usage(payload, input_tokens=input_tokens) == expected


@pytest.mark.parametrize("cached_tokens", [True, "7000", -1, 8_001])
def test_response_cache_usage_marks_malformed_values_invalid(
    cached_tokens: object,
) -> None:
    observation = response_cache_usage(
        {
            "usage": {
                "prompt_tokens_details": {
                    "cached_tokens": cached_tokens,
                }
            }
        },
        input_tokens=8_000,
    )

    assert observation.status == "invalid"
    assert observation.cached_input_tokens is None
    assert observation.source == "usage.prompt_tokens_details.cached_tokens"


def test_response_media_usage_reads_aggregate_and_per_image_details() -> None:
    assert response_media_usage(
        {
            "usage": {
                "prompt_tokens_details": {
                    "image_tokens": 37,
                    "image_tokens_by_image": [12, 25],
                }
            }
        }
    ) == (37, (12, 25))
    assert response_media_usage(
        {"usage": {"prompt_tokens_details": {"image_tokens": 37}}}
    ) == (37, None)
    assert response_media_usage(
        {
            "usage": {
                "prompt_tokens_details": {
                    "image_tokens": 37,
                    "image_tokens_by_image": [12, 24],
                }
            }
        }
    ) == (37, None)


def _client() -> DoubaoClient:
    client = object.__new__(DoubaoClient)
    client.api_key = "secret-value"
    client.config = SimpleNamespace(
        protocol="openai_chat",
        provider="doubao",
        model_id="model-x",
        max_tokens=1000,
        temperature=0.2,
        structured_output=True,
        max_retries=0,
    )
    return client


def test_chat_text_images_sends_individual_pages_without_structured_rewrite(tmp_path) -> None:
    first = tmp_path / "1.png"
    second = tmp_path / "2.png"
    Image.new("RGB", (10, 12), "white").save(first)
    Image.new("RGB", (12, 10), "black").save(second)
    client = _client()
    calls: list[dict[str, object]] = []

    async def fake_post(payload: dict[str, object]) -> dict[str, object]:
        calls.append(payload)
        return {
            "choices": [{"message": {"content": '{"status":"ok"}'}}],
            "usage": {
                "prompt_tokens": 321,
                "completion_tokens": 45,
                "total_tokens": 366,
                "prompt_tokens_details": {
                    "cached_tokens": 64,
                    "image_tokens": 101,
                    "image_tokens_by_image": [40, 61],
                },
            },
        }

    client._post = fake_post  # type: ignore[method-assign]
    response = asyncio.run(client.chat_text_images(
        "system",
        "global user context",
        [("第1页", first, "image/png"), ("第2页", second, "image/png")],
        image_detail="low",
        max_image_count=16,
    ))

    assert response.parsed == {"status": "ok"}
    assert response.input_tokens == 321
    assert response.output_tokens == 45
    assert response.total_tokens == 366
    assert response.input_image_tokens == 101
    assert response.per_image_tokens == (40, 61)
    assert response.cache_usage == CacheUsageObservation(
        status="hit",
        cached_input_tokens=64,
        source="usage.prompt_tokens_details.cached_tokens",
    )
    assert len(calls) == 1
    assert "response_format" not in calls[0]
    content = calls[0]["messages"][1]["content"]
    assert content[0] == {"type": "text", "text": "global user context"}
    assert [item["type"] for item in content] == [
        "text", "text", "image_url", "text", "image_url",
    ]
    assert content[2]["image_url"]["detail"] == "low"


def test_chat_json_sends_strict_json_schema_response_format() -> None:
    client = _client()
    calls: list[dict[str, object]] = []
    schema = {
        "type": "object",
        "properties": {"score": {"type": "integer"}},
        "required": ["score"],
        "additionalProperties": False,
    }

    async def fake_post(payload: dict[str, object]) -> dict[str, object]:
        calls.append(payload)
        return {"choices": [{"message": {"content": '{"score":80}'}}]}

    client._post = fake_post  # type: ignore[method-assign]
    response = asyncio.run(
        client.chat_json(
            "system",
            "user",
            output_contract={
                "mode": "json_schema",
                "name": "aesthetic_result",
                "description": "美感图结果",
                "strict": True,
                "schema": schema,
            },
        )
    )

    assert response.parsed == {"score": 80}
    assert calls[0]["response_format"] == {
        "type": "json_schema",
        "json_schema": {
            "name": "aesthetic_result",
            "description": "美感图结果",
            "strict": True,
            "schema": schema,
        },
    }


def test_chat_json_retries_schema_mismatch_with_a_bounded_error() -> None:
    client = _client()
    calls: list[dict[str, object]] = []

    async def fake_post(payload: dict[str, object]) -> dict[str, object]:
        calls.append(payload)
        return {
            "choices": [
                {"message": {"content": '{"score":80,"extra":true}'}}
            ]
        }

    client._post = fake_post  # type: ignore[method-assign]
    with pytest.raises(DoubaoStructuredOutputError) as exc_info:
        asyncio.run(
            client.chat_json(
                "system",
                "user",
                max_attempts=2,
                output_contract={
                    "mode": "json_schema",
                    "name": "aesthetic_result",
                    "strict": True,
                    "schema": {
                        "type": "object",
                        "properties": {"score": {"type": "integer"}},
                        "required": ["score"],
                        "additionalProperties": False,
                    },
                },
            )
        )

    assert len(calls) == 2
    assert exc_info.value.attempt_count == 2
    assert exc_info.value.technical_error_type == "structured_output_schema_mismatch"


def test_invalid_production_fields_survive_as_rejected_candidates():
    client = _client()
    async def fake_post(payload):
        return {'choices':[{'message':{'content':'{"production_fields":{"seotitle":"过长标题"}}'}}]}
    client._post = fake_post
    with pytest.raises(DoubaoStructuredOutputError) as caught:
        asyncio.run(client.chat_json('system','user',max_attempts=1,output_contract={
            'mode':'json_schema','name':'production','strict':True,'schema':{
                'type':'object','properties':{'production_fields':{'type':'object',
                'properties':{'seotitle':{'type':'string','maxLength':2}},'required':['seotitle'],
                'additionalProperties':False}},'required':['production_fields'],'additionalProperties':False}}))
    assert caught.value.evidence['candidate_production_fields']['seotitle']=='过长标题'
    assert 'maxLength' in caught.value.evidence['validation_error']


# --- 客户端内重试策略（结果不明不重发 / 429 退避 / 不可重试直接抛） -------------

_OK_JSON = {"choices": [{"finish_reason": "stop", "message": {"content": '{"score": 1}'}}],
            "usage": {"prompt_tokens": 10, "completion_tokens": 2, "total_tokens": 12}}


def _scripted_client(monkeypatch, outcomes):
    from app import doubao

    client = _client()
    posts: list[dict] = []
    sleeps: list[float] = []

    async def fake_post(payload):
        posts.append(payload)
        outcome = outcomes[len(posts) - 1]
        if isinstance(outcome, BaseException):
            raise outcome
        return outcome

    async def fake_sleep(delay):
        sleeps.append(delay)

    client._post = fake_post
    monkeypatch.setattr(doubao, "_retry_sleep", fake_sleep)
    return client, posts, sleeps


@pytest.mark.parametrize("multi_image", [False, True])
def test_retry_loop_never_resends_unknown_outcome(tmp_path, monkeypatch, multi_image):
    from app.doubao import DoubaoTransportError

    client, posts, sleeps = _scripted_client(monkeypatch, [DoubaoTransportError("timeout"), _OK_JSON])
    with pytest.raises(DoubaoTransportError):
        if multi_image:
            image = tmp_path / "s.png"
            Image.new("RGB", (2, 2), "white").save(image)
            asyncio.run(client.chat_json_images("system", [("第1页", image, "image/png")], max_attempts=3))
        else:
            asyncio.run(client.chat_json("system", "user", max_attempts=3))
    assert len(posts) == 1, "超时（结果不明）可能已计费，不能自动重发"
    assert sleeps == []


@pytest.mark.parametrize("multi_image", [False, True])
def test_retry_loop_backs_off_on_429_and_resends(tmp_path, monkeypatch, multi_image):
    throttled = DoubaoHTTPError(429, {"Retry-After": "7"})
    client, posts, sleeps = _scripted_client(monkeypatch, [throttled, _OK_JSON])
    if multi_image:
        image = tmp_path / "s.png"
        Image.new("RGB", (2, 2), "white").save(image)
        result = asyncio.run(client.chat_json_images("system", [("第1页", image, "image/png")], max_attempts=3))
    else:
        result = asyncio.run(client.chat_json("system", "user", max_attempts=3))
    assert result.parsed == {"score": 1} and result.attempt_count == 2
    assert len(posts) == 2 and sleeps == [7.0]
    if not multi_image:
        assert posts[1] == posts[0], "429 重发原请求，不追加 JSON 重问提示"


def test_retry_loop_raises_non_retryable_error_immediately(monkeypatch):
    rejected = DoubaoHTTPError(400, {})
    client, posts, _sleeps = _scripted_client(monkeypatch, [rejected, _OK_JSON])
    with pytest.raises(DoubaoHTTPError):
        asyncio.run(client.chat_json("system", "user", max_attempts=3))
    assert len(posts) == 1


def test_openai_responses_json_reask_uses_input_not_messages(monkeypatch):
    """openai_responses 请求体没有 messages：重问不能 KeyError 掩盖原始错误。"""
    bad = {"choices": [{"finish_reason": "stop", "message": {"content": "不是 JSON"}}]}
    client, posts, _sleeps = _scripted_client(monkeypatch, [bad, _OK_JSON])
    client.config.protocol = "openai_responses"
    result = asyncio.run(client.chat_json("system", "user", max_attempts=2))
    assert result.parsed == {"score": 1}
    assert "messages" not in posts[1] and len(posts[1]["input"]) == len(posts[0]["input"]) + 1


def test_pool_timeout_is_not_dispatched_and_retryable(monkeypatch):
    """连接池排队超时：请求没发出，不能当供应商超时（结果不明、保留费用责任）。"""
    from app import provider_http_pool  # noqa: F401
    from app.doubao import DoubaoPoolTimeoutError

    client = _client()
    client.config.timeout_seconds = 5
    client.config.base_url = "https://provider.invalid"
    client.config.api_path = "/chat/completions"
    fired = []
    client._dispatch_hook = lambda: fired.append(1)

    def handler(request):
        raise httpx.PoolTimeout("pool exhausted", request=request)

    transport = httpx.MockTransport(handler)
    original = httpx.AsyncClient
    monkeypatch.setattr(httpx, "AsyncClient", lambda **kwargs: original(transport=transport, **kwargs))
    with pytest.raises(DoubaoPoolTimeoutError) as caught:
        asyncio.run(client._http_post(payload={"model": "fixture"}))
    error = caught.value
    assert error.outcome_unknown is False and error.retryable is True
    assert error.provider_dispatched is False
    assert error.technical_error_type == "pool_timeout"

===== FILE backend/tests/test_doubao_prefix_cache_order.py =====
"""多图请求把锚点排在可变文本之前，让 system prompt + 锚点成为稳定前缀（隐式前缀缓存）。"""
from __future__ import annotations

from pathlib import Path

from app.doubao import ProviderImageSample, _split_samples_for_prefix_cache
from app.vision_input_policy import DetailFidelity


def _sample(label: str, role: str, ordinal: int) -> ProviderImageSample:
    return ProviderImageSample(
        label=label,
        path=Path("/dev/null"),
        mime_type="image/jpeg",
        role=role,
        ordinal=ordinal,
        fidelity=DetailFidelity("high"),
        policy_hash=None,
    )


def test_anchors_go_before_text_and_target_stays_after() -> None:
    samples = (
        _sample("锚点 L1", "anchor", 0),
        _sample("锚点 L2", "anchor", 1),
        _sample("待评图", "target", 2),
    )
    prefix, suffix = _split_samples_for_prefix_cache(samples)
    assert [s.label for s in prefix] == ["锚点 L1", "锚点 L2"]
    assert [s.label for s in suffix] == ["待评图"]


def test_target_only_request_keeps_text_first() -> None:
    samples = (_sample("待评图", "target", 0),)
    prefix, suffix = _split_samples_for_prefix_cache(samples)
    assert prefix == ()
    assert [s.label for s in suffix] == ["待评图"]

===== FILE backend/tests/test_doubao_thinking.py =====
from __future__ import annotations

from types import SimpleNamespace

import pytest

from app.doubao import DoubaoClient


def _client(
    *,
    provider: str = "doubao",
    thinking_mode: str = "auto",
    protocol: str = "openai_chat",
    model_id: str = "model-x",
) -> DoubaoClient:
    client = object.__new__(DoubaoClient)
    client.config = SimpleNamespace(
        protocol=protocol,
        provider=provider,
        model_id=model_id,
        max_tokens=1000,
        temperature=0.2,
        structured_output=True,
        thinking_mode=thinking_mode,
    )
    return client


def test_doubao_auto_does_not_override_provider_default() -> None:
    payload: dict[str, object] = {}
    _, trace_reasoning, trace_thinking = _client()._generation_options(
        payload,
        output_budget=None,
        reasoning_effort="high",
        structured_output=False,
    )
    assert "reasoning_effort" not in payload
    assert "thinking" not in payload
    assert trace_reasoning == "high"
    assert trace_thinking == "auto"


def test_doubao_seed_2_chat_caps_reasoning_and_answer_with_completion_budget() -> None:
    payload: dict[str, object] = {}
    budget, trace_reasoning, trace_thinking = _client(
        model_id="doubao-seed-2-0-lite-260215",
    )._generation_options(
        payload,
        output_budget=4096,
        reasoning_effort="medium",
        structured_output=False,
    )

    assert budget == 4096
    assert payload["max_completion_tokens"] == 4096
    assert payload["reasoning_effort"] == "medium"
    assert "max_tokens" not in payload
    assert "thinking" not in payload
    assert trace_reasoning == "medium"
    assert trace_thinking == "auto"


def test_doubao_seed_2_explicit_thinking_and_effort_are_both_sent() -> None:
    payload: dict[str, object] = {}
    _, trace_reasoning, trace_thinking = _client(
        model_id="doubao-seed-2-0-lite-260215",
        thinking_mode="enabled",
    )._generation_options(
        payload,
        output_budget=4096,
        reasoning_effort="high",
        structured_output=False,
    )

    assert payload["thinking"] == {"type": "enabled"}
    assert payload["reasoning_effort"] == "high"
    assert trace_reasoning == "high"
    assert trace_thinking == "enabled"

@pytest.mark.parametrize("mode", ["enabled", "disabled"])
def test_doubao_explicit_thinking_mode_uses_ark_payload(

    _, trace_reasoning, trace_thinking = _client(
        provider="openai", thinking_mode="disabled"

        payload,
        output_budget=None,
        reasoning_effort="low",

    assert "thinking" not in payload
    assert trace_reasoning == "low"
    assert trace_thinking == "disabled"

        output_budget=512,
        reasoning_effort="high",
