===== FILE backend/tests/test_doubao_vision_policy.py =====
from __future__ import annotations

import asyncio
import json
from pathlib import Path
from types import SimpleNamespace

import pytest
from PIL import Image

from app.doubao import (
    DoubaoCapabilityMismatchError,
    DoubaoClient,
    ProviderImageSample,
)
from app.vision_input_policy import (
    SEED_20_PROFILE,
    DetailFidelity,
    PixelLimitFidelity,
)


def _profile(*, detail_modes: list[str] | None = None) -> dict[str, object]:
    profile = dict(SEED_20_PROFILE)
    profile["profile_key"] = "test-ark-vision-policy-v1"
    profile["detail_modes"] = detail_modes or ["low", "high", "xhigh"]
    return profile


def _client(*, profile: dict[str, object] | None = None) -> DoubaoClient:
    client = object.__new__(DoubaoClient)
    client.api_key = "secret-value"
    client.config = SimpleNamespace(
        protocol="openai_chat",
        provider="doubao",
        model_id="doubao-seed-2-0-lite-260215",
        max_tokens=1000,
        temperature=0.2,
        structured_output=True,
        max_retries=0,
        thinking_mode="auto",
        vision_capability_profile_json=json.dumps(profile or _profile()),
    )
    return client


def _image(path: Path, color: str) -> Path:
    Image.new("RGB", (20, 16), color).save(path, format="PNG")
    return path


def _contract() -> dict[str, object]:
    return {
        "mode": "json_schema",
        "name": "vision_result",
        "strict": True,
        "schema": {
            "type": "object",
            "properties": {"score": {"type": "integer"}},
            "required": ["score"],
            "additionalProperties": False,
        },
    }


def test_each_image_gets_mutually_exclusive_fidelity_and_keeps_order(
    tmp_path: Path,
) -> None:
    client = _client()
    calls: list[dict[str, object]] = []

    async def fake_post(payload: dict[str, object]) -> dict[str, object]:
        calls.append(payload)
        return {"choices": [{"message": {"content": '{"score":80}'}}]}

    client._post = fake_post  # type: ignore[method-assign]
    samples = (
        ProviderImageSample(
            label="锚点 L3 #0",
            path=_image(tmp_path / "anchor.png", "red"),
            mime_type="image/png",
            role="anchor",
            ordinal=0,
            fidelity=DetailFidelity("low"),
            policy_hash="a" * 64,
        ),
        ProviderImageSample(
            label="目标图 #0",
            path=_image(tmp_path / "target.png", "blue"),
            mime_type="image/png",
            role="target",
            ordinal=0,
            fidelity=PixelLimitFidelity(
                min_pixels=3136,
                max_pixels=3_014_080,
            ),
            policy_hash="a" * 64,
        ),
    )

    response = asyncio.run(
        client.chat_json_images(
            "system",
            samples,
            user_prompt="user",
            output_contract=_contract(),
        )
    )

    assert response.parsed == {"score": 80}
    assert len(calls) == 1
    payload = calls[0]
    assert payload["response_format"]["type"] == "json_schema"
    content = payload["messages"][1]["content"]
    # 锚点排在可变文本之前（隐式前缀缓存：system + 锚点是稳定前缀），待评图在文本之后
    assert [item.get("text") for item in content if item["type"] == "text"] == [
        "锚点 L3 #0",
        "user",
        "目标图 #0",
    ]
    image_items = [item for item in content if item["type"] == "image_url"]
    anchor_url = image_items[0]["image_url"]
    target_url = image_items[1]["image_url"]
    assert anchor_url["detail"] == "low"
    assert "image_pixel_limit" not in anchor_url
    assert "detail" not in target_url
    assert target_url["image_pixel_limit"] == {
        "min_pixels": 3136,
        "max_pixels": 3_014_080,
    }


def test_unsupported_xhigh_fails_before_http_dispatch(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    frozen_profile = _profile(detail_modes=["low", "high"])
    client = _client(profile=frozen_profile)
    del client.config.vision_capability_profile_json
    client.config.vision_capability_profile = frozen_profile
    call_count = 0

    async def fake_post(_payload: dict[str, object]) -> dict[str, object]:
        nonlocal call_count
        call_count += 1
        return {"choices": [{"message": {"content": '{"score":80}'}}]}

    client._post = fake_post  # type: ignore[method-assign]
    sample = ProviderImageSample(
        label="目标图 #0",
        path=_image(tmp_path / "target-xhigh.png", "green"),
        mime_type="image/png",
        role="target",
        ordinal=0,
        fidelity=DetailFidelity("xhigh"),
        policy_hash="b" * 64,
    )

    with pytest.raises(DoubaoCapabilityMismatchError) as excinfo:
        asyncio.run(
            client.chat_json_images(
                "system",
                (sample,),
                output_contract=_contract(),
            )
        )

    assert excinfo.value.technical_error_type == "vision_capability_mismatch"
    assert call_count == 0


def test_text_image_call_uses_same_per_image_serializer(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    client = _client()
    calls: list[dict[str, object]] = []

    async def fake_post(payload: dict[str, object]) -> dict[str, object]:
        calls.append(payload)
        return {"choices": [{"message": {"content": "可读文本"}}]}

    client._post = fake_post  # type: ignore[method-assign]
    sample = ProviderImageSample(
        label="锚点 L2 #0",
        path=_image(tmp_path / "text-anchor.png", "yellow"),
        mime_type="image/png",
        role="anchor",
        ordinal=0,
        fidelity=PixelLimitFidelity(min_pixels=3136, max_pixels=2_257_920),
        policy_hash="c" * 64,
    )

    response = asyncio.run(
        client.chat_text_images("system", "user", (sample,))
    )

    assert response.raw_text == "可读文本"
    assert "response_format" not in calls[0]
    content = calls[0]["messages"][1]["content"]
    image_url = next(
        item["image_url"] for item in content if item["type"] == "image_url"
    )
    assert image_url["image_pixel_limit"] == {
        "min_pixels": 3136,
        "max_pixels": 2_257_920,
    }
    assert "detail" not in image_url


def test_prepared_request_sends_the_exact_gated_json_bytes(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    client = _client()
    sample = ProviderImageSample(
        label="目标图 #0",
        path=_image(tmp_path / "prepared-target.png", "purple"),
        mime_type="image/png",
        role="target",
        ordinal=0,
        fidelity=DetailFidelity("high"),
        policy_hash="d" * 64,
    )
    prepared = DoubaoClient.serialize_chat_json_images_request(
        client.config,
        "system",
        (sample,),
        user_prompt="user",
        output_budget=800,
        reasoning_effort="high",
        structured_output=True,
        output_contract=_contract(),
        max_image_count=1,
    )
    sent: list[bytes] = []

    async def fake_post_serialized(body: bytes) -> dict[str, object]:
        sent.append(body)
        return {
            "choices": [{"message": {"content": '{"score":80}'}}],
        }

    client._post_serialized = fake_post_serialized  # type: ignore[method-assign]
    response = asyncio.run(
        client.chat_json_images(
            "system",
            (sample,),
            user_prompt="user",
            output_budget=800,
            reasoning_effort="high",
            structured_output=True,
            output_contract=_contract(),
            max_image_count=1,
            prepared_request=prepared,
        )
    )

    assert response.parsed == {"score": 80}
    assert sent == [prepared.body]

===== FILE backend/tests/test_effective_batch_clock.py =====
"""Maintenance must not consume fallback time; provider queueing must."""
from datetime import datetime, timedelta, timezone
import json

import pytest
from sqlalchemy import create_engine, select, text
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session
from app.database import Base
from app.models import AuditEvent
from test_batch_fallback_execution_integration import fallback_case
from test_native_baseline_incident_mysql import mysql_incident

NOW = datetime(2026, 9, 11, tzinfo=timezone.utc)


@pytest.fixture
def db(tmp_path):
    engine = create_engine(f'sqlite:///{tmp_path / "clock.db"}')
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    with Session(engine) as session:
        yield session
    engine.dispose()


def event(db, action, seconds, *, key='release', subject='production_run', subject_id='1'):
    row = AuditEvent(event_key=f'{key}:{action}', category='runtime_clock', action=action,
        subject_type=subject, subject_id=subject_id, actor='test',
        payload_json=json.dumps({'incident': key}), created_at=NOW+timedelta(seconds=seconds))
    db.add(row)
    db.flush()


def test_overlapping_global_and_child_maintenance_counts_once_and_queue_counts(db):
    from app.effective_batch_clock import effective_queue_time
    event(db, 'maintenance_pause', 100, subject='runtime', subject_id='all')
    event(db, 'maintenance_resume', 400, subject='runtime', subject_id='all')
    event(db, 'maintenance_pause', 200, key='child')
    event(db, 'maintenance_resume', 500, key='child')
    # 1000 seconds wall - union [100,500] = 600 effective seconds.
    assert effective_queue_time(db, run_id=1, queued_at=NOW, now=NOW+timedelta(seconds=1000)) == NOW+timedelta(seconds=400)
    # Mere passage of queue/provider-wait time continues counting.
    assert effective_queue_time(db, run_id=1, queued_at=NOW, now=NOW+timedelta(seconds=1500)) == NOW+timedelta(seconds=400)


def test_open_maintenance_stops_clock_only_for_matching_scope(db):
    from app.effective_batch_clock import effective_queue_time
    event(db, 'maintenance_pause', 100)
    assert effective_queue_time(db, run_id=1, queued_at=NOW, now=NOW+timedelta(seconds=1000)) == NOW+timedelta(seconds=900)
    assert effective_queue_time(db, run_id=2, queued_at=NOW, now=NOW+timedelta(seconds=1000)) == NOW


def test_new_window_discards_past_maintenance_but_keeps_future_pauses(db):
    from app.effective_batch_clock import effective_queue_time
    event(db, 'maintenance_pause', 100)
    event(db, 'maintenance_resume', 400)
    event(db, 'batch_window_restart', 1000, key='new-window')
    event(db, 'maintenance_pause', 1500, key='later')
    event(db, 'maintenance_resume', 1600, key='later')
    assert effective_queue_time(db, run_id=1, queued_at=NOW, now=NOW+timedelta(seconds=2000)) == NOW+timedelta(seconds=1100)


def test_legacy_release_pairs_are_used_without_changing_audit_or_frozen_data(db):
    from app.effective_batch_clock import effective_queue_time
    event(db, 'release_admission_pause', 100)
    event(db, 'release_admission_resume', 300)
    # Actual release keys use a common prefix plus :pause/:resume.
    for row in db.scalars(select(AuditEvent)):
        row.event_key = 'original-release:'+('pause' if row.action.endswith('pause') else 'resume')
    db.flush()
    before = [(r.event_key, r.payload_json) for r in db.scalars(select(AuditEvent))]
    assert effective_queue_time(db, run_id=1, queued_at=NOW, now=NOW+timedelta(seconds=1000)) == NOW+timedelta(seconds=200)
    assert [(r.event_key, r.payload_json) for r in db.scalars(select(AuditEvent))] == before


def test_clock_restart_never_returns_an_online_node_to_batch(db):
    from app.effective_batch_clock import effective_queue_time
    from app.batch_endpoint_recovery import decide
    event(db, 'batch_window_restart', 1000, key='new-window')
    effective = effective_queue_time(db, run_id=1, queued_at=NOW, now=NOW+timedelta(seconds=1200))
    decision = decide(queued_at=effective, now=NOW+timedelta(seconds=1200),
        batch_count=0, online_count=0, online_started=True, online_limit=3)
    assert decision.mode == 'online'
    assert decision.remaining_seconds == 86200


def test_restart_is_idempotent_and_real_executor_uses_new_window(fallback_case):
    from app.models import ContentIngressEvent, ProductionRun, ProductionStepAttempt
    from app.effective_batch_clock import restart_batch_window
    from app.workflow_fixture_executor import _transport_context
    sessions, kwargs = fallback_case
    now = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
    with sessions() as db:
        db.execute(text("CREATE TRIGGER clock_audit_immutable BEFORE UPDATE ON audit_events BEGIN SELECT RAISE(ABORT, 'audit_events is immutable'); END"))
        run = db.get(ProductionRun, db.get(ProductionStepAttempt, kwargs['attempt_id']).run_id)
        run.status = 'paused'
        db.get(ProductionStepAttempt, kwargs['attempt_id']).status = 'retryable'
        original = (run.created_at, run.snapshot_json, run.snapshot_hash)
        db.get(ContentIngressEvent, 7001).received_at = now-timedelta(hours=30)
        event = restart_batch_window(db, run_id=run.id, incident='approved-release', actor='test', now=now)
        assert restart_batch_window(db, run_id=run.id, incident='approved-release', actor='test', now=now+timedelta(hours=1)).id == event.id
        assert (run.created_at, run.snapshot_json, run.snapshot_hash) == original
        db.commit()
    decision, deadline = _transport_context(**kwargs, invocation_key='root')
    assert decision.mode == 'batch'
    assert 86390 < decision.remaining_seconds <= 86400
    assert deadline == now+timedelta(hours=24)
    from app.models import WorkflowModelInvocation
    from app.batch_endpoint_recovery import invocation_prefix
    from app.workflow_runtime import _batch_transport_restart
    with sessions() as db:
        step = db.get(ProductionStepAttempt, kwargs['attempt_id'])
        run = db.get(ProductionRun, step.run_id)
        db.add(WorkflowModelInvocation(production_run_id=run.id, step_attempt_id=step.id,
            invocation_key=invocation_prefix('root')+'batch:1', node_instance_id=step.step_key,
            status='reserved', provider='doubao', model_id='test', created_at=now,
            reserved_input_tokens=100, reserved_output_tokens=50, estimated_cost_micros=1))
        db.flush()
        assert _batch_transport_restart(db, run, step, now+timedelta(seconds=60))['wait_until'] == deadline


def test_maintenance_timestamps_are_inserted_with_immutable_audit(db):
    from app.effective_batch_clock import record_maintenance, effective_queue_time
    db.execute(text("CREATE TRIGGER clock_audit_immutable BEFORE UPDATE ON audit_events BEGIN SELECT RAISE(ABORT, 'audit_events is immutable'); END"))
    first = record_maintenance(db, incident='release', paused=True, actor='test', now=NOW)
    second = record_maintenance(db, incident='release', paused=False, actor='test', now=NOW+timedelta(seconds=60))
    assert first.created_at == NOW and second.created_at == NOW+timedelta(seconds=60)
    assert record_maintenance(db, incident='release', paused=True, actor='test', now=NOW+timedelta(seconds=120)).id == first.id
    assert effective_queue_time(db, run_id=1, queued_at=NOW, now=NOW+timedelta(seconds=120)) == NOW+timedelta(seconds=60)
    db.commit()


def test_mysql_effective_clock_preserves_immutable_audit(mysql_incident):
    from app.models import ProductionRun
    from app.native_baseline_incident import resume_baseline_incident
    from app.effective_batch_clock import record_maintenance, restart_batch_window, effective_queue_time
    sessions, contract, _ = mysql_incident
    now = datetime.now(timezone.utc).replace(microsecond=0)
    with sessions() as db:
        restored = resume_baseline_incident(db, **contract, limit=1)
        run = db.get(ProductionRun, restored['resumed_run_ids'][0])
        run.status = 'paused'
        db.flush()
        original = (run.created_at, run.snapshot_hash, run.snapshot_json)
        pause = record_maintenance(db, incident='mysql-release', paused=True, actor='test', now=now)
        restarted = restart_batch_window(db, run_id=run.id, incident='mysql-release', actor='test', now=now)
        record_maintenance(db, incident='mysql-release', paused=False, actor='test', now=now+timedelta(seconds=60))
        assert restart_batch_window(db, run_id=run.id, incident='mysql-release', actor='test', now=now+timedelta(seconds=120)).id == restarted.id
        assert (run.created_at, run.snapshot_hash, run.snapshot_json) == original
        assert effective_queue_time(db, run_id=run.id, queued_at=now, now=now+timedelta(seconds=120)) == now+timedelta(seconds=60)
        db.commit()
        # The production rule remains enforced; the fix must not disable it.
        from sqlalchemy.exc import DBAPIError
        with pytest.raises(DBAPIError, match='immutable'):
            db.execute(text('UPDATE audit_events SET created_at=:t WHERE id=:id'), {'t':now+timedelta(seconds=1),'id':pause.id})
        db.rollback()


def test_operator_retry_restarts_window_and_only_counts_rows_after_it(db):
    """2026-09-18：人工重试 = 新一代传输预算 + 重置 24 小时时钟；旧账本行只占保留号。"""
    from types import SimpleNamespace
    from app.effective_batch_clock import (effective_queue_time, latest_batch_window_restart,
                                           record_operator_retry, rows_after_restart)
    assert latest_batch_window_restart(db, run_id=7) is None
    first = record_operator_retry(db, run_id=7, actor='ops', step_attempt_ids=[3, 1], now=NOW+timedelta(hours=30))
    assert first.action == 'batch_window_restart' and first.subject_id == '7'
    assert json.loads(first.payload_json) == {'incident': 'operator-retry:1', 'step_attempt_ids': [1, 3]}
    second = record_operator_retry(db, run_id=7, actor='ops', step_attempt_ids=[5], now=NOW+timedelta(hours=31))
    assert second.event_key != first.event_key and json.loads(second.payload_json)['incident'] == 'operator-retry:2'
    assert latest_batch_window_restart(db, run_id=7) == NOW+timedelta(hours=31)
    # 时钟：入队 30 小时前早已过 24 小时截止，重开后从重开时刻起算
    assert effective_queue_time(db, run_id=7, queued_at=NOW, now=NOW+timedelta(hours=32)) == NOW+timedelta(hours=31)
    rows = [SimpleNamespace(created_at=NOW+timedelta(hours=h)) for h in (1, 2, 31, 31.5)]
    kept = rows_after_restart(rows, latest_batch_window_restart(db, run_id=7))
    assert [r.created_at for r in kept] == [NOW+timedelta(hours=31.5)]
    assert rows_after_restart(rows, None) == rows

from datetime import datetime, timedelta, timezone
import json

import pytest
from fastapi.testclient import TestClient
from sqlalchemy import create_engine, func, select
from sqlalchemy.orm import sessionmaker

from app.database import Base, get_db
from app.main import app, COOKIE_NAME
from app.models import User, SessionToken, EvaluationResult, RuntimeOutboxEvent, EmployeeIdentity, UserPreference
from app.security import create_session_token
from tests.test_incremental_correction_queue import _material, _seed_reference_data


@pytest.fixture
def context(tmp_path, monkeypatch):
    monkeypatch.setenv("DINGTALK_ENABLED", "0")
    monkeypatch.setenv("DINGTALK_REQUIRE_EMPLOYEE", "0")
    monkeypatch.setenv("DINGTALK_CORP_ID", "corp-test")
    engine = create_engine("sqlite:///" + str(tmp_path / "employee.sqlite"), connect_args={"check_same_thread": False})
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    sessions = sessionmaker(bind=engine, expire_on_commit=False)
    with sessions() as db:
        _seed_reference_data(db)
        first, _ = _material(db, 1)
        second, _ = _material(db, 2, category="space_image", published=False)
        ids = first.id, second.id
    def database():
        with sessions() as db:
            yield db
    previous = dict(app.dependency_overrides)
    app.dependency_overrides.clear()
    app.dependency_overrides[get_db] = database
    def login(role="category_manager", categories=("model_3d_su",)):
        token, digest = create_session_token()
        with sessions() as db:
            user = User(username="test-" + digest[:12], password_hash="!uninitialized", display_name="测试成员",
                is_admin=role == "admin", role=role, is_active=True, category_keys_json=json.dumps(categories))
            db.add(user); db.flush()
            user_id = user.id
            db.add(SessionToken(token_hash=digest, user_id=user_id, expires_at=datetime.now(timezone.utc) + timedelta(hours=1)))
            db.commit()
        client = TestClient(app, base_url="https://testserver")
        client.cookies.set(COOKIE_NAME, token)
        return client, user_id
    yield sessions, login, ids
    app.dependency_overrides.clear(); app.dependency_overrides.update(previous)
    engine.dispose()


def correction_payload():
    return {"level": "L1", "reason": "人工核验", "idempotency_key": "permission-test", "expected_level": "L3"}


@pytest.mark.parametrize("role,categories,status", [("admin", [], 200), ("product_rd", [], 200), ("category_manager", ["model_3d_su"], 200), ("category_manager", [], 403), ("category_manager", ["space_image"], 403), ("viewer", [], 403)])
def test_real_cookie_role_and_object_authorization(context, role, categories, status):
    sessions, login, ids = context
    client, user_id = login(role, categories)
    response = client.put(f"/api/incremental-corrections/{ids[0]}/level", json=correction_payload())
    assert response.status_code == status, response.text
    with sessions() as db:
        assert db.get(EvaluationResult, ids[0]).level == ("L1" if status == 200 else "L3")
    me = client.get("/api/auth/me")
    assert me.status_code == 200 and me.json()["id"] == user_id
    assert me.json()["role"] == role


def test_mixed_batch_is_rejected_before_any_correction_or_outbox(context):
    sessions, login, ids = context
    client, _ = login()
    with sessions() as db:
        before = db.scalar(select(func.count()).select_from(RuntimeOutboxEvent))
    body = correction_payload(); body.pop("expected_level")
    body["items"] = [{"evaluation_id": i, "expected_level": "L3"} for i in ids]
    assert client.put("/api/incremental-corrections/batch/level", json=body).status_code == 403
    with sessions() as db:
        assert [db.get(EvaluationResult, i).level for i in ids] == ["L3", "L3"]
        assert db.scalar(select(func.count()).select_from(RuntimeOutboxEvent)) == before

    _, login, ids = context

    body = correction_payload() | {"category_key": "space_image"}
    assert client.put(f"/api/incremental-corrections/{ids[0]}/level", json=body).status_code == 403


def test_readonly_preferences_are_personal_and_page_scoped(context):
    sessions, login, _ = context
    one, one_id = login("viewer", [])
    two, _ = login("viewer", [])
    body = {"scope": "global", "expected_revision": 0, "values": {"favorites": ["/workflow/operations"]}}
    assert one.put("/api/me/preferences", json=body).status_code == 200
    assert two.get("/api/me/preferences").json() == {"items": []}
    assert one.put("/api/me/preferences", json=body).status_code == 409
    page = {"scope": "incremental-corrections", "expected_revision": 0, "values": {"filters": {"category_key": "model_3d_su"}, "table": {"density": "compact", "page_size": 50}}}
    assert one.put("/api/me/preferences", json=page).status_code == 200
    assert len(one.get("/api/me/preferences").json()["items"]) == 2
    assert one.put("/api/me/preferences", json=page | {"values": {"password": "never-store"}}).status_code == 422
    with sessions() as db:
        assert "never-store" not in str(list(db.scalars(select(UserPreference.values_json))))

def test_departure_revokes_all_sessions_and_late_join_does_not_reopen(context, monkeypatch):
    from app.employee_lifecycle import process_identity_event
    sessions, login, _ = context
    client, user_id = login("admin", [])
    now = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
    with sessions() as db:
        db.add(EmployeeIdentity(user_id=user_id, corp_id="corp-test", employee_id="employee-test", union_id="union-test", display_name="测试", verified_at=now))
        db.commit()
        # A newer join can arrive first; a late leave still requires manual reopen.
        process_identity_event(db, event_id="join", corp_id="corp-test", event_type="user_add_org", occurred_at=now, employee_ids=["employee-test"])
        db.commit()
        process_identity_event(db, event_id="leave", corp_id="corp-test", event_type="user_leave_org", occurred_at=now-timedelta(seconds=1), employee_ids=["employee-test"])
        db.commit()
        assert db.get(User, user_id).is_active is False
        assert db.scalar(select(func.count()).select_from(SessionToken).where(SessionToken.user_id == user_id)) == 0
        assert process_identity_event(db, event_id="leave", corp_id="corp-test", event_type="user_leave_org", occurred_at=now, employee_ids=["employee-test"]) is False
        db.commit()
    assert client.get("/api/auth/me").status_code == 401


def test_stale_org_error_is_temporary_and_does_not_mark_resigned(context, monkeypatch):
    sessions, login, _ = context
    client, user_id = login("viewer", [])
    with sessions() as db:
        db.add(EmployeeIdentity(user_id=user_id, corp_id="corp-test", employee_id="employee-test", union_id="union-test", display_name="测试", verified_at=datetime.now(timezone.utc)-timedelta(hours=1)))
        db.commit()
    assert client.get("/api/auth/me").status_code == 503
    with sessions() as db:
        assert db.get(User, user_id).is_active is True
        assert db.scalar(select(EmployeeIdentity)).status == "active"

from datetime import datetime, timedelta, timezone
from dataclasses import replace
import json
from urllib.parse import parse_qs, urlsplit

from fastapi import HTTPException
from starlette.requests import Request
from starlette.responses import Response
import httpx
import pytest
from sqlalchemy import create_engine, inspect, select
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from app.database import Base
from app.dingtalk_identity import DingTalkClient, DingTalkSettings, NotCompanyEmployee, IdentityUnavailable
from app.employee_auth_api import create_challenge, consume_challenge, safe_return_path
from app.models import User, SessionToken, EmployeeIdentity

def test_browser_authorization_requests_the_approved_profile_read_scope():
    settings = DingTalkSettings(True, False, "corp", "app", "https://label.test/api/auth/dingtalk/callback", "reference")
    params = parse_qs(urlsplit(DingTalkClient(settings).authorize_url("browser-state")).query)
    assert params["scope"] == ["openid corpid Contact.User.Read"]
    assert params["prompt"] == ["consent"]
    assert params["state"] == ["browser-state"]
    assert params["redirect_uri"] == [settings.redirect_uri]


@pytest.mark.parametrize("status,payload,expected", [
    (403, {"code": "Forbidden.AccessDenied", "message": "private-provider-body"}, "Forbidden.AccessDenied"),
    (200, {"errcode": 60011, "errmsg": "private-provider-body"}, "60011"),
])
def test_identity_failures_log_only_safe_diagnostic_fields(caplog, status, payload, expected):
    settings = DingTalkSettings(True, False, "corp", "app", "https://label.test/api/auth/dingtalk/callback", "reference")
    client = DingTalkClient(settings, httpx.MockTransport(lambda _: httpx.Response(status, json=payload)))
    with pytest.raises(IdentityUnavailable):
        client._request("POST", "https://oapi.dingtalk.com/topapi/user/getbyunionid?access_token=private-access-token",
                        json={"unionid": "private-union-id"})
    assert expected in caplog.text and "/topapi/user/getbyunionid" in caplog.text
    assert all(secret not in caplog.text for secret in ("private-provider-body", "private-access-token", "private-union-id"))


def test_http_identity_requires_exact_private_origin_and_explicit_cookie_opt_in():
    base = DingTalkSettings(True, False, "corp", "app", "http://192.168.1.137:8082/api/auth/dingtalk/callback", "ref", secure_sessions=False)
    assert not base.ready
    allowed = replace(base, lan_http_origin="http://192.168.1.137:8082", allow_insecure_sessions=True)
    assert allowed.ready and not allowed.cookie_secure
    assert not replace(allowed, allow_insecure_sessions=False).ready
    assert not replace(allowed, secure_sessions=True).ready
    assert not replace(allowed, lan_http_origin="http://192.168.1.137:8083").ready
    for host in ("8.8.8.8", "127.0.0.1", "0.0.0.0", "169.254.169.254", "100.64.0.1", "192.168.1.137.attacker.test", "label.test", "[::1]"):
        origin = f"http://{host}:8082"
        assert not replace(allowed, redirect_uri=origin + "/api/auth/dingtalk/callback", lan_http_origin=origin).ready
    for suffix in ("?a=b", "#fragment", "/other"):
        assert not replace(allowed, redirect_uri=allowed.redirect_uri + suffix).ready
    assert not replace(allowed, redirect_uri="http://user@192.168.1.137:8082/api/auth/dingtalk/callback", lan_http_origin="http://user@192.168.1.137:8082").ready
    https = replace(base, redirect_uri="https://label.test/api/auth/dingtalk/callback", secure_sessions=True)
    assert https.ready and https.cookie_secure


def test_lan_identity_rejects_other_origin_public_peer_and_forwarded_header_spoof(monkeypatch):
    import app.employee_auth_api as auth
    config = DingTalkSettings(True, False, "corp", "app", "http://192.168.1.137:8082/api/auth/dingtalk/callback", "ref",
        secure_sessions=False, lan_http_origin="http://192.168.1.137:8082", allow_insecure_sessions=True)
    monkeypatch.setattr(auth, "identity_settings", lambda: config)
    def incoming(host="192.168.1.137:8082", peer="192.168.1.22", scheme="http"):
        return Request({"type":"http", "method":"GET", "path":"/api/auth/dingtalk/start", "scheme":scheme,
            "server":("internal",8080), "client":(peer,1234),
            "headers":[(b"host",host.encode()), (b"x-forwarded-host",b"192.168.1.137:8082"), (b"x-forwarded-for",b"192.168.1.22")]})
    auth.require_identity_origin(incoming())
    for req in (incoming(host="192.168.1.137:8083"), incoming(host="attacker.test"), incoming(peer="8.8.8.8"), incoming(scheme="https")):
        with pytest.raises(HTTPException) as failure:
            auth.require_identity_origin(req)
        assert failure.value.status_code == 403


def request(cookie=""):
    return Request({"type": "http", "method": "GET", "path": "/", "headers": [(b"cookie", cookie.encode())], "client": ("127.0.0.1", 1234), "scheme": "https", "server": ("test", 443)})


def test_state_is_browser_bound_one_use_and_separates_code_flows(tmp_path):
    engine = create_engine("sqlite:///" + str(tmp_path / "state.sqlite"))
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    with Session(engine) as db:
        response = Response()
        state = create_challenge(db, response, request(), "browser", "/workflow/operations")
        cookie = response.headers["set-cookie"].split(";", 1)[0]
        with pytest.raises(HTTPException): consume_challenge(db, request(), state, "browser")
        with pytest.raises(HTTPException): consume_challenge(db, request(cookie), state, "workbench")
        assert consume_challenge(db, request(cookie), state, "browser") == "/workflow/operations"
        with pytest.raises(HTTPException): consume_challenge(db, request(cookie), state, "browser")
    engine.dispose()


@pytest.mark.parametrize("value", ["//evil.example", "https://evil.example", "/\\evil.example", "/login", "/workflow/ok\nLocation:bad", "/workflow/x#fragment"])
def test_redirects_cannot_escape_the_application(value):
    assert safe_return_path(value) == "/"


def test_cookie_write_origin_preserves_test_port_and_pinned_https_proxy(monkeypatch):
    from app.employee_access import check_origin
    import app.dingtalk_identity as identity
    def write(host, source, forwarded=""):
        return Request({"type":"http", "method":"POST", "path":"/", "scheme":"http", "server":("internal",8080),
            "headers":[(b"host",host.encode()),(b"origin",source.encode()),(b"x-forwarded-host",forwarded.encode())]})
    monkeypatch.setattr(identity,"identity_settings",lambda: DingTalkSettings(False,False,"","","",""))
    check_origin(write("192.168.1.137:8082","http://192.168.1.137:8082"))
    with pytest.raises(HTTPException): check_origin(write("192.168.1.137:8082","http://192.168.1.137:8083"))
    monkeypatch.setattr(identity,"identity_settings",lambda: DingTalkSettings(True,True,"corp","app","https://label.test/api/auth/dingtalk/callback","ref"))
    check_origin(write("label.test","https://label.test:443"))
    with pytest.raises(HTTPException): check_origin(write("label.test","https://evil.test","evil.test"))


def test_distinct_code_exchanges_and_corporate_membership(monkeypatch):
    settings = DingTalkSettings(True, True, "corp-test", "client-test", "https://label.test/api/auth/dingtalk/callback", "reference")
    monkeypatch.setattr(DingTalkSettings, "secret", lambda _: "synthetic-test-secret")
    calls = []
    def respond(req):
        calls.append(req.url.path)
        data = json.loads(req.content) if req.content else {}
        if req.url.path == "/v1.0/oauth2/accessToken": return httpx.Response(200, json={"accessToken": "synthetic-app-token", "expireIn": 7200})
        if req.url.path == "/v1.0/oauth2/userAccessToken":
            assert data["grantType"] == "authorization_code" and data["code"] == "browser-code"
            return httpx.Response(200, json={"accessToken": "synthetic-user-token"})
        if req.url.path == "/v1.0/contact/users/me": return httpx.Response(200, json={"unionId": "union-test"})
        if req.url.path == "/topapi/user/getbyunionid": return httpx.Response(200, json={"errcode": 0, "result": {"userid": "employee-test", "contact_type": 0}})
        if req.url.path == "/topapi/v2/user/getuserinfo":
            assert data == {"code": "workbench-code"}
            return httpx.Response(200, json={"errcode": 0, "result": {"userid": "employee-test"}})
        if req.url.path == "/topapi/v2/user/get": return httpx.Response(200, json={"errcode": 0, "result": {"userid": "employee-test", "unionid": "union-test", "name": "测试员工"}})
        raise AssertionError(req.url.path)
    client = DingTalkClient(settings, httpx.MockTransport(respond))
    browser = client.browser_code("browser-code")
    calls.clear()
    workbench = client.workbench_code("workbench-code")
    assert browser == workbench
    assert "/v1.0/oauth2/userAccessToken" not in calls


def test_external_contacts_are_refused_and_api_errors_do_not_leak_tokens(monkeypatch):
    settings = DingTalkSettings(True, True, "corp-test", "client-test", "https://label.test/api/auth/dingtalk/callback", "reference")
    client = DingTalkClient(settings)
    monkeypatch.setattr(client, "_corporate", lambda path, body: {"userid": "employee-test", "unionid": "union-test", "contact_type": 1})
    with pytest.raises(NotCompanyEmployee): client.employee("employee-test")
    bad = DingTalkClient(settings, httpx.MockTransport(lambda _: httpx.Response(403, json={"secret": "should-not-appear"})))
    with pytest.raises(IdentityUnavailable) as caught:
        bad._request("GET", "https://oapi.dingtalk.com/test?access_token=synthetic-secret")
    assert "synthetic-secret" not in str(caught.value) and "should-not-appear" not in str(caught.value)


def test_legacy_sqlite_migration_preserves_accounts_foreign_keys_and_changes_defaults(tmp_path):
    engine = create_engine("sqlite:///" + str(tmp_path / "migration.sqlite"))
    with engine.begin() as connection:
        connection.exec_driver_sql("""CREATE TABLE users (
            id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,
            username VARCHAR(80) NOT NULL UNIQUE,
            password_hash TEXT NOT NULL,
            display_name VARCHAR(80),
            is_active BOOLEAN DEFAULT 1,
            is_admin BOOLEAN DEFAULT 1 NOT NULL,
            role VARCHAR(30) DEFAULT 'admin' NOT NULL,
            permissions_json TEXT DEFAULT '[]',
            created_at TIMESTAMP, last_login_at TIMESTAMP
        )""")
        connection.exec_driver_sql("CREATE TABLE session_tokens (id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY, token_hash VARCHAR(64), user_id INTEGER REFERENCES users(id), expires_at TIMESTAMP, created_at TIMESTAMP)")
        connection.exec_driver_sql("CREATE TABLE historical_actor (id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY, user_id INTEGER REFERENCES users(id))")
        connection.exec_driver_sql("INSERT INTO users(id,username,password_hash,role,is_admin) VALUES (1,'legacy-admin','scrypt$synthetic','admin',1),(2,'legacy-reviewer','scrypt$synthetic','reviewer',0)")
        connection.exec_driver_sql("INSERT INTO historical_actor VALUES(1,1),(2,2)")
        connection.exec_driver_sql("INSERT INTO session_tokens(id,token_hash,user_id) VALUES (1,'synthetic-1',1),(2,'synthetic-2',2)")
        migrate(connection)
        migrate(connection)
        assert connection.exec_driver_sql("SELECT id,password_hash FROM users ORDER BY id").all() == [(1,"scrypt$synthetic"),(2,"scrypt$synthetic")]
        assert connection.exec_driver_sql("SELECT user_id FROM session_tokens").all() == [(1,)]
        assert connection.exec_driver_sql("PRAGMA foreign_key_check").all() == []
        assert inspect(connection).get_foreign_keys("historical_actor")[0]["referred_table"] == "users"
        connection.exec_driver_sql("INSERT INTO users(id,username,password_hash) VALUES(3,'new-user','!uninitialized')")
        assert connection.exec_driver_sql("SELECT is_admin,role,password_initialized FROM users WHERE id=3").one() == (0,"viewer",0)
    engine.dispose()

import asyncio
from datetime import datetime, timedelta, timezone
import json
import logging
import re
import threading
from urllib.parse import parse_qs, urlsplit

from fastapi import HTTPException
from fastapi.testclient import TestClient
import pytest
from sqlalchemy import func, select

from app.main import app, COOKIE_NAME
from app.models import AuditEvent, EmployeeIdentity, IdentityEvent, SessionToken, User, UserPreference
from tests.test_employee_access import context


def test_cookie_auth_database_wait_does_not_block_asgi_event_loop(monkeypatch):
    import app.main as main
    from starlette.requests import Request
    entered, release = threading.Event(), threading.Event()
    sentinel = object()
    def blocking_auth(request, cookie, db, body):
        entered.set()
        assert release.wait(2), "ASGI loop could not release a waiting DB transaction"
        return sentinel
    monkeypatch.setattr(main, "_current_user_database", blocking_auth)
    async def run():
        task = asyncio.create_task(main.current_user(Request({"type":"http", "method":"GET", "headers":[]}), "synthetic", None))
        try:
            while not entered.is_set():
                await asyncio.sleep(0.001)
            release.set()
            assert await task is sentinel
        finally:
            release.set()
    asyncio.run(run())


def fake_provider(monkeypatch, origin="https://testserver"):
    from app.dingtalk_identity import DingTalkSettings, VerifiedEmployee
    import app.employee_auth_api as auth
    import app.employee_lifecycle as lifecycle
    lan = origin.startswith("http:")
    settings = DingTalkSettings(True, False, "corp-test", "client-test", origin + "/api/auth/dingtalk/callback", "synthetic-reference",
        secure_sessions=not lan, lan_http_origin=origin if lan else "", allow_insecure_sessions=lan)
    employee = VerifiedEmployee("corp-test", "employee-test", "union-test", "测试员工")
    class Provider:
        config = settings
        def employee(self, _): return employee
        def browser_code(self, _): return employee
        def workbench_code(self, _): return employee
        def authorize_url(self, state): return "https://login.dingtalk.com/oauth2/auth?state=" + state
    monkeypatch.setattr(auth, "identity_client", Provider)
    monkeypatch.setattr(auth, "identity_settings", lambda: settings)
    monkeypatch.setattr(lifecycle, "identity_client", Provider)
    return employee


def browser_login(client):
    start = client.get("/api/auth/dingtalk/start", follow_redirects=False)
    assert start.status_code == 302, start.text
    state = parse_qs(urlsplit(start.headers["location"]).query)["state"][0]
    return client.get("/api/auth/dingtalk/callback", params={"state": state, "authCode": "synthetic-code"}, follow_redirects=False)


@pytest.mark.parametrize("origin", ["https://testserver", "http://192.168.1.137:8082"])
def test_first_login_registers_reader_then_both_dingtalk_flows_and_password_share_one_user(context, monkeypatch, origin):
    sessions, login, _ = context
    fake_provider(monkeypatch, origin)
    admin, _ = login("admin", [])
    employee = TestClient(app, base_url=origin, client=("192.168.1.22", 1234))
    first = browser_login(employee)
    assert first.headers["location"] == "/account"
    me = employee.get("/api/auth/me").json()
    user_id, username = me["id"], me["username"]
    assert me["role"] == "viewer" and me["is_admin"] is False
    assert me["password_initialized"] is False
    assert employee.get("/api/users").status_code == 403
    assert employee.put("/api/incremental-corrections/1/level", json={}).status_code == 403
    identities = admin.get("/api/employees").json()["items"]
    assert identities[0]["user_id"] == user_id
    assert employee.post("/api/auth/login", json={"username": username, "password": "no-initial-password"}).status_code == 401
    assert browser_login(employee).headers["location"] == "/account"
    session_cookie = next(cookie for cookie in employee.cookies.jar if cookie.name == COOKIE_NAME)
    assert session_cookie.secure == origin.startswith("https:")
    assert employee.get("/api/auth/me").json()["id"] == user_id
    old_cookie = employee.cookies.get(COOKIE_NAME)
    own = employee.post("/api/auth/password", json={"password": "synthetic-own-password"})
    assert own.status_code == 200, own.text
    assert own.json()["password_initialized"] is True
    stale = TestClient(app, base_url=origin); stale.cookies.set(COOKIE_NAME, old_cookie)
    assert stale.get("/api/auth/me").status_code == 401
    employee.post("/api/auth/logout")
    password = employee.post("/api/auth/login", json={"username": username, "password": "synthetic-own-password"})
    assert password.status_code == 200 and password.json()["id"] == user_id
    assert employee.post("/api/auth/password", json={"password": "another-synthetic-password"}).status_code == 403
    workbench = TestClient(app, base_url=origin, client=("192.168.1.22", 1234))
    state = workbench.post("/api/auth/dingtalk/workbench/start").json()["state"]
    work = workbench.post("/api/auth/dingtalk/workbench", json={"state": state, "code": "synthetic-h5-code"})
    assert work.status_code == 200 and work.json()["id"] == user_id
    assert workbench.post("/api/auth/dingtalk/workbench", json={"state": state, "code": "synthetic-h5-code"}).status_code == 400
    from app.employee_lifecycle import process_identity_event
    with sessions() as db:
        process_identity_event(db, event_id="leave-api", corp_id="corp-test", event_type="user_leave_org",
            occurred_at=datetime.now(timezone.utc), employee_ids=["employee-test"])
        db.commit()
        assert db.scalar(select(func.count()).select_from(User).where(User.id == user_id)) == 1
        assert db.scalar(select(func.count()).select_from(AuditEvent).where(AuditEvent.action == "employee_self_registered")) == 1
    assert employee.get("/api/auth/me").status_code == 401
    assert workbench.get("/api/auth/me").status_code == 401
    assert employee.post("/api/auth/login", json={"username": username, "password": "synthetic-own-password"}).status_code == 401
    assert browser_login(employee).headers["location"] == "/login?identity_error=disabled"

@pytest.mark.parametrize("active", [True, False])
def test_existing_admin_binding_and_password_are_never_replaced_or_reactivated(context, monkeypatch, active):

def test_registration_does_not_match_names_and_conflicts_roll_back(context, monkeypatch):
    import hashlib
    from app.dingtalk_identity import VerifiedEmployee
    from app.employee_lifecycle import employee_login_user
    sessions, login, _ = context
    verified = fake_provider(monkeypatch)
    _, admin_id = login("admin", [])
    with sessions() as db:
        db.get(User, admin_id).display_name = verified.display_name
        db.commit()
        reader = employee_login_user(db, verified)
        assert reader.id != admin_id and reader.role == "viewer" and not reader.is_admin
        db.commit()
        before_users = db.scalar(select(func.count()).select_from(User))
        before_identities = db.scalar(select(func.count()).select_from(EmployeeIdentity))
        # Same union ID under a different employee ID must not relink or add users.
        with pytest.raises(HTTPException) as conflict:
            employee_login_user(db, VerifiedEmployee("corp-test", "other-employee", "union-test", "同名员工"))
        assert conflict.value.status_code == 409
        db.rollback()
        assert db.scalar(select(func.count()).select_from(User)) == before_users
        assert db.scalar(select(func.count()).select_from(EmployeeIdentity)) == before_identities
        with pytest.raises(HTTPException) as outsider:
            employee_login_user(db, VerifiedEmployee("other-corp", "external", "external", "外部人员"))
        assert outsider.value.status_code == 403
        db.rollback()
        # Do not attach a new identity to an existing account even if its reserved
        # generated username is already present.
        key = hashlib.sha256(b"corp-test\0collision").hexdigest()[:32]
        db.add(User(username="dd_" + key, display_name="旧管理员", is_admin=True, role="admin"))
        db.commit()
        with pytest.raises(HTTPException) as collision:
            employee_login_user(db, VerifiedEmployee("corp-test", "collision", "new-union", "测试员工"))
        assert collision.value.status_code == 409
        db.rollback()
        assert db.scalar(select(func.count()).select_from(EmployeeIdentity)) == before_identities

def test_all_registered_employee_write_entrances_fail_closed_for_reader(context):
    from app.employee_access import WRITE_POLICIES
    _, login, _ = context
    client, _ = login("viewer", [])
    def contexts():
        for route in app.routes:
            if hasattr(route, "effective_route_contexts"):
                for entry in route.effective_route_contexts():
                    yield entry.path, entry.original_route
            elif hasattr(route, "dependant"):
                yield route.path, route
    def has_user(dependant):
        return any(getattr(child.call, "__name__", "") == "current_user" or has_user(child) for child in dependant.dependencies)
    checked = 0
    for path, route in contexts():
        if not has_user(route.dependant): continue
        for method in route.methods - {"GET", "HEAD", "OPTIONS"}:
            policy = WRITE_POLICIES.get((method, path.rstrip("/")))
            if policy and policy[1] in {"self", "read"}: continue
            actual = re.sub(r"\{[^}]+\}", "1", path)
            response = client.request(method, actual, json={})
            assert response.status_code == 403, (method, path, response.status_code, response.text)
            checked += 1
    assert checked >= 200


def test_guard_rechecks_after_service_commit_and_rejects_revoked_actor(context):
    from app.authz import protect_write_transaction
    sessions, login, _ = context
    _, actor_id = login("product_rd", [])
    with sessions() as original:
        actor = original.get(User, actor_id)
        protect_write_transaction(original, actor)
        original.commit()
        with sessions() as revocation:
            actor = revocation.get(User, actor_id)
            actor.permission_revision += 1
            actor.role = "viewer"
            revocation.commit()
        original.add(UserPreference(user_id=actor_id, scope="global", revision=1, values_json="{}"))
        with pytest.raises(HTTPException, match="权限已变更"):
            original.commit()
        original.rollback()
    with sessions() as db:
        assert db.scalar(select(func.count()).select_from(UserPreference)) == 0


def test_stream_ack_follows_commit_duplicate_is_safe_and_bad_org_is_retried(context):
    from app.employee_identity_worker import handle_envelope
    sessions, login, _ = context
    client, user_id = login("admin", [])
    now = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
    with sessions() as db:
        db.add(EmployeeIdentity(user_id=user_id, corp_id="corp-test", employee_id="employee-test", union_id="union-test", display_name="测试", verified_at=now))
        db.commit()
    event = {"type": "EVENT", "headers": {"messageId": "message-1", "eventId": "stream-1", "eventCorpId": "corp-test", "eventType": "user_leave_org", "eventBornTime": int(now.timestamp() * 1000)}, "data": json.dumps({"userId": ["employee-test"]})}
    for _ in range(2):
        ack, disconnect = asyncio.run(handle_envelope(event, sessions))
        assert json.loads(ack["data"])["status"] == "SUCCESS" and not disconnect
    with sessions() as db:
        assert db.get(User, user_id).is_active is False
        assert db.scalar(select(func.count()).select_from(IdentityEvent)) == 1
    assert client.get("/api/auth/me").status_code == 401
    event["headers"]["eventCorpId"] = "another-company"
    ack, _ = asyncio.run(handle_envelope(event, sessions))
    assert json.loads(ack["data"])["status"] == "LATER"

def test_employee_cutover_requires_two_bound_admins_and_live_matching_stream(context, monkeypatch, tmp_path):
    from types import SimpleNamespace
    from app.dingtalk_identity import DingTalkSettings
    import app.employee_access_readiness as gate
    import app.employee_identity_worker as worker
    import app.config as config
    sessions, login, _ = context
    settings = DingTalkSettings(True, False, "corp-test", "client-test", "https://testserver/api/auth/dingtalk/callback", "synthetic-reference")
    monkeypatch.setattr(gate, "identity_settings", lambda: settings)
    monkeypatch.setattr(worker, "identity_settings", lambda: settings)
    monkeypatch.setattr(worker, "get_settings", lambda: SimpleNamespace(data_dir=tmp_path))
    monkeypatch.setattr(config, "get_settings", lambda: SimpleNamespace(data_dir=tmp_path))
    monkeypatch.setenv("DINGTALK_STREAM_ENABLED", "1")
    _, first = login("admin", [])
    _, second = login("admin", [])
    with sessions() as db:
        for user_id in (first, second):
            db.add(EmployeeIdentity(user_id=user_id, corp_id="corp-test", employee_id=f"employee-{user_id}", union_id=f"union-{user_id}", display_name="测试", verified_at=datetime.now(timezone.utc)))
        db.commit()
        assert gate.readiness(db)["cutover_ready"] is False
        worker._stream_status(True)
        assert gate.readiness(db)["cutover_ready"] is True
        db.get(User, second).is_active = False; db.commit()
        assert gate.readiness(db)["cutover_ready"] is False
        db.get(User, second).is_active = True; db.commit()
        path = tmp_path / "runtime/employee-stream-status.json"
        saved = json.loads(path.read_text())
        path.write_text(json.dumps({**saved, "configuration_key": "another-application"}))
        assert gate.readiness(db)["stream_connected"] is False
        path.write_text(json.dumps({**saved, "checked_at": (datetime.now(timezone.utc)-timedelta(minutes=2)).isoformat()}))
        assert gate.readiness(db)["stream_connected"] is False
        worker._stream_status(False)
        assert gate.readiness(db)["cutover_ready"] is False
        path.write_text("[]")
        assert gate.readiness(db)["stream_connected"] is False

===== FILE backend/tests/test_employee_mysql.py =====
"""Opt-in tests for an isolated, empty MySQL fixture; never platform databases."""
from concurrent.futures import ThreadPoolExecutor
from datetime import datetime, timedelta, timezone
import os
import threading

from fastapi import HTTPException
import pytest
from sqlalchemy import create_engine, event, func, inspect, make_url, select
from sqlalchemy.orm import sessionmaker

from app.authz import lock_access_users, protect_write_transaction
from app.database import configure_mysql_dbapi_connection
from app.migrations.employee_access_132 import migrate
from app.migrations.mysql_baseline import mysql_metadata
from app.models import AuditEvent, EmployeeIdentity, LoginChallenge, User, UserPreference


@pytest.fixture(scope="module")
def isolated_mysql():
    url = os.getenv("EMPLOYEE_MYSQL_TEST_URL")
    if not url:
        pytest.skip("EMPLOYEE_MYSQL_TEST_URL not configured")
    parsed = make_url(url)
    assert parsed.host in {"127.0.0.1", "localhost"} and parsed.database == "labelsystem_test_auth_132"
    engine = create_engine(parsed, pool_pre_ping=True)
    event.listen(engine, "connect", lambda connection, _: configure_mysql_dbapi_connection(connection))
    with engine.connect() as connection:
        assert inspect(connection).get_table_names() == [], "fixture must be a fresh empty database"
    yield engine
    engine.dispose()  # Lifecycle owner removes only this disposable container.


def test_upgrade_mysql_131_users_preserves_hashes_ids_references_and_is_idempotent(isolated_mysql):
    engine = isolated_mysql
    metadata = mysql_metadata()
    with engine.begin() as connection:
        metadata.tables["users"].create(connection)
        metadata.tables["session_tokens"].create(connection)
        for name in ("category_keys_json", "permission_revision", "password_initialized"):
            connection.exec_driver_sql(f"ALTER TABLE users DROP COLUMN {name}")
        for name in ("auth_method", "identity_verified_at"):
            connection.exec_driver_sql(f"ALTER TABLE session_tokens DROP COLUMN {name}")
        connection.exec_driver_sql("ALTER TABLE users ALTER COLUMN is_admin SET DEFAULT 1")
        connection.exec_driver_sql("ALTER TABLE users ALTER COLUMN role SET DEFAULT 'admin'")
        connection.exec_driver_sql("INSERT INTO users(id,username,password_hash,display_name,is_active,is_admin,role,created_at) VALUES(1,'synthetic-admin','scrypt$synthetic','Admin',1,1,'admin',UTC_TIMESTAMP()),(2,'synthetic-legacy','scrypt$legacy','Legacy',1,0,'manager',UTC_TIMESTAMP())")
        connection.exec_driver_sql("INSERT INTO session_tokens(token_hash,user_id,expires_at,created_at) VALUES('synthetic-1',1,DATE_ADD(UTC_TIMESTAMP(),INTERVAL 1 DAY),UTC_TIMESTAMP()),('synthetic-2',2,DATE_ADD(UTC_TIMESTAMP(),INTERVAL 1 DAY),UTC_TIMESTAMP())")
        connection.exec_driver_sql("CREATE TABLE historical_actor(id INT PRIMARY KEY,user_id INT NOT NULL,FOREIGN KEY(user_id) REFERENCES users(id))")
        connection.exec_driver_sql("INSERT INTO historical_actor VALUES(1,1),(2,2)")
        migrate(connection)
        # Simulate interrupted MySQL DDL before its password backfill.
        connection.exec_driver_sql("UPDATE users SET password_initialized=0")
        migrate(connection)
        assert connection.exec_driver_sql("SELECT password_initialized FROM users ORDER BY id").all() == [(1,), (1,)]
        assert connection.exec_driver_sql("SELECT id,password_hash FROM users ORDER BY id").all() == [(1,"scrypt$synthetic"),(2,"scrypt$legacy")]
        assert connection.exec_driver_sql("SELECT user_id FROM session_tokens").all() == [(1,)]
        assert connection.exec_driver_sql("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM historical_actor JOIN users ON users.id=historical_actor.user_id").scalar_one() == 2
        connection.exec_driver_sql("INSERT INTO users(id,username,password_hash,display_name,is_active,created_at) VALUES(3,'new-viewer','!uninitialized','Viewer',1,UTC_TIMESTAMP())")
        assert connection.exec_driver_sql("SELECT is_admin,role,password_initialized FROM users WHERE id=3").one() == (0,"viewer",0)
        assert inspect(connection).get_foreign_keys("employee_identities")[0]["referred_table"] == "users"


def test_mysql_permission_revocation_waits_for_authorized_transaction_then_rejects_next(isolated_mysql):
    sessions = sessionmaker(bind=isolated_mysql, expire_on_commit=False, autoflush=False)
    writer_locked, release_writer, revoker_started = threading.Event(), threading.Event(), threading.Event()
    def write():
        with sessions() as db:
            protect_write_transaction(db, db.get(User, 1))
            writer_locked.set()
            assert release_writer.wait(10)
            db.add(UserPreference(user_id=1, scope="global", revision=1, values_json="{}"))
            db.commit()
    def revoke():
        assert writer_locked.wait(10)
        with sessions() as db:
            revoker_started.set()
            lock_access_users(db, [1])
            row = db.get(User, 1)
            row.is_active = False
            row.permission_revision += 1
            db.commit()
    with ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=2) as pool:
        first, second = pool.submit(write), pool.submit(revoke)
        assert revoker_started.wait(10)
        release_writer.set()
        first.result(timeout=15); second.result(timeout=15)
    with sessions() as db:
        assert db.get(UserPreference, (1, "global")) is not None
        with pytest.raises(HTTPException): protect_write_transaction(db, db.get(User, 1))
        db.rollback()


def test_mysql_one_use_challenge_has_one_winner_across_sessions(isolated_mysql):
    from app.employee_auth_api import _challenge_cookie, consume_challenge
    from app.security import hash_session_token
    from starlette.requests import Request
    sessions = sessionmaker(bind=isolated_mysql)
    state, nonce = "synthetic-state", "synthetic-browser"
    with sessions() as db:
        db.add(LoginChallenge(state_hash=hash_session_token(state), browser_hash=hash_session_token(nonce), method="browser",
            return_to="/", expires_at=datetime.now(timezone.utc)+timedelta(minutes=1)))
        db.commit()
    barrier = threading.Barrier(2)
    def consume():
        with sessions() as db:
            request = Request({"type":"http", "headers":[(b"cookie", f"{_challenge_cookie()}={nonce}".encode())]})
            barrier.wait(timeout=10)
            try: return consume_challenge(db, request, state, "browser")
            except HTTPException: return "rejected"
    with ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=2) as pool:
        futures = [pool.submit(consume) for _ in range(2)]
        assert sorted(f.result(timeout=15) for f in futures) == ["/", "rejected"]


@pytest.mark.parametrize("preverified", [False, True])
def test_mysql_concurrent_first_login_creates_exactly_one_readonly_account(isolated_mysql, monkeypatch, preverified):
    from app.dingtalk_identity import VerifiedEmployee
    from app.employee_lifecycle import employee_login_user, verified_identity
    monkeypatch.setenv("DINGTALK_CORP_ID", "corp-test")
    sessions = sessionmaker(bind=isolated_mysql, expire_on_commit=False, autoflush=False)
    with isolated_mysql.begin() as connection:
        mysql_metadata().tables["audit_events"].create(connection, checkfirst=True)
    employee = VerifiedEmployee("corp-test", f"parallel-{preverified}", f"union-{preverified}", "并发测试员工")
    if preverified:
        with sessions() as db:
            verified_identity(db, employee)
            db.commit()
    barrier = threading.Barrier(2)
    seen = threading.local()
    def synchronize_initial_read(_connection, _cursor, statement, parameters, _context, _many):
        if (not getattr(seen, "done", False) and statement.startswith("SELECT")
                and "WHERE employee_identities.corp_id" in statement and "FOR UPDATE" not in statement
                and employee.employee_id in parameters.values()):
            seen.done = True
            barrier.wait(timeout=10)
    event.listen(isolated_mysql, "after_cursor_execute", synchronize_initial_read)
    def login():
        with sessions() as db:
            user = employee_login_user(db, employee)
            assert (user.role, user.is_admin, user.permissions_json, user.category_keys_json) == ("viewer", False, "[]", "[]")
            db.commit()
            return user.id
    try:
        with ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=2) as pool:
            futures = [pool.submit(login) for _ in range(2)]
            ids = [future.result(timeout=20) for future in futures]
        assert ids[0] == ids[1]
    finally:
        event.remove(isolated_mysql, "after_cursor_execute", synchronize_initial_read)
    with sessions() as db:
        identity = db.scalar(select(EmployeeIdentity).where(EmployeeIdentity.employee_id == employee.employee_id))
        assert identity.user_id == ids[0]
        assert db.scalar(select(func.count()).select_from(AuditEvent).where(
            AuditEvent.action == "employee_self_registered", AuditEvent.subject_id == str(ids[0]))) == 1

===== FILE backend/tests/test_endpoint_fallback_display.py =====
import pytest
from sqlalchemy import select
from app.models import WorkflowDefinition
from app.doubao_endpoints import create_endpoint
from test_content_ingress_incremental_routing import _fixture, _close
from test_doubao_endpoints import _operator


@pytest.mark.parametrize('problem', ['none', 'missing', 'mismatch', 'disabled_policy'])
def test_endpoint_list_exposes_authoritative_fallback_readiness(monkeypatch, problem):
    monkeypatch.setenv('LABEL_SYSTEM_BATCH_ONLINE_FALLBACK', 'false' if problem == 'disabled_policy' else 'true')
    engine, db, _, _ = _fixture()
    try:
        definition = db.scalar(select(WorkflowDefinition))
        batch = create_endpoint(db, endpoint_id='ep-bi-20260909104719-bkhmk', display_name='batch', category_key='space_image', foundation_model='doubao-seed-2-0-lite-260428', actor='test')
        online = create_endpoint(db, endpoint_id='ep-20260901120000-abcde', display_name='online', category_key='space_image', foundation_model='doubao-seed-2-0-lite-260428', actor='test')
        batch.verification_status = online.verification_status = 'verified'
        definition.doubao_batch_endpoint_row_id = batch.id
        definition.doubao_endpoint_row_id = None if problem == 'missing' else online.id
        if problem == 'mismatch': online.foundation_model = 'doubao-seed-2-0-lite-260215'
        db.commit()
        response = _operator(db).get('/api/doubao-endpoints?category_key=space_image')
        assert response.status_code == 200
        rows = response.json()['workflow_routes']
        row = next(item for item in rows if item['workflow_definition_id'] == definition.id)
        assert row['fallback_ready'] is (problem == 'none')
        assert row['batch_endpoint_row_id'] == batch.id
        assert row['fallback_policy_enabled'] is (problem != 'disabled_policy')
        if problem == 'none':
            assert row['batch_max_attempts'] == 3
            assert row['deadline_seconds'] == 86400
        else:
            assert row['blocker']
    finally:
        _close(engine, db)


@pytest.mark.parametrize('escalation', ['1', '0'])
def test_endpoint_list_says_whether_the_24h_escalation_is_switched_on(monkeypatch, escalation):
    """接入点就绪与「会不会转在线」是两件事，页面必须分别看得到。"""
    monkeypatch.setenv('LABEL_SYSTEM_BATCH_ONLINE_FALLBACK', 'true')
    monkeypatch.setenv('LABEL_SYSTEM_ONLINE_FALLBACK_ESCALATION', escalation)
    engine, db, _, _ = _fixture()
    try:
        definition = db.scalar(select(WorkflowDefinition))
        batch = create_endpoint(db, endpoint_id='ep-bi-20260909104719-bkhmk', display_name='batch', category_key='space_image', foundation_model='doubao-seed-2-0-lite-260428', actor='test')
        online = create_endpoint(db, endpoint_id='ep-20260901120000-abcde', display_name='online', category_key='space_image', foundation_model='doubao-seed-2-0-lite-260428', actor='test')
        batch.verification_status = online.verification_status = 'verified'
        definition.doubao_batch_endpoint_row_id = batch.id
        definition.doubao_endpoint_row_id = online.id
        db.commit()
        row = next(item for item in _operator(db).get('/api/doubao-endpoints?category_key=space_image').json()['workflow_routes']
                   if item['workflow_definition_id'] == definition.id)
        # 关掉升级不会让冻结策略消失：两个接入点仍然就绪，截止仍是 24 小时。
        assert row['fallback_ready'] is True and row['blocker'] is None
        assert row['deadline_seconds'] == 86400
        assert row['online_escalation_enabled'] is (escalation == '1')
    finally:
        _close(engine, db)

    assert (

            {
                "format_version": "common-modifiers-v2",
                "hard_defect_penalty": {
