def test_component_hash_is_stable_across_key_order_and_changes_with_content() -> None:
    catalog = _catalog()

    first = catalog.canonical_component_hash(
        {"enabled": True, "penalties": {"render_3d": -5, "real_photo": 0}}
    )
    reordered = catalog.canonical_component_hash(
        {"penalties": {"real_photo": 0, "render_3d": -5}, "enabled": True}
    )
    changed = catalog.canonical_component_hash(
        {"enabled": True, "penalties": {"render_3d": -6, "real_photo": 0}}
    )

    assert first == reordered
    assert first != changed
    assert len(first) == 64

===== FILE backend/tests/test_evaluation_components.py =====
from __future__ import annotations

import json
import hashlib

import pytest
from sqlalchemy import create_engine, select
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from app.database import Base
from app.models import (
    CategoryIncrementalMechanismBinding,
    EvaluationCategoryProfile,
    PromptVersion,
    RuleModuleVersion,
    WorkflowNodeAssetBinding,
    WorkflowDefinition,
    WorkflowVersion,
)


def _engine(tmp_path):
    engine = create_engine(f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / 'evaluation-components.db'}")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    return engine


def _profile(db: Session, category_key: str) -> None:
    db.add(
        EvaluationCategoryProfile(
            category_key=category_key,
            display_name=f"{category_key} 类目",
            status="active",
            allowed_mime_types_json='["image/jpeg"]',
            preprocess_config_json="{}",
            pipeline_config_json="{}",
            created_by="ops",
        )
    )
    db.flush()


def _prompt_content(stage: str = "C") -> dict[str, str]:
    return {
        "stage": stage,
        "system_prompt": "你是结构化评测助手。",
        "user_prompt": "请只输出约定字段。",
        "rubric_version": "rubric-v1",
    }


def _redline_content() -> dict:
    return {
        "enabled": True,
        "hit_level": "L5",
        "hit_score_cap": 49,
        "rules": [],
    }


def test_prompt_c_exists_before_first_version_and_generates_immutable_version(
    tmp_path,
) -> None:
    from app.evaluation_components import (
        create_component_asset,
        generate_component_version,
        get_component_working_copy,
        save_component_working_copy,
    )

    engine = _engine(tmp_path)
    try:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            _profile(db, "space_image")
            asset = create_component_asset(
                db,
                category_key="space_image",
                asset_key="prompt_c",
                kind="prompt",
                display_name="提示词 C",
                prompt_stage="C",
                created_by="ops",
            )
            working = get_component_working_copy(db, asset.id)
            assert json.loads(working.content_json) == {"stage": "C"}

            saved = save_component_working_copy(
                db,
                asset_id=asset.id,
                expected_lock_version=1,
                content=_prompt_content(),
                updated_by="ops",
            )
            assert saved.lock_version == 2

            generated = generate_component_version(
                db, asset_id=asset.id, created_by="ops"
            )
            prompt = db.get(PromptVersion, generated.id)

            assert generated.kind == "prompt"
            assert generated.version == "component-v1"
            assert generated.reference_facts == {
                "current_incremental": False,
                "historical": False,
                "unreferenced": True,
            }
            assert prompt is not None
            assert prompt.status == "published"
            assert prompt.source == "workflow_component"
            assert db.scalar(
                select(WorkflowNodeAssetBinding).where(
                    WorkflowNodeAssetBinding.asset_id == asset.id,
                    WorkflowNodeAssetBinding.prompt_version_id == prompt.id,
                )
            )
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def test_prompt_versions_deduplicate_unchanged_working_copy(tmp_path) -> None:
    from app.evaluation_components import (
        create_component_asset,
        generate_component_version,
        save_component_working_copy,
    )

    engine = _engine(tmp_path)
    try:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            _profile(db, "space_image")
            asset = create_component_asset(
                db,
                category_key="space_image",
                asset_key="prompt_c",
                kind="prompt",
                display_name="提示词 C",
                prompt_stage="C",
                created_by="ops",
            )
            save_component_working_copy(
                db,
                asset_id=asset.id,
                expected_lock_version=1,
                content=_prompt_content(),
                updated_by="ops",
            )

            first = generate_component_version(
                db, asset_id=asset.id, created_by="ops"
            )
            repeated = generate_component_version(
                db, asset_id=asset.id, created_by="ops"
            )

            assert first == repeated
            assert db.scalars(
                select(PromptVersion).where(
                    PromptVersion.category_key == "space_image",
                    PromptVersion.source == "workflow_component",
                )
            ).all() == [db.get(PromptVersion, first.id)]

            archived = db.get(PromptVersion, first.id)
            assert archived is not None
            archived.status = "archived"
            db.flush()
            replacement = generate_component_version(
                db, asset_id=asset.id, created_by="ops"
            )

            assert replacement.id != first.id
            assert db.get(PromptVersion, replacement.id).status == "published"
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def test_rule_module_versions_deduplicate_and_stale_working_copy_is_rejected(
    tmp_path,
) -> None:
    from app.evaluation_components import (
        EvaluationComponentError,
        create_component_asset,
        generate_component_version,
        save_component_working_copy,
    )

    engine = _engine(tmp_path)
    try:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            _profile(db, "space_image")
            asset = create_component_asset(
                db,
                category_key="space_image",
                asset_key="redline",
                kind="rule_module",
                display_name="红线过滤",
                module_type_key="eligibility_redline",
                created_by="ops",
            )
            save_component_working_copy(
                db,
                asset_id=asset.id,
                expected_lock_version=1,
                content=_redline_content(),
                updated_by="ops",
            )
            first = generate_component_version(
                db, asset_id=asset.id, created_by="ops"
            )
            repeated = generate_component_version(
                db, asset_id=asset.id, created_by="ops"
            )

            assert first == repeated
            assert db.scalars(
                select(RuleModuleVersion).where(
                    RuleModuleVersion.asset_id == asset.id
                )
            ).all() == [db.get(RuleModuleVersion, first.id)]

            with pytest.raises(EvaluationComponentError) as excinfo:
                save_component_working_copy(
                    db,
                    asset_id=asset.id,
                    expected_lock_version=1,
                    content=_redline_content(),
                    updated_by="stale-operator",
                )
            assert excinfo.value.code == "component_working_copy_conflict"
            assert excinfo.value.status_code == 409
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def test_cross_category_copy_has_independent_working_copy(tmp_path) -> None:
    from app.evaluation_components import (
        copy_component_asset,
        create_component_asset,
        get_component_working_copy,
        save_component_working_copy,
    )

    engine = _engine(tmp_path)
    try:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            _profile(db, "space_image")
            _profile(db, "material_image")
            source = create_component_asset(
                db,
                category_key="space_image",
                asset_key="redline",
                kind="rule_module",
                display_name="红线过滤",
                module_type_key="eligibility_redline",
                created_by="ops",
            )
            source_copy = save_component_working_copy(
                db,
                asset_id=source.id,
                expected_lock_version=1,
                content=_redline_content(),
                updated_by="ops",
            )

            copied = copy_component_asset(
                db,
                source_asset_id=source.id,
                target_category_key="material_image",
                target_asset_key="redline_copy",
                display_name="红线过滤副本",
                created_by="ops",
            )
            copied_copy = get_component_working_copy(db, copied.id)

            assert copied.id != source.id
            assert copied.copied_from_asset_id == source.id
            assert copied_copy.content_json == source_copy.content_json
            assert copied_copy.id != source_copy.id

            changed = {**_redline_content(), "hit_score_cap": 39}
            save_component_working_copy(
                db,
                asset_id=copied.id,
                expected_lock_version=copied_copy.lock_version,
                content=changed,
                updated_by="material-ops",
            )
            assert json.loads(get_component_working_copy(db, source.id).content_json)[
                "hit_score_cap"
            ] == 49
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def test_component_creation_rejects_invalid_kind_and_prompt_stage(tmp_path) -> None:
    from app.evaluation_components import (
        EvaluationComponentError,
        create_component_asset,
    )

    engine = _engine(tmp_path)
    try:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            _profile(db, "space_image")
            with pytest.raises(EvaluationComponentError) as excinfo:
                create_component_asset(
                    db,
                    category_key="space_image",
                    asset_key="prompt_without_stage",
                    kind="prompt",
                    display_name="没有节点标识",
                    created_by="ops",
                )
            assert excinfo.value.code == "prompt_stage_invalid"

            with pytest.raises(EvaluationComponentError) as excinfo:
                create_component_asset(
                    db,
                    category_key="space_image",
                    asset_key="custom_code",
                    kind="custom_python",
                    display_name="自定义代码",
                    created_by="ops",
                )
            assert excinfo.value.code == "component_kind_invalid"
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def test_hiding_component_changes_discovery_not_version_references(tmp_path) -> None:
    from app.evaluation_components import (
        component_reference_facts,
        create_component_asset,
        generate_component_version,
        save_component_working_copy,
        set_component_discoverability,
    )

    engine = _engine(tmp_path)
    try:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            _profile(db, "space_image")
            asset = create_component_asset(
                db,
                category_key="space_image",
                asset_key="redline",
                kind="rule_module",
                display_name="红线过滤",
                module_type_key="eligibility_redline",
                created_by="ops",
            )
            save_component_working_copy(
                db,
                asset_id=asset.id,
                expected_lock_version=1,
                content=_redline_content(),
                updated_by="ops",
            )
            generated = generate_component_version(
                db, asset_id=asset.id, created_by="ops"
            )
            definition = WorkflowDefinition(
                workflow_key="space_image.component_reference",
                name="组件引用测试",
                description="",
                owner="platform",
                allowed_categories_json='["space_image"]',
                created_by="ops",
            )
            db.add(definition)
            db.flush()
            workflow = WorkflowVersion(
                workflow_definition_id=definition.id,
                version="1",
                workflow_schema_version="workflow-v3",
                step_manifest_json=json.dumps(
                    [
                        {
                            "node_instance_id": "redline-1",
                            "kind": "rule_module",
                            "config": {
                                "rule_module_version_id": generated.id
                            },
                        }
                    ]
                ),
                edge_manifest_json="[]",
                input_schema_json='{"type":"object"}',
                output_schema_json='{"type":"object"}',
                resource_policy_json='{"category_key":"space_image"}',
                canonical_hash=hashlib.sha256(
                    b"component-reference"
                ).hexdigest(),
                created_by="ops",
            )
            db.add(workflow)
            db.flush()

            set_component_discoverability(
                db, asset_id=asset.id, is_discoverable=False
            )
            historical = component_reference_facts(
                db, rule_module_version_id=generated.id
            )
            assert asset.is_discoverable is False
            assert historical == {
                "current_incremental": False,
                "historical": True,
                "unreferenced": False,
            }

            db.add(
                CategoryIncrementalMechanismBinding(
                    category_key="space_image",
                    workflow_version_id=workflow.id,
                    lock_version=1,
                    updated_by="ops",
                )
            )
            db.flush()
            current = component_reference_facts(
                db, rule_module_version_id=generated.id
            )
            assert current == {
                "current_incremental": True,
                "historical": False,
                "unreferenced": False,
            }
    finally:
        engine.dispose()

===== FILE backend/tests/test_evaluation_components_api.py =====
from __future__ import annotations

import importlib

from fastapi import FastAPI
from fastapi.testclient import TestClient
from sqlalchemy import create_engine
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session, sessionmaker
from sqlalchemy.pool import StaticPool

from app.database import Base, get_db
from app.models import EvaluationCategoryProfile, User


def _client(role: str = "product_rd") -> TestClient:
    module = importlib.import_module("app.evaluation_components_api")
    engine = create_engine(
        "sqlite://",
        connect_args={"check_same_thread": False},
        poolclass=StaticPool,
    )
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    SessionForTest = sessionmaker(bind=engine, expire_on_commit=False)
    with SessionForTest() as db:
        for key in ("space_image", "material_image"):
            db.add(
                EvaluationCategoryProfile(
                    category_key=key,
                    display_name=f"{key} 类目",
                    status="active",
                    allowed_mime_types_json='["image/jpeg"]',
                    preprocess_config_json="{}",
                    pipeline_config_json="{}",
                    created_by="admin",
                )
            )
        db.commit()

    def db_override():
        with SessionForTest() as db:
            yield db

    def user_override() -> User:
        return User(
            id=1,
            username=role,
            password_hash="x",
            display_name=role,
            role=role,
            is_admin=role == "admin",
        )

    app = FastAPI()
    app.include_router(module.build_evaluation_components_router(user_override))
    app.dependency_overrides[get_db] = db_override
    return TestClient(app)


def test_component_api_manages_prompt_c_from_discovery_to_version() -> None:
    client = _client()

    module_types = client.get("/api/evaluation-components/module-types")
    assert module_types.status_code == 200
    assert module_types.json()["items"][0]["display_name"] == "准入与红线"

    created = client.post(
        "/api/evaluation-components",
        json={
            "category_key": "space_image",
            "asset_key": "prompt_c",
            "kind": "prompt",
            "display_name": "提示词 C",
            "prompt_stage": "C",
        },
    )
    assert created.status_code == 201, created.text
    asset_id = created.json()["id"]

    listing = client.get(
        "/api/evaluation-components", params={"category_key": "space_image"}
    )
    assert listing.status_code == 200
    assert [item["asset_key"] for item in listing.json()["items"]] == ["prompt_c"]

    detail = client.get(f"/api/evaluation-components/{asset_id}")
    assert detail.status_code == 200
    assert detail.json()["asset_key"] == "prompt_c"

    working = client.get(
        f"/api/evaluation-components/{asset_id}/working-copy"
    )
    assert working.status_code == 200
    assert working.json()["content"] == {"stage": "C"}

    saved = client.put(
        f"/api/evaluation-components/{asset_id}/working-copy",
        json={
            "expected_lock_version": 1,
            "content": {
                "stage": "C",
                "system_prompt": "你是评测助手。",
                "user_prompt": "只输出结构化结果。",
                "rubric_version": "rubric-v1",
            },
        },
    )
    assert saved.status_code == 200, saved.text
    assert saved.json()["lock_version"] == 2

    generated = client.post(
        f"/api/evaluation-components/{asset_id}/versions"
    )
    assert generated.status_code == 201, generated.text
    assert generated.json()["kind"] == "prompt"
    assert "status" not in generated.json()

    versions = client.get(
        f"/api/evaluation-components/{asset_id}/versions"
    )
    assert versions.status_code == 200
    assert versions.json()["items"][0]["version"] == "component-v1"
    assert "status" not in versions.json()["items"][0]


def test_component_api_copies_rule_module_and_reports_optimistic_conflict() -> None:
    client = _client()
    created = client.post(
        "/api/evaluation-components",
        json={
            "category_key": "space_image",
            "asset_key": "redline",
            "kind": "rule_module",
            "display_name": "红线过滤",
            "module_type_key": "eligibility_redline",
        },
    )
    assert created.status_code == 201, created.text
    asset_id = created.json()["id"]
    payload = {
        "expected_lock_version": 1,
        "content": {
            "enabled": True,
            "hit_level": "L5",
            "hit_score_cap": 49,
            "rules": [],
        },
    }
    assert client.put(
        f"/api/evaluation-components/{asset_id}/working-copy", json=payload
    ).status_code == 200

    generated = client.post(f"/api/evaluation-components/{asset_id}/versions")
    assert generated.status_code == 201
    version_items = client.get(
        f"/api/evaluation-components/{asset_id}/versions"
    ).json()["items"]
    assert version_items[0]["content"]["hit_level"] == "L5"

    stale = client.put(
        f"/api/evaluation-components/{asset_id}/working-copy", json=payload
    )
    assert stale.status_code == 409
    assert stale.json()["detail"]["code"] == "component_working_copy_conflict"

    copied = client.post(
        f"/api/evaluation-components/{asset_id}/copy",
        json={
            "target_category_key": "material_image",
            "target_asset_key": "redline_copy",
            "display_name": "红线过滤副本",
        },
    )
    assert copied.status_code == 201, copied.text
    assert copied.json()["copied_from_asset_id"] == asset_id
    assert copied.json()["category_key"] == "material_image"


def test_component_api_permissions_separate_editing_and_version_generation() -> None:
    viewer = _client("viewer")
    assert viewer.get("/api/evaluation-components/module-types").status_code == 200
    forbidden = viewer.post(
        "/api/evaluation-components",
        json={
            "category_key": "space_image",
            "asset_key": "prompt_c",
            "kind": "prompt",
            "display_name": "提示词 C",
            "prompt_stage": "C",
        },
    )
    assert forbidden.status_code == 403
    assert forbidden.json()["detail"]["code"] == "component_write_forbidden"

    manager = _client("product_rd")
    created = manager.post(
        "/api/evaluation-components",
        json={
            "category_key": "space_image",
            "asset_key": "redline",
            "kind": "rule_module",
            "display_name": "红线过滤",
            "module_type_key": "eligibility_redline",
        },
    )
    assert created.status_code == 201
    asset_id = created.json()["id"]
    assert manager.put(
        f"/api/evaluation-components/{asset_id}/working-copy",
        json={
            "expected_lock_version": 1,
            "content": {
                "enabled": True,
                "hit_level": "L5",
                "hit_score_cap": 49,
                "rules": [],
            },
        },
    ).status_code == 200
    assert manager.post(
        f"/api/evaluation-components/{asset_id}/versions"
    ).status_code == 201


def test_main_app_registers_component_management_routes() -> None:
    from app.main import app

    paths = {route.path for route in app.routes if hasattr(route, "path")}
    for included in app.routes:
        router = getattr(included, "original_router", None)
        if router is not None:
            paths.update(route.path for route in router.routes)
    assert "/api/evaluation-components" in paths
    assert "/api/evaluation-components/module-types" in paths
    assert "/api/evaluation-components/{asset_id}/working-copy" in paths

===== FILE backend/tests/test_evaluation_mechanism_migration.py =====
from __future__ import annotations

from copy import deepcopy
import hashlib
import json
import logging

import pytest
from sqlalchemy import create_engine, select
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from app.category_evaluation_v3_revisions import revision_bundle
from app.database import Base
from app.dimension_schema_registry import canonical_json
from app.evaluation_component_catalog import MODULE_TYPE_KEYS
from app.inspiration_category_seed import (
    build_inspiration_classification_map,
    build_inspiration_subcategory_dimensions,
    build_inspiration_v3_contract,
)
from app.models import (
    CategoryEvaluationV3Revision,
    CategoryIncrementalMechanismBinding,
    EvaluationComponentWorkingCopy,
    EvaluationCategoryProfile,
    RuleModuleVersion,
    LegacyEvaluationRevisionMapping,
    WorkflowVersion,
    WorkflowNodeAsset,
)


def _contract(category_key: str = "space_image") -> dict:
    return {
        "schema_version": "evaluation-category-profile-v3",
        "category_key": category_key,
        "profile_type": "image-aesthetic-v3",
        "level_semantics_version": "levels-v1",
        "prompt_bindings": {"call_a_version": "a1", "call_b_version": "b1"},
        "redline_policy": {
            "format_version": "redline-policy-v1",
            "enabled": True,
            "hit_level": "L5",
            "hit_score_cap": 49,
            "rules": [],
        },
        "track_classification": {
            "format_version": "track-classification-v1",
            "tracks": [
                {
                    "key": "class_one",
                    "label": "一类",
                    "base_score": 40,
                    "dimension_max": 60,
                    "track_cap": 100,
                    "dimension_schema_ref": {
                        "schema_key": "space_aesthetic",
                        "version": "1.3.0",
                    },
                }
            ],
            "default_track": "class_one",
        },
        "common_modifiers": {
            "format_version": "common-modifiers-v2",
            "media_type_penalty": {
                "enabled": True,
                "penalties": {"real_photo": 0, "render_3d": -5},
                "baseline": "real_photo",
            },
            "high_score_veto": {"enabled": False},
            "hard_defect_penalty": {
                "enabled": True,
                "per_hit": 10,
                "source": "hard_defects",
            },
        },
        "anchor_mechanism": {
            "enabled": False,
            "max_anchor_images": 5,
            "anchors": [],
        },
        "level_scale": {
            "version": "category-level-scale-v1",
            "levels": [
                {"level": "L1", "enabled": True, "min_score": 80},
                {"level": "L2", "enabled": True, "min_score": 60},
                {"level": "L3", "enabled": True, "min_score": 40},
                {"level": "L4", "enabled": True, "min_score": 20},
                {"level": "L5", "enabled": True, "min_score": 0},
            ],
        },
    }


def _bundle(category_key: str = "space_image") -> dict:
    contract = _contract(category_key)
    return {
        "category_key": category_key,
        "revision": 1,
        "contract_hash": hashlib.sha256(
            canonical_json(contract).encode("utf-8")
        ).hexdigest(),
        "contract": contract,
        "classification_map": {
            "format_version": "classification-map-v1",
            "category_to_subcategory": {},
        },
        "subcategory_dimensions": {},
        "dimension_deduction_rules": {},
        "media_penalty_enabled": True,
    }


def _revision(
    db: Session,
    *,
    revision: int,
    contract: dict,
    status: str,
) -> CategoryEvaluationV3Revision:
    row = CategoryEvaluationV3Revision(
        category_key="space_image",
        display_name=f"空间图评测机制 r{revision}",
        revision=revision,
        status=status,
        contract_json=canonical_json(contract),
        classification_map_json=canonical_json(
            {
                "format_version": "classification-map-v1",
                "category_to_subcategory": {},
            }
        ),
        subcategory_dimensions_json="{}",
        dimension_deduction_rules_json="{}",
        media_penalty_enabled=True,
        contract_hash=hashlib.sha256(
            canonical_json(contract).encode("utf-8")
        ).hexdigest(),
        created_by="legacy",
    )
    db.add(row)
    db.flush()
    return row


def test_split_and_projection_cover_every_registered_module_without_loss() -> None:
    from app.evaluation_mechanism_migration import (
        compare_legacy_projection,
        project_modules_to_legacy_bundle,
        split_legacy_revision,
    )

    legacy = _bundle()
    slices = split_legacy_revision(legacy)
    projected = project_modules_to_legacy_bundle(slices)
    report = compare_legacy_projection(legacy, projected)

    assert set(slices) == set(MODULE_TYPE_KEYS)
    assert slices["eligibility_redline"]["hit_level"] == "L5"
    assert slices["track_routing"]["default_track"] == "class_one"
    assert slices["media_adjustment"]["penalties"]["render_3d"] == -5
    assert slices["score_level_matcher"]["levels"][0]["level"] == "L1"
    assert report.compatible is True
    assert report.unclassified_paths == ()
    assert report.different_paths == ()


def test_unclassified_field_blocks_compatibility_switch() -> None:
    from app.evaluation_mechanism_migration import (
        compare_legacy_projection,
        project_modules_to_legacy_bundle,
        split_legacy_revision,
    )

    legacy = _bundle()
    legacy["contract"]["unknown_rule_block"] = {"x": 1}
    slices = split_legacy_revision(legacy)
    report = compare_legacy_projection(
        legacy, project_modules_to_legacy_bundle(slices)
    )

    assert report.compatible is False
    assert "contract.unknown_rule_block.x" in report.unclassified_paths


def test_current_inspiration_contract_is_fully_classified_and_reversible() -> None:
    from app.evaluation_mechanism_migration import (
        compare_legacy_projection,
        project_modules_to_legacy_bundle,
        split_legacy_revision,
    )

    contract["category_key"] = "inspiration_image"

        "category_key": "inspiration_image",

            canonical_json(contract).encode("utf-8")
        ).hexdigest(),
        "contract": contract,

        "subcategory_dimensions": build_inspiration_subcategory_dimensions(),
        "dimension_deduction_rules": {},
        "media_penalty_enabled": True,

    slices = split_legacy_revision(legacy)
    report = compare_legacy_projection(

    assert report.unclassified_paths == ()

        ".png"
    )


def test_legacy_level_scale_round_trip_does_not_leak_aesthetic_threshold_helper() -> None:
    """Removing helper-field isolation would make historical r10-style contracts diverge."""

    from app.evaluation_mechanism_migration import (
        compare_legacy_projection,
        project_modules_to_legacy_bundle,
        split_legacy_revision,
    )

    legacy = _bundle("inspiration_image")
    original_level_scale = deepcopy(legacy["contract"]["level_scale"])
    legacy["contract"]["aesthetic_foundation"] = {
        "score_thresholds": [
            {"level": "L1", "min_score": 88},
            {"level": "L2", "min_score": 76},
            {"level": "L3", "min_score": 64},
            {"level": "L4", "min_score": 52},
        ]
    }

    slices = split_legacy_revision(legacy)
    projected = project_modules_to_legacy_bundle(slices)
    report = compare_legacy_projection(legacy, projected)

    assert report.compatible is True
    assert report.different_paths == ()
    assert projected["contract"]["level_scale"] == original_level_scale
    assert (
        projected["contract"]["aesthetic_foundation"]["score_thresholds"]
        == legacy["contract"]["aesthetic_foundation"]["score_thresholds"]
    )


def test_historical_inspiration_policy_fields_are_losslessly_classified() -> None:
    """Dropping any test-server r13/r21 compatibility field would block native import."""

    from app.evaluation_mechanism_migration import (
        compare_legacy_projection,
        project_modules_to_legacy_bundle,
        split_legacy_revision,
    )

    legacy = _bundle("inspiration_image")
    foundation = legacy["contract"].setdefault("aesthetic_foundation", {})
    foundation.update(
        {
            "dimension_applicability_policy": {
                "enabled": True,
                "non_space_types": ["专业展板", "视觉设计"],
                "inapplicable_is_not_defect": True,
            },
            "holistic_aesthetic": {
                "enabled": True,
                "key": "overall_aesthetic",
                "aggregation_role": "calibration_modifier",
                "score_adjustment": {
                    "grade_5": {"max_upward_adjustment": 5}
                },
            },
            "anchor_comparison_enabled": False,
            "use_anchors": False,
        }
    )
    legacy["contract"]["evidence_governance"] = {
        "format_version": "human-evidence-governance-v1",
        "human_truth_priority": True,
        "automatic_candidate_may_publish": False,
    }

    slices = split_legacy_revision(legacy)
    projected = project_modules_to_legacy_bundle(slices)
    report = compare_legacy_projection(legacy, projected)

    assert report.compatible is True
    assert report.unclassified_paths == ()
    assert report.different_paths == ()
    assert slices["dimension_scoring"]["dimension_applicability_policy"] == (
        foundation["dimension_applicability_policy"]
    )
    assert slices["dimension_scoring"]["holistic_aesthetic"] == foundation[
        "holistic_aesthetic"
    ]
    assert projected == legacy


def test_component_history_reuses_unchanged_slices_and_is_idempotent(
    tmp_path,
) -> None:
    from app.evaluation_mechanism_migration import (
        migrate_category_component_history,
    )

    engine = create_engine(f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / 'mechanism-migration.db'}")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    try:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            db.add(
                EvaluationCategoryProfile(
                    category_key="space_image",
                    display_name="空间图",
                    status="active",
                    allowed_mime_types_json='["image/jpeg"]',
                    preprocess_config_json="{}",
                    pipeline_config_json="{}",
                    created_by="admin",
                )
            )
            first_contract = _contract()
            second_contract = deepcopy(first_contract)
            second_contract["common_modifiers"]["media_type_penalty"][
                "penalties"
            ]["render_3d"] = -6
            third_contract = deepcopy(second_contract)
            third_contract["redline_policy"]["hit_score_cap"] = 39
            revisions = [
                _revision(
                    db,
                    revision=1,
                    contract=first_contract,
                    status="retired",
                ),
                _revision(
                    db,
                    revision=2,
                    contract=second_contract,
                    status="retired",
                ),
                _revision(
                    db,
                    revision=3,
                    contract=third_contract,
                    status="active",
                ),
            ]

            first = migrate_category_component_history(
                db, "space_image", actor="system:migration"
            )
            second = migrate_category_component_history(
                db, "space_image", actor="system:migration"
            )

            assert first.created_counts == {
                "assets": 7,
                "component_versions": 9,
            }
            assert second.created_counts == {
                "assets": 0,
                "component_versions": 0,
            }
            assert first.revision_slices == second.revision_slices
            assert (
                first.revision_slices[revisions[0].id]["track_routing"]
                == first.revision_slices[revisions[2].id]["track_routing"]
            )
            assert (
                first.revision_slices[revisions[0].id]["media_adjustment"]
                != first.revision_slices[revisions[1].id]["media_adjustment"]
            )
            assert (
                first.revision_slices[revisions[1].id]["eligibility_redline"]
                != first.revision_slices[revisions[2].id][
                    "eligibility_redline"
                ]
            )
            assert len(db.scalars(select(WorkflowNodeAsset)).all()) == 7
            assert len(db.scalars(select(RuleModuleVersion)).all()) == 9
            assert all(
                report.compatible for report in first.compatibility_reports.values()
            )
            assert revision_bundle(revisions[-1])["revision"] == 3
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def test_component_history_bootstraps_rule_working_copy_from_latest_revision(
    tmp_path,
) -> None:
    from app.evaluation_mechanism_migration import (
        migrate_category_component_history,
    )

    engine = create_engine(f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / 'mechanism-working-copy.db'}")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    try:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            db.add(
                EvaluationCategoryProfile(
                    category_key="space_image",
                    display_name="空间图",
                    status="active",
                    allowed_mime_types_json='["image/jpeg"]',
                    preprocess_config_json="{}",
                    pipeline_config_json="{}",
                    created_by="admin",
                )
            )
            first_contract = _contract()
            second_contract = deepcopy(first_contract)
            second_contract["common_modifiers"]["media_type_penalty"][
                "penalties"
            ]["render_3d"] = -6
            _revision(
                db,
                revision=1,
                contract=first_contract,
                status="retired",
            )
            latest_revision = _revision(
                db,
                revision=2,
                contract=second_contract,
                status="active",
            )

            first = migrate_category_component_history(
                db, "space_image", actor="system:migration"
            )
            second = migrate_category_component_history(
                db, "space_image", actor="system:migration"
            )

            asset = db.scalar(
                select(WorkflowNodeAsset).where(
                    WorkflowNodeAsset.category_key == "space_image",
                    WorkflowNodeAsset.asset_key == "media_adjustment",
                )
            )
            assert asset is not None
            working = db.scalar(
                select(EvaluationComponentWorkingCopy).where(
                    EvaluationComponentWorkingCopy.asset_id == asset.id
                )
            )
            assert working is not None
            latest_version_id = first.revision_slices[latest_revision.id][
                "media_adjustment"
            ]
            latest_version = db.get(RuleModuleVersion, latest_version_id)
            assert latest_version is not None
            assert json.loads(working.content_json) == json.loads(
                latest_version.content_json
            )
            assert working.base_rule_module_version_id == latest_version.id
            assert working.lock_version == 1
            assert working.updated_by == "system:migration"
            assert second.revision_slices[latest_revision.id][
                "media_adjustment"
            ] == latest_version.id
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def test_component_history_does_not_overwrite_operator_working_copy(tmp_path) -> None:
    from app.evaluation_components import (
        create_component_asset,
        get_component_working_copy,
        save_component_working_copy,
    )
    from app.evaluation_mechanism_migration import (
        migrate_category_component_history,
        split_legacy_revision,
    )

    engine = create_engine(f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / 'mechanism-operator-copy.db'}")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    try:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            db.add(
                EvaluationCategoryProfile(
                    category_key="space_image",
                    display_name="空间图",
                    status="active",
                    allowed_mime_types_json='["image/jpeg"]',
                    preprocess_config_json="{}",
                    pipeline_config_json="{}",
                    created_by="admin",
                )
            )
            db.flush()
            asset = create_component_asset(
                db,
                category_key="space_image",
                asset_key="media_adjustment",
                kind="rule_module",
                display_name="媒介调整",
                module_type_key="media_adjustment",
                created_by="operator",
            )
            operator_content = deepcopy(
                split_legacy_revision(_bundle())["media_adjustment"]
            )
            operator_content["penalties"]["render_3d"] = -11
            save_component_working_copy(
                db,
                asset_id=asset.id,
                expected_lock_version=1,
                content=operator_content,
                updated_by="operator",
            )
            _revision(
                db,
                revision=1,
                contract=_contract(),
                status="active",
            )

            migrate_category_component_history(
                db, "space_image", actor="system:migration"
            )

            working = get_component_working_copy(db, asset.id)
            assert json.loads(working.content_json) == operator_content
            assert working.base_rule_module_version_id is None
            assert working.lock_version == 2
            assert working.updated_by == "operator"
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def test_migration_rejects_preexisting_asset_with_wrong_module_type(tmp_path) -> None:
    from app.evaluation_mechanism_migration import (
        EvaluationMechanismMigrationError,
        migrate_category_component_history,
    )

    engine = create_engine(f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / 'mechanism-conflict.db'}")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    try:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            db.add(
                EvaluationCategoryProfile(
                    category_key="space_image",
                    display_name="空间图",
                    status="active",
                    allowed_mime_types_json='["image/jpeg"]',
                    preprocess_config_json="{}",
                    pipeline_config_json="{}",
                    created_by="admin",
                )
            )
            _revision(
                db,
                revision=1,
                contract=_contract(),
                status="active",
            )
            db.add(
                WorkflowNodeAsset(
                    category_key="space_image",
                    asset_key="eligibility_redline",
                    kind="rule_module",
                    display_name="错误类型",
                    module_type_key="track_routing",
                    created_by="manual",
                )
            )
            db.flush()

            try:
                migrate_category_component_history(
                    db, "space_image", actor="system:migration"
                )
            except EvaluationMechanismMigrationError as exc:
                assert exc.code == "legacy_module_asset_conflict"
            else:
                raise AssertionError("wrong module type was reused")
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def test_revision_history_generates_one_workflow_mapping_per_legacy_revision(
    tmp_path,
) -> None:
    from app.evaluation_mechanism_migration import migrate_category_revision_history

    engine = create_engine(f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / 'revision-history.db'}")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    try:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            db.add(
                EvaluationCategoryProfile(
                    category_key="space_image",
                    display_name="空间图",
                    status="active",
                    allowed_mime_types_json='["image/jpeg"]',
                    preprocess_config_json="{}",
                    pipeline_config_json="{}",
                    created_by="admin",
                )
            )
            for revision, status in ((1, "retired"), (2, "retired"), (3, "active")):
                contract = _contract()
                contract["redline_policy"]["hit_score_cap"] = 50 - revision
                _revision(
                    db,
                    revision=revision,
                    contract=contract,
                    status=status,
                )

            first = migrate_category_revision_history(
                db, "space_image", actor="system:migration"
            )
            second = migrate_category_revision_history(
                db, "space_image", actor="system:migration"
            )

            assert first.created_counts == {
                "assets": 7,
                "component_versions": 9,
                "workflow_versions": 3,
                "mappings": 3,
            }
            assert second.created_counts == {
                "assets": 0,
                "component_versions": 0,
                "workflow_versions": 0,
                "mappings": 0,
            }
            assert first.mapping_ids == second.mapping_ids
            assert len(first.mapping_ids) == 3
            assert len(db.scalars(select(LegacyEvaluationRevisionMapping)).all()) == 3
            workflows = db.scalars(select(WorkflowVersion)).all()
            assert {row.version for row in workflows} == {
                "legacy-r1",
                "legacy-r2",
                "legacy-r3",
            }
            assert all(row.workflow_schema_version == "workflow-v3" for row in workflows)
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def test_existing_level_categories_get_idempotent_native_workflows(tmp_path) -> None:
    from app.evaluation_component_catalog import MODULE_TYPE_KEYS
    from app.native_workflow_migration import ensure_native_category_workflows
    from app.seed import seed_defaults
    from app.workflow_registry import persisted_workflow_manifest

    engine = create_engine(f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / 'native-category-migration.db'}")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    try:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            db.add(
                EvaluationCategoryProfile(
                    category_key="inspiration_image",
                    display_name="灵感图",
                    status="active",
                    allowed_mime_types_json='["image/jpeg","image/png"]',
                    preprocess_config_json='{"preprocess":"image"}',
                    pipeline_config_json="{}",
                    created_by="system:test",
                )
            )
            seed_defaults(db)
            seeded_native = db.scalars(
                select(WorkflowVersion).where(
                    WorkflowVersion.version.like("native-r%")
                )
            ).all()
            assert {
                row.definition.allowed_categories_json
                for row in seeded_native
            } == {
                '["inspiration_image"]',
                '["model_3d_su"]',
            }
            first = {
                category_key: ensure_native_category_workflows(
                    db,
                    category_key,
                    actor="system:native-workflow-migration",
                )
                for category_key in ("inspiration_image", "model_3d_su")
            }
            db.flush()
            first_ids = {
                category_key: [row.id for row in rows]
                for category_key, rows in first.items()
            }

            second = {
                category_key: ensure_native_category_workflows(
                    db,
                    category_key,
                    actor="system:native-workflow-migration",
                )
                for category_key in ("inspiration_image", "model_3d_su")
            }
            db.flush()

            assert {
                category_key: [row.id for row in rows]
                for category_key, rows in second.items()
            } == first_ids
            assert db.scalar(select(CategoryIncrementalMechanismBinding)) is None
            for category_key, rows in first.items():
                revisions = db.scalars(
                    select(CategoryEvaluationV3Revision).where(
                        CategoryEvaluationV3Revision.category_key == category_key
                    )
                ).all()
                assert len(rows) == len(revisions)
                mappings = db.scalars(
                    select(LegacyEvaluationRevisionMapping).where(
                        LegacyEvaluationRevisionMapping.legacy_revision_id.in_(
                            [revision.id for revision in revisions]
                        )
                    )
                ).all()
                assert {mapping.workflow_version_id for mapping in mappings} == {
                    row.id for row in rows
                }
                for workflow in rows:
                    assert workflow.workflow_schema_version == "workflow-v3"
                    assert workflow.status == "active"
                    manifest = persisted_workflow_manifest(workflow)
                    assert manifest["category_key"] == category_key
                    assert [
                        node["config"]["module_type_key"]
                        for node in manifest["nodes"]
                        if node["kind"] == "rule_module"
                    ] == list(MODULE_TYPE_KEYS)
                    assert all(
                        node["config"].get("mode") in {"parallel", "exclusive"}
                        for node in manifest["nodes"]
                        if node["kind"] == "branch"
                    )
                    prompt_nodes = [
                        node for node in manifest["nodes"]
                        if node["kind"] == "prompt"
                    ]
                    assert [
                        node["config"]["asset_key"] for node in prompt_nodes
                    ] == ["prompt_a", "prompt_b"]
                    assert all(
                        len(node["config"]["component_snapshot"]["content_hash"])
                        == 64
                        and len(node["config"]["model_snapshot"]["snapshot_hash"])
                        == 64
                        for node in prompt_nodes
                    )
                    assert all(
                        len(node["config"]["component_snapshot"]["content_hash"])
                        == 64
                        for node in manifest["nodes"]
                        if node["kind"] == "rule_module"
                    )
                    readiness = json.loads(workflow.validation_report_json)[
                        "native_readiness"
                    ]
                    assert readiness["compatible"] is True
                    assert readiness["legacy_revision_id"] in {
                        revision.id for revision in revisions
                    }
                    assert (
                        manifest["resource_policy"]
                        ["legacy_projection_snapshot"]
                        ["available"]
                        is True
                    )
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def test_frozen_historical_projection_drift_warns_without_startup_error(
    tmp_path,
    caplog: pytest.LogCaptureFixture,
) -> None:
    from app.native_workflow_migration import ensure_native_category_workflows
    from app.seed import seed_defaults

    engine = create_engine(f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / 'historical-projection-drift.db'}")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    try:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            db.add(
                EvaluationCategoryProfile(
                    category_key="inspiration_image",
                    display_name="灵感图",
                    status="active",
                    allowed_mime_types_json='["image/jpeg","image/png"]',
                    preprocess_config_json='{"preprocess":"image"}',
                    pipeline_config_json="{}",
                    created_by="system:test",
                )
            )
            seed_defaults(db)
            workflows = ensure_native_category_workflows(
                db,
                "inspiration_image",
                actor="system:historical-drift-test",
            )
            assert workflows
            workflows[0].step_manifest_json = "[]"
            db.flush()

            caplog.clear()
            with caplog.at_level(
                logging.WARNING,
                logger="app.native_workflow_migration",
            ):
                preserved = ensure_native_category_workflows(
                    db,
                    "inspiration_image",
                    actor="system:historical-drift-test",
                )

            assert preserved == []
            assert db.get(WorkflowVersion, workflows[0].id) is workflows[0]
            drift_records = [
                record
                for record in caplog.records
                if "历史机制投影" in record.getMessage()
            ]
            assert len(drift_records) == 1
            assert drift_records[0].levelno == logging.WARNING
            assert not [record for record in caplog.records if record.levelno >= logging.ERROR]
    finally:
        engine.dispose()
